# Sanming Tonghui · Juan Nine
## A Comprehensive Gathering of the Three Fates

**Attributed to Wan Minying of the Ming dynasty.** From the imperially commissioned *Siku Quanshu*.[^01]

*Reading conventions.* This juan continues the day–hour reference with Ji, Geng, Xin, Ren, and Gui days. Each group contains the twelve double-hours; each hour contains the six occurrences of that day stem in the sixty-pair cycle. In the recorded cases, **year / month** completes the chart with the day and hour immediately above. Dates in a biographical comment are not additional natal pillars. **〈 〉** preserves the source’s small-character commentary; square brackets mark supplied readings or repairs. Capitalized terms retain the technical meanings used in the preceding translations. The predictions and social judgments belong to the historical text: they are not established findings about living people. Competing methods and unresolved readings remain distinct.[^02]

## 1. Ji Days, Jiazi Hour
### 六已日甲子時斷
<!-- source: L14-L59 -->

> A Ji day born at Jiazi hour:  
> Officer Star stands in the open; Wealth lies concealed.  
> With support communicating with the month’s qi,  
> A lifetime’s food and clothing comes from Heaven.

On a Ji day at Jiazi hour, Officer is visible and Wealth hidden. Ji takes Jia as Officer, Gui as Wealth, and Xin as Food; Zi is Tianyi Noble Person. At Zi, Jia is visible, Gui concealed, and Xin at Birth. A supported body communicating with the month’s qi gives wealth and distinction. Even without bodily strength, luck carrying Earth qi is good.

**Jichou day.** Transformation brings distinction. Wu or Yin in year and month shortens life; a month communicating with Earth qi gives distinction. Avoid Jiayin month: a violent death amid distinction; Dingwei month: punishment; Dingchou month: loss of the ancestral establishment, displacement, and violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiyou / Dingsi:** a prefect. **Yisi / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yisi / Dinghai:** Grand Secretary Yu Shenxing. **Jiaxu / Bingzi:** Minister He Kuan; Junior Provincial Administrator Pan Chungu shared the chart. **Jichou / Jiaxu:** military distinction. **Guichou / Yimao:** a legal clerk. **Xinhai / Xinchou:** wandering in search of a livelihood.〉[^03]

**Jimao day.** First the ancestral estate is lost, then prosperity comes; or prosperity is accompanied by injury. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give distinction. Wu strengthens the body, Zi gathers noble influences, and Hai is Officer’s Birth: all give great distinction. Avoid Jisi month: ruin and violent death; Gengshen month: a violent, bloody death; Renzi month: death through punishment and harm. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengyin / Jiashen:** the Ming Hongzhi emperor, who reigned eighteen years; a worthy ruler, said here to have died in Bingyin year. **Gengzi / Wuzi:** a minister. **Jiyou / Gengwu:** a Junior Guardian. **Guihai / Guihai:** an official in the Personnel administration. **Jiachen / Yihai:** a provincial administration commissioner.〉[^04]

**Jisi day, [Jiazi] hour.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give third-rank censorial office, requiring Water-and-Wood luck. Western luck accomplishes nothing; in the south, Wealth and Officer lack qi, giving empty fame and meager profit—distinction without prominence. Zi or Yin in year and month gives manifest distinction in southeast luck. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 transformation into Earth brings blessing, but not prominence. Avoid Renyin month: punishment; Yiwei month: injury through punishment; Guiyou month: early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Jimao:** a chancellor. **Jiazi / Jisi:** a privy councillor. **Guihai / Jiayin:** a chief censor. **Guihai / Yimao:** a prefect. **Bingyin / Xinchou:** minor distinction. **Jiawu / Jiaxu:** immense wealth. **Gengshen / Wuzi:** Shen Kun, palace-examination first, who ended his life in prison; this is the account in the *Star Cases*.〉[^05]

**Jiwei day.** High standing: the body sits upon Officer’s Storehouse. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months are auspicious. Yin and Hai months strengthen Officer: literary brilliance unfolds, and distinction comes readily. Si and Chou give literary distinction attained late; Yin and Mao give poverty and low station. Avoid Jiashen month: death with the body mutilated; Bingzi month: solitary poverty; Dingchou month: punishment and harm.

〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Wuyin:** a provincial graduate. **Dingchou / Jiyou:** a surveillance commissioner.〉

**Jiyou day.** Yin, Shen, Wu, Wei, and Chou in year and month give literary distinction and censorial office. Wu month with northeast luck gives the highest rank. Avoid Gengyin **year**: ruin and violent death; Jisi month: misfortune; Wuxu month: solitude and bloody death. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Renzi:** Shen Kun, palace-examination first, according to the original text. **Renyin / Jiachen:** a capital prefect. **Xinhai / Gengyin:** a vice minister. **Wuwu / Yimao:** Zhu Zhifan, palace-examination first and minister.〉[^06]

**Jihai day.** Spring and summer bring flourishing Wealth and Officer and are auspicious. Autumn, winter, and the season-ending months turn away from Lu and drive off Horse: ominous. Yin month with Metal-and-Fire luck gives a bureau directorship; Wu month with northeast luck, gold insignia and purple robes. Avoid Renyin month: violent death; Renshen month: solitary poverty and punishment; Guiyou month: ruin. 〈Recorded cases—**Renxu / Xinhai:** a ministry bureau director. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a provincial graduate. **Guiwei / Guihai:** Censor Zhang Dao, childless and immensely rich. **Dinghai / Renyin:** a bureau official by inherited privilege.〉

〈Qi Piercing Ox and Dipper:  
Broad learning and abundant literary grace.  
Before his time, a scholar at the study window;  
When the time arrives, his name spreads fragrance.〉

> A Ji day welcomes Jiazi hour:  
> Wealth and Officer in Double Beauty—distinction rare indeed.  
> Once wind and cloud assemble at the appointed time,  
> Alone he strides the moon’s palace, plucking cassia.

> A Ji day at Jiazi hour:  
> Transformation generates rich Earth, nourishing the foundation.  
> Wealth and Officer lend their aid and display a radiant light,  
> Like an Azure Dragon playing in Water.  
> Without Geng Metal, Mao, or Wu,  
> Lu arrives, and blessing is complete.  
> The common person prospers and has scope to act;  
> The gentleman succeeds in the examinations.

## 2. Ji Days, Yichou Hour
### 六已日乙丑時斷
<!-- source: L60-L101 -->
〈For the six Ji days below, the unfavorable months are to be considered together with the hour, as above.〉

> A Ji day born at Yichou hour:  
> Killings Star restrained does not inflict injury.  
> A strong body in the pillars brings much honor and distinction;  
> Without assistance, one toils by day and night.

On a Ji day at Yichou hour, Exclusive Wealth encounters ghost. At Chou, visible Yi is ghost and hidden Gui is Wealth. Support that communicates with bodily strength gives distinction; corresponding luck is also auspicious. If ghost is assisted into strength beyond what the body can sustain, food and clothing remain ordinary.

**Jichou day.** Indirect Officer upon the hour gives military office and a command, but the region must also be examined. Mao month with Metal-and-Water luck gives sixth- or seventh-rank distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Yihai:** Vice Minister Liu Yue. **Renwu / Jisi:** an official in Personnel. **Gengyin / Jimao:** a prefect. **Yisi / Renwu:** Chan Master Foyin. **Wuwu / Renxu:** Gong Nai, Chancellor of the Directorate of Education and vice minister. **Dingsi / Guimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Guihai:** the mother of Yue Yuansheng, Hesheng, and Jinsheng; all three sons attained the metropolitan degree.〉

**Jimao day.** Zi or Shen month with Metal-and-Earth luck gives a marquis or earl. Si, You, Chou, and Xu in year and month are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Yiyou:** a vice minister. **Gengchen / Jimao:** Minister Liu Guangji, childless. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** a chief censor. **Bingzi / Xinchou:** distinction. **Yihai / Renwu:** distinction. **Gengxu / Bingxu:** Sun Jigao, palace-examination first and minister, according to the *Star Cases*. **Jiachen / Jisi:** a supervising secretary for the Ministry of Rites.〉[^07]

**Jisi day.** Yin and Mao months give the Indirect-Officer configuration. Without You or Xu, this is a fate of distinction through literary advancement. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Yimao:** Jiang, palace-examination first. **Bingyin / Xinmao:** the Prince of Chu. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** Governor Qin Yao, banished to frontier service. **Wushen / Gengshen:** first in the military palace examination.〉

**Jiwei day.** Hour and day clash; a wife generating wealth makes it acceptable. Mao and Wu in year and month place Metal Spirit in Fire’s country; southwest luck gives distinction. Chen and Xu complete the four season-ending branches: great distinction. Ding and Xin exposed in the stems, making pure yin, also give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Wushen:** a censor. **Gengwu / Jimao:** a bureau director. **Yimao / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinmao / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingwei / Dingwei:** Right Censor-in-Chief Wan Shide. **Bingxu / Xinmao:** a provincial case marked ominous.〉[^08]

**Jiyou day.** Mao month gives Indirect Officer; Chen month, Wealth and Killings with distinction. Shen and You give Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed; Si and Wu, Seal-Ribbon: all give distinction. Chou and Xu also give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Renchen:** Minister Yin Tai. **Wuxu / Yichou:** Hanlin scholar Cui Xian. **Jiwei / Xinwei:** a renowned general. **Gengxu / Bingxu:** Sun Jigao, palace-examination first, according to the original text.〉

**Jihai day.** Mao month gives great distinction; Shen month with Water-and-Wood luck gives provincial censorial authority. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Gengchen:** a transmission commissioner. **Wuyin / Renxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Xinmao / Guisi:** a bureau official by inherited privilege.〉

〈Indirect Officer upon the Hour:  
A strong body and flourishing Seal are exceptional.  
If there is no rescuing assistance,  
Name and profit alike become empty.〉

> At Yichou hour, the body is weak;  
> Within the Storehouse, Wealth Star lies concealed.  
> Without a key, advancement is difficult;  
> Going abroad, one finds the heart’s desire.

> A Ji day at Yichou hour:  
> Depletion and ghost within the Storehouse stir calamity.  
> The ancestors’ estate waxes and wanes;  
> In hopes and undertakings, wealth goes and comes.  
> Xu and Wei, through punishment and clash, bring blessing;  
> Without a key, one moves from place to place.  
> Parents and the ranks of geese are hard to depend upon;  
> With rescue, middle and later life attain distinction.

## 3. Ji Days, Bingyin Hour
### 六巳日丙寅時斷
<!-- source: L102-L151 -->

> A Ji day born at Bingyin hour:  
> Hidden Officer and visible Seal strengthen the body.  
> The month communicates with Wood, without clash or breaking:  
> Distinction rests upon the Three Terraces and Eight Seats.

On a Ji day at Bingyin hour, Officer and Seal are born and flourishing. Ji takes Jia as Officer and Bing as Seal; at Yin, Jia flourishes and Bing is born. Without damage, and with the month’s qi communicating with a Wood configuration, there is great distinction. Wood-and-Fire travel is welcome, Metal and Water unsuitable. The annual ruler follows the same rule.

**Jichou day.** In spring Officer flourishes; in summer Seal flourishes; autumn gives Completion; winter is ordinary. Chen and Xu in year and month give manifest literary distinction; pure Chou, a long life. Xu month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives fifth or sixth rank. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 a Fire-and-Earth image gives distinction, with blood disorders. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Renshen:** a chancellor. **Jihai / Jiaxu:** Chief Censor Zhang Ji. **Yichou / Renwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengchen / Jichou:** an assistant prefect.〉

**Jimao day.** Wu and Chen in year and month give literary brilliance and great distinction; Yin and Wu give third rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Bingchen:** a minister. **Bingzi / Gengwu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Jiachen / Guiyou:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Jiayin / Bingyin:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** Liu Cha, palace-examination first. **Renchen / Xinhai:** a ministry secretary. **Wuyin / Guihai:** a provincial graduate. **Bingchen / Gengzi:** Xiao Liangyu, metropolitan graduate and senior provincial administrator. **Renyin / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengyin / Xinsi:** a supervising secretary for the Ministry of War. **Jiayin / Gengwu:** a bureau director.〉

**Jisi day.** Injury through punishment first, prosperity afterward. Yin month gives Direct Officer and literary distinction. Hai and Zi give distinction in Water-and-[Wood] luck. Pure Xu gives third-rank military office. Shen and Hai in year and month complete the Four Births: great distinction, though possibly not a good end. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Bingzi:** Chief Censor Wei Qianji. **Renxu / Renxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Jihai / Bingyin:** a supervising secretary. **Dinghai / Guihai:** a provincial graduate.〉[^09]

**Jiwei day.** Birth in a region of substantial Earth gives distinction. Water-and-Wood years and months give distinction through northeast luck. 〈One account says:〉 commonly born to a secondary wife, transferred to another household, or born without the father living to see the birth. 〈Recorded cases—**Renxu / Wushen:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Jiayin / Bingzi:** power and distinction. **Jisi / Gengwu:** a provincial graduate. **Jiazi / Dingchou:** Yuan, palace-examination first.〉

**Jiyou day.** A month communicating with a Wood-and-Fire configuration gives distinction; Wood-and-Fire luck also brings honor and advancement. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Dingchou:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Jiawu / Bingyin:** a surveillance commissioner. **Jiawu / Guiyou:** a deputy commissioner.〉

**Jihai day.** In spring Officer flourishes and gives distinction; summer and autumn are ordinary; winter’s flourishing Wealth is auspicious. Yin, Shen, Chou, Si, Wu, Chen, and Xu in year and month give distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 the Fire-and-Wood image gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Dingchou:** Grand Secretary Jin Gui. **Wuxu / Dingsi:** Minister Xiong Jia. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** Vice Minister Shen Yingshi. **Wuyin / Bingchen:** a chancellor. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** a commander-in-chief. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** an assistant regional commander, killed in battle. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Jimao / Yihai:** a court minister. **Jichou / Yihai:** Ni Jiaqing, metropolitan graduate and bureau director in Revenue, a gentleman from Shangyuan.〉

〈A Name upon the Tiger [List]:  
Wealth and Officer shine within and without.  
If neither clash nor breaking occurs,  
He must become a duke or high minister.〉[^10]

> A Ji day at Bingyin hour is extraordinary:  
> Young and still unrecognized, he is rich in literary gifts.  
> Luck enters Mao, and a bright moon is born;  
> On level steps through azure clouds, he reaches the imperial land.

> A Ji day at Bingyin hour:  
> Officer Star and Seal-Ribbon reach Birth.  
> The Hall of Learning and Triple Combination welcome splendor;  
> Wide reading brings intelligence and refinement.  
> Year and month free of clash and breaking  
> Surely set the name upon the golden list.  
> Luck through flourishing Officer brings success:  
> The fate of a person of the highest caliber.

## 4. Ji Days, Dingmao Hour
### 六巳日丁卯時斷
<!-- source: L152-L197 -->

> A Ji day born at Dingmao hour:  
> In stem and branch, hidden ghost and the insubstantial Owl.  
> Assistance in the pillars alone makes blessing;  
> Without assistance, advancement is hard to attain.

On a Ji day at Dingmao hour, Ji takes Ding as Seal and Yi as ghost; at Mao, Ding is visible and Yi concealed. Without rescuing support in year and month, refinement lacks substance, with success and failure by turns. Xin in the month restraining the adverse influence, together with a strong body, gives distinction; strengthening luck is also auspicious.

**Jichou day.** Shen–Zi–Chen gives military office; Hai–Mao–Wei, honor and distinction. Si–You–Chou gives good fortune in northeast luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiyou / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiyou / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Dingsi / Yisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiyou / Renshen:** a provincial graduate. **Jiashen / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengshen / Wuzi:** a minister in the judicial administration. **Dingmao / Jiachen:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jimao day.** Si and You in year and month give suitable restraint; Geng and Shen combining with Killings give great distinction through literary advancement. Zi month punishes Killings and encounters Noble Person: military command or judicial office. If the branches are pure Seal or form the triple Wood combination, luck entering Yin introduces mixed Officer; a flowing year that also clashes with that luck brings violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Yiyou:** Minister Shi Dao. **Gengshen / Gengchen:** Minister Wu Shan, firm and upright. **Guiwei / Jiazi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renshen / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinsi / Dingyou:** metropolitan graduate Yuan Fuzheng. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** Song Kaichun, bureau director in Revenue, from Pingyuan. The original text of the *Sanming Tonghui* that I obtained was given to me by this gentleman. **Yimao / Wuzi:** great distinction.〉[^11]

**Jisi day.** Without clash or breaking, wealth and distinction. Yin, Wu, and Chen in year and month give adverse punishment and injury. Hai month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives distinction. Spring birth with Jia and Yi exposed makes Officer and Killings too strong: with restraint and transformation there is still distinction; otherwise, misfortune and early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Guimao / Xinyou:** Vice Minister Lu Tong. **Bingzi / Xinmao:** Lü Diaoyang, palace-examination second and Grand Secretary; pure and cautious, with a metropolitan-graduate son. **Wuwu / Bingchen:** a commander. **Bingxu / Dingyou:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiwei day.** If not distinguished, then rich. Mao, Xu, Hai, and Chou in year and month are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Yimao:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Jiyou day.** Nine successes and ten reversals, but prosperity in later life. Year and month communicating with the body’s Birth and flourishing, and Xin and Gui above in the stems restraining Ding and Yi, are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Jiyou:** a vice minister. **Jichou / Dingmao:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Renxu / Dingwei:** Jia Mo, palace-examination first. **Dingmao / Bingwu:** an assistant commissioner. **Guichou / Yimao:** a prefect.〉

**Jihai day.** Autumn birth gives restrained Indirect Officer and wealth and distinction. Spring shortens life; summer strengthens the body and is auspicious; winter is ordinary. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Xinmao:** a transmission commissioner. **Yichou / Jiaxu:** a vice censor-in-chief. **Jisi / Dingchou:** a bureau director.〉

〈Indirect Officer and Indirect Seal:  
The person’s nature is firm and unyielding.  
A strong body is greatly auspicious;  
Weakness makes an ordinary life.〉

> For a Ji day at Mao hour, blessing is undermined;  
> Neither seeking fame nor seeking profit is suitable.  
> If the Body Palace contains punishment or control,  
> One leaves the native well and wanders east and west.

> A Ji day meeting Dingmao hour:  
> Inverted Food and Indirect Officer mingle.  
> You, Geng, and Xin break it, bringing troubled waters;  
> Plans and thoughts cannot find passage.  
> Little can be expected of parents or the ranks of geese;  
> After flowers fall, roots and shoots take hold anew.  
> Through fullness and loss, leaving home may establish a household;  
> Fortune begins amid mountains, woods, and ravines.[^12]

## 5. Ji Days, Wuchen Hour
### 六巳日戊辰時斷
<!-- source: L198-L245 -->

> A Ji day born at Wuchen hour:  
> The body gains its position and encounters Wealth Spirit.  
> Estates, wealth, distinction, and much sincerity;  
> Jia and Yi in the monthly framework bring Lu and Noble Person.

On a Ji day at Wuchen hour, Wealth’s Storehouse occupies its own place. Ji takes Ren and Gui as Wealth, entering the Tomb at Chen. Ji Earth holds its proper position: the person is sincere, trustworthy, rich, and distinguished. Communication with the month’s qi, or Jia and Yi exposed, gives Lu in the birth month and great distinction. Chen month strengthens the body, but gives no help from parents; seeing Jia and transforming into Earth gives great wealth. In winter and summer, flourishing Wealth generates Officer, bringing wealth and distinction. Western luck is ordinary.

**Jichou day.** Solitude. Yin and Mao months strengthen Lu; Chen month with Jia exposed is judged as transformed qi and gives distinction. Wu gives substantial wealth; Yin and You, censorial office. Zi and Chou in year and month with Jia exposed give gold and purple and close attendance in southeast luck; northwest luck is feared for retirement from office. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Yihai:** minister of the Court of Imperial Seals. **Yichou / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengwu / Yiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Yiwei / Bingxu:** a censor. **Renchen / Guimao:** Censor Fu Yingzhen, whose caption records remonstrance and posthumous appointment as a vice minister in a court. **Jiawu / Gengwu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^13]

**Jimao day.** Mao month gives censorial office. Water-and-Wood years and months with northeast luck are judged alike. 〈Recorded cases—**[Guiwei] / Renxu:** Chief Censor Luo Yao. **Gengyin / Yiyou:** a censor. **Guiyou / Wuwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dinghai / Dingwei:** a commander-in-chief. **Guiwei / Yimao:** palace-examination third. **Jiayin / Dingmao:** distinction. **Yimao / Guiwei:** wealth. **Wuwu / Jiazi:** skill at the game of go. **Yimao / Dinghai:** a provincial graduate. **Renyin / Jiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuchen / Jiayin:** Shu Hongzhi, palace-examination third.〉[^14]

**Jisi day.** Solitude, followed by late development. Spring brings noble Officer, summer stability, autumn violent misfortune, winter flourishing Wealth. Years and luck follow the same rule. 〈Recorded cases—**Renxu / Renyin:** Prefect Zhuang Renchun, who competed with the people for gain and came to harm. **Wuzi / Xinyou:** Gao Qinshun, salt commissioner for the Two Huai; illicit gains were recovered and he was publicly executed. **Renyin / Xinhai:** Li Tingji, first in the provincial and metropolitan examinations, second in the palace examination, and Grand Secretary; he accepted no gifts. **Wuyin / Yichou:** an assistant commissioner.〉

**Jiwei day.** Chou month gives auspicious Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer. Yin, Chen, Si, Hai, Wu, and Xu in year and month give literary distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinsi / Renchen:** Ding Shimei, palace-examination first; he rose no higher than vice minister and died in Dingchou year. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** a deputy commissioner. **Renshen / Bingwu:** a deputy commissioner. **Dingyou / Renzi:** an assistant prefect. **Wushen / Jiwei:** poverty and hardship. **Bingchen / Xinchou:** a monk.〉

**Jiyou day.** Spring strengthens Officer; summer is ordinary and auspicious; autumn gives violence and cruelty; winter strengthens Wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Guichou:** a vice minister. **Guisi / Dingsi:** an assistant bureau director. **Dingyou / Guimao:** a censor. **Gengwu / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Renwu:** Fang Fengnian, metropolitan graduate in Renxu and Grand Secretary; a violent death in Bingxu year. **Guiwei / Jiazi:** wealthy, entering the Secretariat through a contribution.〉

**Jihai day.** Chou month gives distinguished Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer. Xu month with Wood-and-Water luck gives sixth or seventh rank. Yin, Wu, Zi, and Chen in year and month give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Bingyin:** Chief Censor Zhou Guang. **Wuzi / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Guisi / Jiayin:** a tribute student. **Guiwei / Yimao:** Vice Censor-in-Chief Ye Yousheng. **Renyin / Xinhai:** a commander-in-chief.〉

〈Emerald Bamboo and Crimson Peach:  
Red glimmers faintly amid green.  
If Yin does not bring control or harm,  
The Storehouse opens upon the money-dragon.〉

> Chen hour upon a Ji day is no ordinary thing:  
> Within, the money-dragon guards the Storehouse.  
> Without Companions or Rob Wealth, and with auspicious luck,  
> Wealth and distinction surely bring broad estates.

> A Ji day meeting Wuchen hour:  
> The body meets a flourishing Storehouse, full to abundance.  
> Flowers fall, then truly bloom again;  
> Chou and Xu punish and clash, and Wealth grows strong.  
> Renshen gives Wealth and Officer in Double Beauty;  
> Wives are repeated, children succeed only late.  
> Parents and the ranks of geese are middling in their affairs:  
> A fate that stands alone and establishes itself.[^15]

## 6. Ji Days, Jisi Hour
### 六巳日己巳時斷
<!-- source: L246-L289 -->

> A Ji day born at Jisi hour:  
> Metal Spirit and Fire accord with one another.  
> Without the month’s qi, judge an ordinary life;  
> With it, honor comes through the higher examinations.

On a Ji day at Jisi hour, Metal and Fire combine. Ji takes Bing as Seal; Bing within Si is established and flourishing. Jisi is also Metal Spirit, seated in Fire’s position and joining with it. With a Fire month’s qi, no Wealth Star in the pillars damaging Seal, and no Wealth luck, blessing develops extraordinarily. Without the month’s qi, or with birth in winter, refinement lacks substance. Even without that communication, southern luck is auspicious.

**Jichou day.** Chen month with northwest luck gives distinction. Wu and Wei strengthen Fire and give great distinction. Shen, Zi, Xu, and Si are also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinchou / Yiwei:** provincial graduate Zhou Qixiang. **Xinmao / Dingyou:** an Imperial Bodyguard officer, violently killed. **Renchen / Dingwei:** a chancellor.〉

**Jimao day.** Flanking Chen’s Wealth Storehouse gives great wealth. Mao also takes Si as Traveling Horse; the two Si are considered together, giving “Seated upon Horse.” Further birth in Wu month gives both Lu and Horse, an especially distinguished fate. In Hai month, Wealth flourishes and Officer encounters Birth; in Si month, Metal Spirit encounters Fire. All give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiyou / Gengwu:** Governor Li Zhi. **Renzi / Guichou:** Weng Zhengchun, palace-examination first and minister; a clerk from Huguang shared the chart.〉[^16]

**Jisi day.** Wu in year and month gives imposing authority and illustrious reputation. Spring brings filial children and a worthy wife; autumn and winter are ordinary, distinguished but not prominent. Chen and Xu give Wealth, Seal, Officer, and Food; Shen gives Hurting Officer generating Wealth. All are judged auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Renxu:** Minister Kang Taihe, who died in Dingchou year. **Jiaxu / Wuchen:** Chief Censor Lin Tingyu. **Wuxu / Gengshen:** a transmission commissioner. **Gengchen / Jiachen:** a transport commissioner. **Jiachen / Renshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Renxu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Jiwei day.** This is Arching Lu. Without Yang Blade, Seven Killings, or Wu filling the empty position, and without Void, it gives manifest distinction. Hai, Chou, Chen, and Shen in year and month, with eastern luck, give the highest civil or military rank. This configuration is inferior to Dingsi–Dingwei, for Ding has proper Lu whereas Ji has lodged Lu. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Dinghai:** a ministry bureau director. **Gengzi / [Guiwei]:** a provincial graduate. **Yiyou / Jichou:** wealth. **Jisi / Bingzi:** longevity.〉[^17]

**Jiyou day.** In summer, Metal Spirit enters Fire’s country: manifest distinction. Years and luck follow the preceding rules. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Jiaxu:** Academician Zhao Zi'ang. **Jiyou / Jisi:** an academician. **Wuwu / Jiazi:** a provincial graduate. **Yisi / Xinsi:** a commander of a thousand households.〉

**Jihai day, [Jisi] hour.** Summer birth fills the eye with wealth; one enjoys life with a spirited bearing. Winter is ordinary. Xu month with southeast luck gives a state preceptor's gold and purple; Yin month also gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Dingsi:** Minister Qian Rujing. **Dingchou / Jiachen:** a deputy commissioner. **Yimao / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jihai / Jisi:** an assistant prefect.〉[^18]

〈Flourishing Fire and Metal Spirit:  
The southern course has favorable qi.  
If birth falls in a winter month,  
Wealth and Lu are only hollow flowers.〉

> A Ji day again encountering Jisi hour:  
> Metal Spirit’s transformation into strength must be fitting.  
> In southern Li, Wealth and Officer become manifest;  
> Eastern Yin and Mao are also exceptional.

> A Ji day meeting Jisi hour:  
> Summer birth, Bing Fire, and Metal Spirit.  
> Without Xu, Hai, or Gengshen,  
> And without breaking, reputation resounds.  
> One parent declines, the other prospers;  
> Punishment and Void make the enterprise falter.  
> To know advancement and a changed [household standing],  
> Look to southern luck where Fire flourishes.[^19]

## 7. Ji Days, Gengwu Hour
### 六巳日午時斷
<!-- source: L290-L331 -->

> A Ji day born at Wu hour:  
> Lu returning to the hour brings flourishing honor.  
> Officer Star appearing in the pillars is feared;  
> Concealed Brilliance, however, calls for another judgment.

On a Ji day at Gengwu hour, the Day’s Lu Returns to the Hour. Ji sees Geng as Hurting Officer and Yi as its proper ghost. At Wu, visible Geng combines with Yi: Hurting Officer combines with Killings, giving independent achievement. If year and month have no Yi and Hurting Officer approaches Defeat, but Jia and Bing appear in the pillars, this is Concealed Brilliance. With the configuration intact and free of clash or breaking, great distinction follows.[^20]

**Jichou day.** Yin-month birth gives distinction; summer is ominous, autumn violent, winter rich in Wealth but with few children. 〈One account says:〉 distinction throughout. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Renchen:** Chief Censor Lin Maoda. **Gengwu / Dinghai:** Vice Minister Yang Sizhong. **Gengshen / Xinsi:** an assistant commissioner. **Gengzi / Dinghai:** an assistant prefect. **Bingchen / Dingyou:** first in the military palace examination.〉

**Jimao day.** Ji’s Lu occupies Wu. Jia and Bing, or Yin and Wu, in year and month give great distinction. In Mao month Killings flourishes; Geng combining with it gives authority and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Yisi:** Minister Bi Qiang, from Shidai in Chizhou, Jiangnan; a Zhejiang licentiate shared the chart. **Jiashen / Yihai:** a ministry secretary. **Yiwei / Wuzi:** a renowned general. **Guimao / Yimao:** a Grand Preceptor. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** a provincial graduate. **Dingyou / Bingwu:** a provincial graduate. **Wushen / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Dingmao / Dingwei:** a ministry secretary. **Dingwei / Gengxu:** Luo Chaoguo, minister in the southern capital.〉

**Jisi day.** Distinction. Yin–Wu–Xu months place Metal Spirit in Fire’s country, judged through Seal-Ribbon: manifest distinction. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei in year and month give a marquis or earl. Luck combining with Killings must prevent a good end. You month gives distinction in southeast luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Dingwei:** Qu Jingchun, metropolitan-examination first, who rose no higher than vice minister; another account gives Bingwu month. **Yichou / Bingxu:** an imperial son-in-law. **Wuzi / Guihai:** a school instructor.〉

**Jiwei day.** Turning away from Lu and Hurting Officer: injury to the Six Relations. Without breaking, later life prospers. Wei and Xu in year and month give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Xinwei:** a transmission commissioner. **Xinyou / Yiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jimao / Jiaxu:** a commander-in-chief. **Bingshen / Dingyou:** an assistant regional commander. **Guihai / Jiwei:** a prefect. **Renchen / Wushen:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiyou day.** 〈Missing in the original.〉

〈Recorded case—**Bingshen / Guisi:** Ma Shiying, admitted to the Grand Secretariat at the end of the Ming and subjected to punishment.〉[^21]

**Jihai day.** Wu and Xu in year and month give Wealth, Officer, Lu, and Seal: great distinction. Yin year and Wu month give immense wealth; Mao month gives distinction in western luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Guichou:** Chief Censor Mao Gang. **Renshen / Bingwu:** a chancellor. **Renchen / Yisi:** a chief councillor. **Dingmao / Jiachen:** an assistant commissioner. **Xinwei / Yiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** a ministry secretary. **Bingzi / Jiawu:** a county magistrate.〉

〈The Day’s Lu Returning to the Hour:  
Ascent to azure clouds has its appointed time.  
If Officer comes to entangle it,  
Clash and breaking take away its excellence.〉

> Ji meeting Gengwu hour has Returning Lu;  
> Without breaking or clash, blessing may unfold.  
> If Bing and Jia appear in the pillars,  
> Virtue enriches the person, wealth enriches the house.

> A Ji day meeting Gengwu hour  
> Is called Turning from Lu and Hurting Officer.  
> Clash, punishment, breaking, and harm bring troubles without end;  
> Blood kin and the Six Relations are ice and burning coals.  
> Should Jia and Bing appear in the pillars,  
> Concealed Brilliance gives purity and leisure.  
> Reach as high as you please for the moon’s crimson cassia:  
> No calculation is needed to know wealth and distinction.

## 8. Ji Days, Xinwei Hour
### 六巳日未時斷
<!-- source: L332-L369 -->

> A Ji day born at Xinwei hour:  
> Food God and Officer’s Storehouse welcome one another.  
> Wood communicating with the month’s qi promises distinction;  
> Without that communication, it is a wealthy fate.

On a Ji day at Xinwei hour, Food God assists Officer. Ji takes Xin as Food and Jia as Officer; Wei is Officer’s Storehouse. At Wei, Xin is visible and Jia concealed. Support communicating with the month’s qi gives distinction. Food God born and flourishing surpasses Wealth and Officer. A month communicating with Wood qi strengthens Officer and is especially distinguished; without communication, there is blessing. Corresponding luck also gives distinction.[^22]

**Jichou day.** If not distinguished, then rich. A month communicating with Earth qi gives wealth; with Wood qi, distinction. 〈One account says:〉 punishment and misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Guimao:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Bingyin:** Tan Xiang, given the additional title of vice minister; he entered service through calligraphy and was later punished. **Xinchou / Jihai:** a woman of distinction.〉

**Jimao day.** You, Xu, Hai, Mao, and Wei in year and month give distinction; southeast luck gives gold and purple. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Guimao:** a chief councillor. **Dingchou / Guimao:** a chancellor. **Xinwei / Bingshen:** a ministry secretary. **Yimao / Wuzi:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jisi day.** A Metal-Spirit day. Yin, Wu, and Xu months give distinction. Hai, Mao, Chen, and Wu, with Metal, Fire, Water, or Wood luck, give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Dingmao:** Shang Lu, first in all three examinations, Grand Secretary, and renowned minister. **Jiachen / Wuchen:** the highest rank. **Jimao / Gengwu:** a deputy commissioner. **Jimao / Jisi:** a metropolitan graduate; died. **Renshen / Jiachen:** an assistant prefect. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiwei day.** Spring, summer, or Chou month gives an exceptional bearing, clear and discriminating speech, high reputation, and weighty emolument. Autumn and winter are ordinary. Wood-and-Fire luck brings punishment and harm, yet develops wealth. 〈One account says:〉 self-injury amid wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Renwu:** a privy councillor. **Guisi / Bingchen:** a deputy commissioner. **Jiwei / Renshen:** wealth. **Gengwu / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Jiyou day.** Spring brings reversals; summer is auspicious; autumn shortens life; winter brings abundant wealth. Mao month with Metal-and-Water luck gives fifth rank or higher. Chou month with southwest luck gives a bureau directorship. Chen and Xu are the Storehouses of Wealth and Seal and are both auspicious. 〈One account says:〉 prosperity after ominous punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinsi / Renchen:** Minister Cai Kelian. **Gengxu / Wuzi:** Lin Jiansu’s father, posthumously made minister. **Jiayin / Bingzi:** an associate prefect.〉

**Jihai day.** Spring gives distinction, summer stability, autumn poverty, winter wealth. You month is Ji Earth’s Birth, and Food God encounters Lu: enjoyment comes naturally. 〈One account says:〉 an ominous loss amid distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Renshen:** a chief councillor. **Renshen / Jiyou:** a bureau director.〉[^23]

〈Officer Star in the Hour’s Storehouse:  
Spring is most auspicious.  
Luck travels where Officer flourishes,  
And the name naturally spreads fragrance.〉

> A Ji day encountering Xinwei hour:  
> Who knows the solitude beside the lamp-lit window?  
> When luck brings flourishing Wealth and flourishing Officer,  
> Name and profit both arrive—do not resent their lateness.

> A Ji day at Xinwei hour:  
> Food sits upon Officer’s Storehouse, which must be opened.  
> Chou and Xu punish and clash, revealing Officer and Wealth;  
> Bolted shut, it obstructs the road ahead.  
> The gentleman’s literary gifts receive blessing;  
> The common person excels in trade.  
> A key opens the blessing allotted in the fate:  
> Wealth goes and comes, yet there is always wealth.

## 9. Ji Days, Renshen Hour
### 六巳日申時斷
<!-- source: L370-L405 -->

> A Ji day born at Renshen hour:  
> The Heavenly Origin is damaged, its qi incomplete.  
> If the Heavenly Origin loses its footing and has no support,  
> Poverty or early death makes life hard to prolong.

On a Ji day at Renshen hour, Water flourishes and Earth is insubstantial. Ji takes Jia as Officer, Ren as Wealth, and Geng as Turning away from Lu. At Shen, Geng flourishes, Ren is born, Jia is extinguished, and Ji Earth’s qi is defeated. Communication with the Earth qi of the season-ending months, or luck carrying Earth qi, is auspicious. Losing the season, without support or communication with the month’s qi, gives either poverty or early death; otherwise disability and wretchedness in later life.

**Jichou day.** You in year and month, with Seal exposed in the stems, gives wealth, distinction, love of propriety, and a generous nature. Chen and Xu are auspicious. A Hai year gives the great configuration Qian Clear and Kun Level. Yin and Shen make Officer and Noble assist one another: all are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Dingyou:** a chancellor. **Guiwei / Guihai:** a senior provincial administrator. **Renzi / Yisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Bingxu:** distinction. **Wushen / Bingchen:** wealth. **Jichou / Renshen:** wealth.〉

**Jimao day.** Chen month gives Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer and early distinction. Hai month with southern luck gives fifth or sixth rank; Yin and Shen years give third or second rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Gengzi:** a chancellor. **Xinhai / Wuxu:** a chancellor. **Xinhai / Gengyin:** a chancellor. **Renshen / Renzi:** a junior provincial administrator. **Jiayin / Renshen:** a vice minister. **Renzi / Jiachen:** Wang, palace-examination first. **Xinmao / Dingyou:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Yichou / Jichou:** a chief censor. **Renyin / Jiyou:** Rujin, son of Lord Sun Zhonglie, in the Imperial Bodyguard.〉

**Jisi day.** Great misfortune and punishment. Si, You, and Chou months give Hurting Officer, auspicious in Wealth luck. Yin–Wu–Xu places Metal Spirit in Fire’s country and gives distinction in southwest luck. Shen–Zi–Chen gives distinction in Wood-and-Fire luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Renchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yisi / Bingxu:** a censor.〉

**Jiwei day.** Birth in Si month gives strings of cash rotting and grain grown old in storage. Hai, Zi, or Yin month, with Metal-and-Fire luck, gives a marquis or earl. Chen and Hai in year and month give distinction in close attendance. 〈Recorded cases—**Guisi / Renxu:** a prefect. **Yihai / Xinsi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Wuwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiwei / Jiashen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Yichou:** wealth and three sons.〉[^24]

**Jiyou day.** Si, You, and Chou months give auspicious Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed. Yin month brings violence and misfortune; Mao and Wei give military office; Zi and Wei give a duke or marquis. 〈One account says:〉 disordered mind and kidney illness, with great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Jiwei:** Commander-in-Chief Yu Dayou, described as cunning. **Wushen / Yichou:** Chief Censor Dai Shizhong. **Gengwu / Jiashen:** a chief censor. **Guihai / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengxu / Guiwei:** a provincial graduate.〉[^25]

**Jihai day.** Mao and Wei form the triple combination gathering Officer; Seal exposed in the stems gives great distinction. Wuzi month has flourishing Wealth generating Officer. At You, Ji Earth receives generation; Jia and Bing exposed are both auspicious.

〈Luan and Phoenix Perching in the Wutong:  
Soaring aloft is no great difficulty.  
But should punishment, control, or breaking come,  
Toil will never allow repose.〉

> Ji as day stem meeting Shen hour  
> Is best without breaking or clash.  
> Even though Officer is absent from the pillars,  
> Flourishing Wealth still establishes the foundation.

> Renshen hour meeting a Ji day:  
> Triple Combination is fitting within it.  
> Tianyi Noble Person enters the framework directly;  
> Employ Wealth and Officer for manifest distinction.  
> Wu, Ji, Chen, and Xu attain their places;  
> Know broad learning and literary accomplishment.  
> A worthy wife, filial children, blessing and refinement complete:  
> Punishment and breaking trouble middle life.

## 10. Ji Days, Guiyou Hour
### 六巳日酉時斷
<!-- source: L406-L447 -->

> A Ji day born at Guiyou hour:  
> In Bath’s country, Water and Earth are muddied.  
> Wealth and Food hidden in the branch gather and scatter;  
> A weak body losing its ground can scarcely preserve its years.

On a Ji day at Guiyou hour, Water and Earth are turbid. Ji takes Gui as Wealth and Xin as Food; at You, Gui is ill and Xin flourishes. Companions seize Wealth, which repeatedly gathers and disperses. If the body is also weak and loses its ground, success and failure alternate; otherwise life is shortened. A month communicating with bodily strength, or corresponding luck, is auspicious.

**Jichou day, [Guiyou] hour.** Spring is ordinary, summer stable. Autumn gives Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed, bringing imposing authority; winter gives wealth. Bingyin or Si month places Metal Spirit with Fire and gives distinction. Chou month with Metal-and-Water luck gives a bureau directorship; Yi exposed gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Xinsi:** a mobile-force commander, who lived fifty-nine years. **Yisi / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^26]

**Jimao day.** Day and hour meet as Mao and You: relocation and injury through punishment. Hurting Officer also carries Killings, indicating violence of disposition and delay in affairs. Birth in a season-ending month is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Renzi:** the Ming Jianwen ruler. Water and Earth are defeated at You hour; Shattering is offended, fitting the loss of the realm and a wandering life. **Dingmao / Guimao:** a deputy commissioner.〉

**Jisi day.** Si, You, and Chou months give Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed, bringing military or censorial office. Yin, Mao, and Xu months place Metal Spirit in Fire’s country: distinction and eloquence, but speech can injure that distinction. Restrain the heart and retire early, for Water and Earth are defeated at You. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Jiazi:** a deputy commissioner. **Jisi / Renshen:** distinction and [early death]. **Jimao / Bingyin:** minor distinction and early retirement. **Gengshen / Wuyin:** wealthy, but robbed.〉[^27]

**Jiwei day.** Xu, Hai, Chou, and Chen in year and month give distinction in western luck. Zi and Si give great distinction; Mao and Chen give close attendance but not great rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuzi / Dingsi:** Minister Yang Zhao; [four stems and] four branches exchange Noble Persons, all present. **Renchen / Gengxu:** wealth. **Jiwei / Jiaxu:** a transport commissioner.〉[^28]

**Jiyou day.** Indirect Wealth encountering Food gives prominence. Chou, Wei, Zi, and Wu are all auspicious. You month gives a relative of the inner palace. If Si and You both occur in year and month, Shattering Sha is offended: a lifetime of ruin and wandering, without fruition. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Xinyou:** a prefect. **Xinwei / Xinchou:** distinction. **Renzi / Bingwu:** a provincial graduate. **Jiazi / Dingmao:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jihai day.** A month communicating with Earth qi gives distinction in Wood-and-Fire luck. 〈Recorded case—**Renwu / Guichou:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈Food God Generating Wealth:  
Punishment and injury—and still more clash—are feared.  
If none of these signs offends,  
Sooner or later the household’s fortunes change.〉

> Ji meeting Gui Water at You hour:  
> Food God born and flourishing brings composure.  
> But a weak body with clash, control, and breaking  
> Is reckoned no more than middling.

> A Ji day meeting Guiyou hour:  
> Indirect Wealth welcomes Food God.  
> Neither the ranks of geese nor parents can be relied upon;  
> Character and feeling are unsettled.  
> Wealth comes and goes, gathering and dispersing;  
> Much is seen before the eyes, but little can be kept.  
> Flowers open, flowers fall, then open anew:  
> This fate first resists, then follows smoothly.

## 11. Ji Days, Jiaxu Hour
### 六巳日戌時斷
<!-- source: L448-L491 -->

> A Ji day born at Jiaxu hour:  
> The wife follows the husband, transforming into true Earth.  
> Communication with the month’s qi makes Lu’s source deep;  
> Reverse this, and judge an ordinary life.

On a Ji day at Jiaxu hour, the wife follows the husband in transformation. Ji combines with Jia Wood, forming an Earth configuration; Earth Spirit gathers refinement, and the source of Lu is deep and substantial. Without transformation in the month, take Jia as Officer and Bing as Seal: within Xu Earth, Jia Wood takes form and Bing Fire joins the configuration. Communication with the month’s qi gives distinction. Without it, and with punishment, clash, breaking, or harm, the outcome is ordinary. Even if the person prospers, the parents are lost early.

〈Recorded case placed here in the text—**Renchen / Guimao:** a censor.〉[^29]

**Jichou day.** Injury to father; excessive strength. Chen-month birth gives substantial Earth. Si, Wu, Wei, and Shen occupy Coming to Office and Imperial Prosperity and are auspicious; Hai, Xu, Yin, and Mao bring Earth to Illness or Death and are ominous. You and Chen give third or fourth rank, with retirement in middle life. Hai with Wood-and-Fire luck gives sixth or seventh rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiachen / Guiyou:** a school instructor. **Dingsi / Xinhai:** Assistant Prefect Zhou Jichang. **Renxu / Renzi:** provincial-examination first.〉

**Jimao day.** Bing, Ding, Wu, and Xu in year and month, with Geng and Xin in the stems restraining Officer and Killings, and birth amid bright mountains and clear waters, give second or third rank. Wei, You, or Chou month with Fire-and-Earth luck gives fourth or fifth rank. Wu month with northeast luck gives great distinction; Zi [month] with Wood-and-Fire luck gives censorial office. Chen and Si, with support, give the highest rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Renzi:** Vice Minister Wang Zhengguo. **Jisi / Wuchen:** Chief Censor Chen Yi. **Renwu / Yisi:** great wealth. **Yiyou / Bingxu:** a prefect. **Wuchen / Yimao:** wealth and five sons. **Xinyou / Gengyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Xinyou / Renchen:** a ministry bureau director.〉[^30]

**Jisi day.** Yin–Wu–Xu months place Metal Spirit in Fire’s country: manifest distinction. Hai and Xu in year and month give prefectural or county office and an outstanding reputation. Zi month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives the highest rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Guiyou:** an assistant bureau director. **Dingchou / Renyin:** a supervising secretary. **Wushen / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jiwei day.** Injury and punishment. Yin and Wu in year and month give third or fourth rank; Xu month with southeast luck, fifth rank. Pure Xinmao in year and month brings punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Renxu:** a bureau director, with a ministry secretary and a provincial graduate also given under the same pair. **Wuxu / Yichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renwu / Gengxu:** received an honorary official title; both sons were distinguished.〉

**Jiyou day.** Chen and Chou punish and clash, increasing the sources of wealth. You and Xu harm one another: name and profit are middling. Zi month ennobles Ji; Yin month gives Officer and Seal, with distinction in Seal luck. Pure Yiyou in year and month gives obstinacy and violence. 〈One account says:〉 solitary and away from home. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Bingxu:** Minister Li Kezhai. **Gengyin / Wuzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinmao / Renchen:** a provincial graduate. **Jiaxu / Guiyou:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Jihai day.** A month communicating with Earth qi needs Wood luck; a Water-and-Wood month needs luck strengthening the body. Both give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingyou / Xinhai:** Minister Song Jing. **Yichou / Gengchen:** an academician. **Jiwei / Bingzi:** a censor.〉

〈Autumn Leaves Meeting Frost:  
In a moment green turns red.  
Only let the signs of clash and control arrive,  
And the leaves scatter east and west.〉

> Jia and Ji transform into Earth, its qi stored within;  
> If a key is found, blessing unfolds at ease.  
> Should Wealth appear, that Wealth does not gather:  
> Like leaves encountering the depths of autumn.

> A Ji day meeting Jiaxu hour:  
> The wife following the husband in transformation is good.  
> The Storehouse’s Wealth waits only for a key;  
> Ren, Shen, and the Chou spirit bring free passage.  
> Parents suffer early death, solitude, punishment, or control;  
> The ranks of geese, flowers, and fruit find little harmony.  
> But should the appointed luck arrive all at once,  
> The household flourishes in abundant ease.

## 12. Ji Days, Yihai Hour
### 六巳日亥時斷
<!-- source: L492-L551 -->

> A Ji day born at Yihai hour:  
> Officer hidden, Killings exposed—no excellence yet.  
> Only Metal restraining Killings makes it auspicious;  
> Without a strong body, the misfortune is plain.

On a Ji day at Yihai hour, Killings is visible and Officer concealed. Ji takes Jia as Officer and Yi as ghost. At Hai, visible Yi and hidden Jia make mixed Officer and Killings. Xin in the pillars restraining Yi removes Killings and retains Officer; communication with the month’s qi gives manifest distinction. At Hai, Water flourishes and Earth is insubstantial: wandering, lost livelihood, Wealth that cannot be gathered, success and failure, advance and retreat. Body and ghost must be balanced for good fortune. Even without the month’s qi, corresponding luck is auspicious.

**Jichou day.** Si, You, and Chou months restrain Killings and retain Officer, giving distinction. Wu month strengthens the body and also gives distinction. Zi month with Fire luck gives seventh or eighth rank. Mao and Wei with Xin exposed to restrain Yi give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Jihai:** Grand Secretary Yan Ne. **Bingwu / Gengzi:** Chief Censor Qin Shang. **Renshen / Guichou:** a vice minister. **Jisi / Gengwu:** a deputy commissioner. **Jichou / Gengwu:** Ministry Secretary Wu Yiqin, who died [young]. **Guimao / Yichou:** Minister Shen Yingwen; a National University student surnamed Cheng from Shexian shared the chart and was immensely rich. **Yichou / Jichou:** Vice Minister Zhao Biao.〉[^31]

**Jimao day.** Mao month gives Indirect Officer. Heavy Killings and a yielding body are judged as Abandoning the Fate. Hidden Ren gives lofty spirit and aspiration. Restraint in the pillars, with Earth-and-Metal luck, gives third or fourth rank. Chou month gives Mixed Qi; Chen and Si with western luck give gold and purple. Hai month with Water luck shortens life. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinsi / Guisi:** a minister. **Guiwei / Wuwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Dinghai:** poor and dissolute, with many unworthy sons; unable to escape in an ominous year. **Renchen / Xinhai:** poverty and suffering. **Xinsi / Jihai:** disabling illness.〉

**Jisi day.** Yin–Wu–Xu months place Metal Spirit in Fire’s country and give distinction. Si month with northwest luck gives the rank of duke or high minister. Mao and Wu give censorial office. Pure Zi and Hai in year and month make Wealth side with Killings: judge Abandonment, giving great military command. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Dingwei:** Grand Secretary Li Xiya. **Wushen / [Dingsi]:** Minister Jin Youzi, a renowned minister. **Wuzi / Guihai:** Commander-in-Chief Qi Jiguang, a renowned general. **Jihai / Renshen:** Shen Maoxue, palace-examination first. **Yimao / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^32]

**Jiwei day.** Without punishment, breaking, or mixture, there is pure, lofty wealth and distinction, with praiseworthy literary learning. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Dingmao:** Vice Minister Cui Dongzhou. **Gengxu / Jichou:** Hanlin scholar Huang Zuo. **Yihai / Dinghai:** Chief Censor Cai Baishi. **Bingyin / Dingyou:** Li Min, palace-examination first. **Bingchen / Renchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiyou / Dingsi:** an associate prefect. **Renchen / Renyin:** a bureau official by inherited privilege.〉

**Jiyou day.** Spring and summer bring advancement. In autumn restraint is excessive: an impoverished scholar. Winter strengthens Wealth. Wu, Si, and Xu in year and month give sixth or seventh rank; Yin month with Metal-and-Fire luck gives fourth or fifth rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Bingxu:** Vice Minister Dong Fen. **Yihai / Xinsi:** a provincial administrator. **Yimao / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yihai / Dinghai:** a ministry bureau director. **Jiawu / Yihai:** a military-circuit intendant. **Guiyou / Yimao:** a person subjected to punishment.〉

**Jihai day.** Xin exposed in year and month to restrain gives distinction; even without restraint, exceptional advancement is indicated. Xu month gives censorial office; Si also gives distinction. Chou is a palace of substantial Earth, bringing third rank. 〈One account says:〉 self-punishment, much success and failure, and wealth late in life. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Jiayin:** Supreme Commander Ying Jia, a renowned minister. **Guichou / Gengshen:** a prefect. **Gengyin / Bingxu:** a chief supervising secretary. **Yichou / Dinghai:** Hanlin Compiler Huang Mu. **Jiachen / Wuchen:** Shu Fen, palace-examination first. **Jimao / Guiwei:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

〈Those born on these six days at this hour are often men of letters, entering through high examination success and occupying prestigious, responsible offices.〉

〈A Fish Entering the Deep Pool:  
Its purpose fulfilled, it becomes whale and giant turtle.  
Without punishment, Void, control, or breaking,  
Its name is marked in the metropolitan administration.〉

> The Heavenly Origin Yihai stands within the hour;  
> Traveling Horse and Birth are no ordinary things.  
> A strong body and strong Killings bring blessing and Lu together;  
> Achievement and advancement naturally light the face with joy.

> A Ji day meeting Yihai hour:  
> Indirect Officer welcomes Direct Wealth.  
> A strong body makes it good as well;  
> Mixture among the Heavenly Origins cuts the blessing in half.  
> Engagement and withdrawal, advance and retreat are unsettled;  
> The Six Relations and the ranks of geese rise and decline.  
> As luck proceeds, Lu and Horse naturally arrive:  
> Wealth, distinction, and untroubled freedom.

## 13. Geng Days, Bingzi Hour
### 六庚日子時斷
<!-- source: L552-L607 -->

> A Geng day born at Bingzi hour:  
> Body and ghost both decline; Retreat Spirit is strong.  
> With support, splendor; without it, low station.  
> Ghost at Birth and flourishing makes long life difficult.

On a Geng day at Bingzi hour, body and ghost are both weak. Geng takes Gui as Hurting Officer and Bing as ghost. At Zi, Geng dies, Bing Fire lacks qi, and Gui Water is established and flourishing. A supported body is auspicious. Without support, and with luck that weakens the body and strengthens ghost, one is windblown, short-lived, and lowly. A month communicating with Fire qi needs western luck for distinction; a weak body does not obtain that result.

**Gengzi day.** Distinction. Further Zi in year and month clashes forth Ding Officer and Ji Seal from Wu: Flying Heavenly Lu and Horse. Without Wealth or Officer filling the empty position in the pillars, there is distinction. A monk or Daoist remains unstained by a speck of dust and accomplishes all dharmas; an ordinary person gains name and profit, but injures wife and children. 〈One account says:〉 poverty before wealth. Avoid Jisi month: ruin, punishment, and poverty; Guiwei month: solitude; Jihai month: punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Dingyou:** Chancellor Qiao Xingjian. **Dingmao / Renzi:** a vice minister in a court. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a vice minister in a court. **Guiyou / Guihai:** a bureau director. **Jiachen / Bingzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Jiayin:** Minister Guo Zizhang. **Gengchen / Guiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiwei / Bingzi:** a ministry bureau director.〉

**Gengyin day.** Spring birth carries Wealth and Killings; Metal-and-Fire luck gives gold and purple. Summer strengthens Killings and gives great distinction. Autumn strengthens the body and requires southern luck for distinction. In winter, body and ghost are both weak: ordinary. Pure Wu in year and month gives the rank of duke or high minister. Renzi places Food before and Killings behind; with the birth of the first yang, Bing Fire obtains qi, giving the highest distinction attainable by a subject. Avoid Guiyou month: ruin and misfortune; Xinhai month: bloodshed; Jiazi month: early death.

〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Renzi:** the Ming Chenghua emperor. **Dingwei / Yisi:** Huo Tao, metropolitan-examination first and minister, a renowned minister. **Bingwu / Jiawu:** Huang, Minister of Personnel. **Renyin / Yisi:** Chen, palace-examination first. **Jiyou / Xinwei:** Vice Minister He Tianqu. **Guihai / Renxu:** Chief Censor Wei Lianggui. **Xinmao / Guisi:** a deputy commissioner. **Gengwu / Dinghai:** a censor. **Dingchou / Wushen:** a provincial graduate. **Jiwei / Jisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renxu / Renyin:** Vice Minister Chen Yao. **Dingmao / Guichou:** provincial-examination first. **Renchen / Yichou:** a remonstrance official, who died in the fourth month of Xinsi year. **Dingyou / Renzi:** a Grand Tutor. **Gengxu / Wuyin:** Minister Wang Zuo. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** provincial examination in Yimao, metropolitan degree in Jiaxu; served in an administrative circuit and a salt circuit. Xu and Ren luck were both unfavorable. The original has a lacuna.〉[^33]

**Gengchen day.** A month communicating with Wood-and-Fire qi finds excellence in western luck. Avoid Yisi month: ruin and punishment; Dingyou month: ominous punishment amid flourishing; Jichou month: ruin and misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Bingshen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yisi / Xinsi:** metropolitan-examination first. **Jisi / Jiaxu:** an official in Rites. **Jiyou / Bingzi:** a county magistrate. **Jiayin / Xinwei:** two provincial graduates shared the chart; one had three sons, the other none. **Xinsi / Gengyin:** Lin Yuanmei; five ministers among his sons and grandsons. **Wuchen / Jiazi:** Yuan Hongdao, in Personnel.〉

**Gengwu day.** Distinction. Hour and day clash, raising concern for wife and children. Southwest luck is welcome. A Fire-and-Wood month gives third- or fourth-rank censorial office. Autumn birth leaves Bing Fire without qi, making offspring difficult. Avoid Jisi month: ancestral ruin and misfortune; Jihai month: robbery and misfortune; Guichou month: solitude. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Guichou:** Minister He Ao. **Xinwei / Gengzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jimao / Guiyou:** a commander-in-chief.〉

**Gengshen day.** Shen-month birth in a region of substantial Earth gives distinction. Chen and Wei in year and month make western Metal luck auspicious. Xinsi month brings ominous punishment; Xinhai month, solitude and early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuchen / Jiwei:** a duke. **Jiazi / [Xinwei]:** Grand Secretary Li Ben; another account gives Renwu hour. **Yichou / Dinghai:** a bureau director. **Bingwu / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuyin / Gengshen:** a mobile-force commander. **Renshen / Renyin:** provincial-examination first. **Bingshen / Dingyou:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^34]

**Gengxu day.** Spring or summer birth with southwest luck, or autumn birth with Wood-and-Fire luck, gives distinction. Avoid Yisi month: ominous ruin; Yihai month: official trouble and much ominous punishment; Jichou month: ruin and misfortune; Wuxu month: violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Yichou:** Chancellor Wen Yanbo. **Guichou / Renxu:** Vice Minister Ma Xiyuan. **Yimao / Bingxu:** Ren, vice censor-in-chief. **Wuchen / Wuwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingxu / Xinchou:** an official in Personnel. **Yimao / Dinghai:** great distinction. **Renchen / Jiachen:** Minister Xiao Daheng. **Xinchou / Jiawu:** an assistant commissioner. **Bingzi / Wuxu:** Vice Minister Huang Maoguan, killed amid mutinous troops. Deputy Commissioner Shen Jie shared the chart. Huang was from Min, Shen from Wei. Shen died first, Huang later; Shen had no son, Huang one.〉[^35]

〈A Swimming Fish Escaping the Net:  
It leaps and reaches the Heavenly Ford.  
Luck turns misfortune into blessing;  
When the time arrives, poverty cannot hold it.〉

> A Geng day encountering Bingzi hour:  
> Hurting Officer joining the configuration is no rare excellence.  
> Parents and ancestral estate are hard to establish;  
> Swallow’s chamber and luan’s perch take root elsewhere.

> A Geng day meeting Bingzi hour:  
> It punishes Officer and turns from Lu to settle the body.  
> Parents suffer injury; hardship comes early;  
> The ranks of geese cannot live in harmony.  
> Abandoning study for trade develops blessing;  
> Estates see diligence first and idleness later.  
> The household’s wealth takes shape only in old age:  
> A fate dark at first, then bright.

## 14. Geng Days, Dingchou Hour
### 六庚日丑時斷
<!-- source: L608-L661 -->
〈For the six Geng days below, unfavorable months are to be considered together with the hour, as above.〉

> A Geng day born at Dingchou hour:  
> Officer meets noble ground, but Fire is too slight.  
> Wood-and-Fire luck opens the way to carriage and crown;  
> Without that passage, standing alone brings only an empty name.

On a Geng day at Dingchou hour, Metal is heavy and Fire slight. Geng takes Ding as Officer and Ji as Seal. At Chou, Ding Fire’s qi is slight and Ji Earth occupies its proper position. A month communicating with Wood-and-Fire qi brings Officer and Seal to Birth and flourishing and gives distinction; without it, only an empty name. A month in which Fire flourishes and Earth is generated gives wealth. Even without that, corresponding luck brings reputation.

**Gengzi day.** Spring and summer give distinction; autumn is ordinary; winter gives solitude and injury. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Dinghai:** a military-preparedness commissioner. **Renwu / Yisi:** a prefect. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** a ministry secretary. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** a provincial graduate. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** Vice Minister He Lang. **Renzi / Bingwu:** Zhou Wenzhu, vice chancellor of the Directorate of Education. **Xinyou / Bingyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Guihai:** dependent upon others for livelihood.〉

**Gengyin day.** A foremost examination placing. Yin, Mao, Wu, Wei, and Hai months give a refined, lofty fate; Wu and Wei luck bring manifest distinction. Years and months communicating with Fire and Earth give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Yichou:** Lun Yixun, metropolitan-examination first and palace-examination second, who rose no higher than vice minister. **Yisi / Jichou:** a provincial graduate. **Renyin / Guichou:** a commander-in-chief. **Wuwu / Renxu:** vice minister of the Court of Imperial Sacrifices. **Guichou / Guihai:** “zhong,” an incomplete caption. **Jimao / Renwu:** a provincial graduate who met calamity.〉[^36]

**Gengchen day.** Chou month gives wealth but shortens life; Si gives close attendance and great distinction. A month communicating with flourishing Fire and generated Earth gives distinction. Without it, corresponding luck still gives wealth and reputation. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuchen / Wuwu:** a chancellor. **Wuzi / Guihai:** a provincial graduate. **Jiawu / Wuchen:** Supervising Secretary Hu Ruqin. **Yiyou / Yiyou:** Gu Xichou, Minister of Rites. **Jisi / Gengwu:** dependent upon others for livelihood.〉

**Gengwu day.** A month communicating with Earth qi gives distinction; otherwise, wealth and reputation. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Wuzi:** Chancellor Yan Meng. **Gengzi / Wuyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Gengyin / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiawu / Xinwei:** an associate prefect. **Wuxu / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate. **Jisi / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate. **Jichou / Gengwu:** wealth. **Renzi / Jiyou:** Yang Daobin, palace-examination second and vice minister. **Jiayin / Xinwei:** Zhang Sixiu, palace-examination second, banished to frontier service. **Renxu / Jiyou:** struck by thunder at thirty-four. **Gengchen / Renwu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^37]

**Gengshen day.** Chou month with Metal-and-Fire luck gives the highest rank. Chen, Si, Wu, Wei, and Xu months strengthen both Officer and Seal and give distinction. Shen and You make the body too strong, requiring Wood and Fire. Yin and Mao make Wealth too strong, requiring Metal and Water. Hai and Zi leave Metal cold and Water chilly, requiring Fire-and-Earth luck for distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Gengchen:** a minister. **Renshen / Jiyou:** Chief Censor Chen Xuchuang. **Jisi / Jisi:** a deputy commissioner. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a censor. **Renwu / Bingwu:** an assistant prefect. **Bingwu / Gengzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jisi / Gengwu:** provincial-examination first. **Gengzi / Dinghai:** Lin Chun ze, metropolitan graduate and prefect, from Houguan; lived one hundred and four years. **Wushen / Xinyou:** a childless censor.〉

**Gengxu day.** Spring strengthens Wealth; summer strengthens Officer; autumn is plain; winter lacks force. 〈One account says:〉 punishment, followed by development after forty. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Dingwei:** a vice minister. **Gengshen / Renwu:** a prefect. **Yiwei / Wuzi:** a county magistrate. **Jiwei / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate. **Wuxu / Dingsi:** a provincial graduate. **Dingyou / Guichou:** provincial-examination first. **Dingyou / Dingwei:** a military-circuit intendant. **Yiyou / Jimao:** Huang Daozhou, director of the Heir Apparent’s Household; the caption calls him a pretender to the Learning of the Way. He received the court flogging in Gengchen and was killed while leading troops in Bingxu.〉[^38]

〈Qi Exhaling a Crimson Rainbow:  
Granary gates and Storehouse doors open.  
One morning the appointed luck arrives,  
And blessing and Lu come of themselves.〉

> Geng and Ding joining, complete at Chou hour:  
> A lovely image, the bright moon shining in blue Heaven.  
> Without punishment or control, it is truly rare;  
> Wealth and Officer prosper, and the years grow long.

> A Geng day meeting Dingchou hour:  
> Direct Officer and Wealth’s Storehouse accompany it.  
> Wu, Wei, and Xu months give blessing in abundance;  
> Geng finds the golden coffer within the hour.  
> Metal encountering Fire becomes a useful vessel;  
> Children must be sheltered and a wife receive a patent.  
> Song and wine are never far away:  
> Surely a life of leisure and delight.[^39]

## 15. Geng Days, Wuyin Hour
### 六庚日寅時斷
<!-- source: L662-L705 -->

> A Geng day born at [Wuyin] hour:  
> Fire is born, Metal extinguished, and blessing diminished.  
> Yet a month of Following Reformation, or an autumn birth,  
> Can make a pillar and cornerstone of the imperial house.[^40]

On a Geng day at Wuyin hour, Fire is born and Metal extinguished. Geng takes Bing as ghost and Wu as Inverted Food. At Yin, visible Wu combines with Gui and transforms into Fire, while Geng Metal’s qi is extinguished. Without a month of flourishing Metal or rescuing assistance, there is early death, poverty, and low station. In Si month Geng is born and Bing strong: body and ghost are both powerful. Western luck brings fierce courage and military distinction. Shen, You, Chou, and Xu months combine Metal and Fire, transforming ghost into Officer; luck strengthening the body adds distinction.

**Gengzi day.** Yin and Wu in year and month give examination success and advancement. Pure Shen gives third or fourth rank. Chou month with Metal-and-Fire luck gives a duke or high minister. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Jichou:** Minister Lü Zhen. **Jiwei / Guiyou:** the highest rank. **Renwu / Renyin:** a vice minister. **Jichou / Wuchen:** a surveillance commissioner. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengyin / Bingxu:** an assistant regional commander sentenced to death. **Bingwu / Guisi:** an associate prefect. **Yichou / Renwu:** Li Tengfang, deputy director of the Heir Apparent’s Household. **Dingmao / Xinhai:** an assistant transmission commissioner. **Wuwu / Yimao:** a duke. **Bingxu / Gengyin:** the Prince of Fu.〉

**Gengyin day.** A month communicating with a Fire configuration, or autumn birth with luck strengthening the body, gives distinction. Gengzi month brings its own Death; Xinyou in the year brings poverty, early death, or disability. A declining body encountering excessively strong Bing is judged alike. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Yihai:** a minister. **Renchen / Jiyou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Renchen / Renyin:** a surveillance commissioner. **Renzi / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengyin / Jichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jihai / Bingyin:** a garrison commander. **Dingmao / Dingwei:** a county magistrate. **Yiyou / Gengchen:** a subprefect. **Gengyin / Wuyin:** the highest rank. **Jihai / Bingxu:** a censor.〉

**Gengchen day.** Spring or summer birth with Bing and Ding exposed gives distinction in luck strengthening the body. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Xinmao:** a minister. **Renzi / Renyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renxu / Renyin:** a subprefect. **Wuxu / Jiayin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingmao / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate. **Renxu / Dingwei:** a capital prefect. **Wuchen / Jiazi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Gengwu day.** In Yin–Wu–Xu months Metal declines and Fire flourishes, indicating disability. Pure Yin, however, gives the greatest distinction. Autumn’s strong Metal frequently gives distinction, but Loss and Robbery in the hour controlling the body are ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Bingyin:** chancellor of the Directorate of Education. **Gengwu / Wuyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Gengshen / Wuyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingchou / Jiyou:** an assistant commissioner. **Wuxu / Gengshen:** a surveillance commissioner. **Renwu / Bingwu:** a commander-in-chief. **Gengzi / Jichou:** a commander. **Bingshen / Gengyin:** wealth and longevity. **Xinsi / Bingshen:** a violent death.〉

**Gengshen day.** Hour and day clash, raising concern for wife and children. Yin, Mao, and Chen months with Fire-and-Metal luck give a marquis or earl. Spring birth with southwest luck gives great distinction. Yin month’s distinction does not endure. 〈One account says:〉 punishment first, good fortune afterward. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Bingxu:** Minister Peng Shao. **Jiyou / Wuchen:** Minister Yu Zijun, a renowned minister. **Guihai / Renxu:** Chen, palace-examination first. **Bingxu / Jihai:** a wealthy provincial graduate. **Dingchou / Guimao:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Gengxu day.** Distinction. Xu month gives distinguished Mixed-Qi Wealth, Officer, and Seal-Ribbon. Gengchen year and Jimao month give a marquis or earl. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Bingshen:** Privy Councillor [Fu] Boshou. **Jiwei / Dingchou:** a Secretariat official. **Renshen / Gengxu:** a transport commissioner. **Dinghai / Guichou:** a vice minister in a court. **Renyin / Renyin:** Liu Tang, in the Imperial Bodyguard. **Dingwei / Dingwei:** inherited privilege, but poverty. **Yisi / Guiwei:** Vice Minister Chen Yumo. **Yiyou / Bingzi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^41]

〈A Skilled Artisan Carving Jade:  
At first, the right encounter has not come.  
One morning a clever craftsman appears;  
The vessel is completed, and Noble Person raises it.〉

> A Geng day at Yin hour deserves great praise;  
> Without punishment, control, or breaking, [splendor] shines.  
> Luck comes, and an eminent person offers help;  
> The time arrives, adding flowers to brocade.

> A Geng day at Wuyin hour is refined;  
> Indirect Seal and Inverted Food are hard to contain.  
> Do not grieve at being unrecognized in youth:  
> This fate may be poor or rich.  
> Time arrives and develops Wealth and blessing;  
> Luck comes, a boat sailing with the current.  
> Metal and Water in the month accord further;  
> Without breaking, achievement is complete.

## 16. Geng Days, Jimao Hour
### 六庚日卯時斷
<!-- source: L706-L745 -->

> A Geng day born at Jimao hour:  
> The original fate receives Embryo, and success comes through the wife.  
> Support in the pillars meeting flourishing Geng  
> Brings abundant Wealth and Lu, blessing and longevity together.

On a Geng day at Jimao hour, the original fate is born from Embryo. Geng Metal receives Embryo at Mao and sees Ji as generating qi and Seal-Ribbon. Geng takes Yi as Wealth; Mao contains flourishing Yi, so blessing develops through the wife. If the pillars flourish, Ding gives generation, and the birth month carries Lu, distinction follows. Support gives wealth. Communication with generating qi and flourishing Wealth, or luck that brings flourishing Wealth, gives distinction.[^42]

**Gengzi day.** Zi and Mao punish one another, raising concern for wife and children. Zi in year and month gives distinction; Chou and Wei give third rank. 〈One account says:〉 loss of the ancestral estate and land, followed by great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinmao / Gengyin:** a prefect. **Jiawu / Yisi:** Minister Wan [Lun]; a wealthy person shared the chart.〉[^43]

**Gengyin day.** Hai and Mao months give the Wealth-Star configuration: early honor and early retirement. Wu and Xu form the triple Officer configuration; Metal and Water exposed in the stems give good fortune in northwest luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Jiaxu:** a chief councillor. **Dingmao / Guimao:** an assistant transport commissioner. **Yihai / Bingxu:** Grand Secretary Wang Xijue’s father. **Guisi / Yichou:** a woman whose son was a vice censor-in-chief.〉

**Gengchen day.** Solitude. Year and month communicating with Wood qi, together with support, give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Guimao:** a chancellor. **Xinyou / Bingshen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Bingchen / Gengzi:** provincial-examination first.〉

**Gengwu day.** Punishment and breaking. Shen–Zi–Chen and Si–You–Chou give distinction through southeast luck; Yin–Wu–Xu and Hai–Mao–Wei require northwest luck for a favorable result. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Yiyou:** a censor. **Renyin / Jiyou:** an assistant director in an educational institution. **Wuchen / Jimao:** wealth. **Guiyou / Yimao:** first in a military examination.〉

**Gengshen day.** Mao month gives the Wealth-Star configuration: if not distinguished, then rich. Chou and Si in year and month give pure Hanlin distinction, with office reaching vice-ministerial rank. 〈One account says:〉 poverty in youth, a small calamity in middle life, injury to wife, and few children. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Dingmao:** a chancellor. **Jimao / Jiayin:** a chief councillor. **Jiawu / Wuchen:** a commander-in-chief.〉

**Gengxu day.** Orphaned young, with a lowly mother, but distinguished in middle life. Mao, You, Chen, and Chou months give the rank of duke or high minister. 〈Recorded cases—**Wushen / Guihai:** an official in a southern ministry. **Renchen / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yichou / Guiwei:** a transport commissioner. **Dingyou / Yisi:** Li Dai, Minister of Personnel, childless.〉

〈Embryo Star and the Original Fate:  
Flourishing Wealth welcomes a strong body.  
When the time arrives, advancement is fitting;  
In middle and later years the name spreads fragrance.〉

> The Heavenly Origins Geng and Ji link at Mao hour;  
> Lu meets the written word, and wealth and distinction are complete.  
> The Four Pillars without punishment, clash, control, or breaking:  
> Noble Person receives and leads one into azure Heaven.

> A Geng day meeting Jimao hour:  
> Wealth-and-Officer luck passes freely.  
> Without punishment or clash, an honorific patent is certain;  
> With breaking, middle life is troubled.  
> The ranks of geese and Six Relations offer little support;  
> One establishes an inexhaustible livelihood oneself.  
> Time arrives, bringing advancement and manifest achievement;  
> An eminent person naturally gives employment.

## 17. Geng Days, Gengchen Hour
### 六庚日辰時斷
<!-- source: L746-L809 -->

> A Geng day born at Gengchen hour:  
> Metal and Fire, born in autumn, present a pure image.  
> Kuigang embraces both high and low station;  
> Wealth and Officer’s preferences differ across the six positions.[^44]

On a Geng day at Gengchen hour, Metal and Water are clear and white. Of the six Geng days, Gengxu and Gengchen are Kuigang: Wealth, Officer, punishment, and clash are feared. Their absence gives a coarse, bold, fiercely courageous yet distinguished person; their presence brings a hundred troubles. Gengzi, Gengyin, Gengwu, and Gengshen welcome Wealth and Officer. Autumn birth gives graceful refinement and, if not distinction, wealth. If the body does not transform, but Jia, Yi, and Ding emerge, and birth falls in a Fire-and-Wood region, judge Wealth and Officer likewise. Communication with the month’s qi and favorable luck are welcome.

**Gengzi day.** Shen-month birth gives the Well-Railing Fork. Without Bing, Ding, Si, Wu, Yin, or Xu in either pillars or luck, there is distinction.
