Pure Mao in year and month gives the rank of duke or marquis. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Jichou:** a metropolitan court minister. **Gengchen / Yiyou:** provincial-examination first. **Jiashen / Renshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wushen / Yichou:** a commander-in-chief. **Jisi / Bingyin:** Guo Xiyan, Counselor on Virtue; executed and his head displayed for remonstrance in Gengshen year. How grievous!〉

〈Another case—**Gengzi / Gengchen:** grandfather and father were both distinguished; in Shen luck and a Shen year, sentenced to military service. Truly, stubborn Metal needs Fire. Another—**Gengchen / Gengchen:** a robber, executed by dismemberment. Three Kuigang pillars flourished exclusively, with no Fire to restrain them: an expanse of stubborn Metal. Its becoming a robber and meeting disaster was fitting. Further cases—**Dingchou / Yisi:** a mobile-force commander. **Guisi / Jiazi:** an assistant commissioner.〉[^45]

**Gengyin day.** Dragon and Tiger Arching the Gate. Years and months communicating with Fire and Earth give distinction. Pure Gengchen gives a prince or marquis; otherwise, extreme misfortune. If year and month meet Wood and Fire and luck travels there again, the highest rank follows. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengwu / Xinsi:** Hanlin Compiler Wang Daoli. **Jisi / Wuchen:** a chief censor. **Yichou / Bingxu:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Renxu / Renzi:** immense wealth and a violent death. **Jiwei / Renshen:** Liu Tingzhu, Prefect of Hejian, murdered by a servant. **Wuzi / Jiayin:** Hanlin bachelor Sun Ting. **Yihai / Bingxu:** Hanlin bachelor Xiang Dingxuan. **Xinchou / Dingyou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner.〉

**Gengchen day.** Kuigang. Without Wealth, Officer, or Xu in year and month, distinction; with clash and breaking, wealth. Shen and Zi in year and month make the Well-Railing Fork, auspicious in southwest luck. Pure Gengchen and Gengshen, or Yiyou transforming into Metal, with no Fire qi at all, make a violent, base person. 〈One account says:〉 punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Gengchen:** Jiaxu luck clashed with Blade; in Wuchen year and Jiwei minor luck, executed by slow dismemberment at fifty-four. **Renyin / Dingwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** Chief Censor Wang Ding. **Jiashen / Yihai:** a capital prefect. **Wuzi / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate. **Jiaxu / Yihai:** a prefect. **Dinghai / Wushen:** Minister Zhang Jiayin; a censor also shared the chart. **Jiashen / Gengwu:** a commander-in-chief and a regional military commissioner. **Jiayin / Wuchen:** military distinction; killed in battle. **Bingshen / Jiawu:** Wang Linzhou, whose junior title is incomplete. **Yimao / Gengchen:** a regional commander.〉[^46]

**Gengwu day.** Self-punishment; Metal Spirit enters Fire’s country. Shen month with Fire-and-Wood luck can give sixth rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingsi / Dingwei:** Wang Jiabin, in Personnel Appointments. **Dingchou / Jiyou:** a censor. **Xinchou / Yiwei:** a prefect. **Bingzi / Jihai:** a provincial graduate. **Jimao / Jisi:** a chief councillor.〉

**Gengshen day.** Zi and Shen in year and month give the Well-Railing Fork. Pillars and luck without Bing, Ding, Si, or Wu give distinction. Pure Gengshen and Gengchen, with western, northern, or eastern luck, give the highest military rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Wushen / Gengshen:** a commandery prince. **Yisi / Jiashen:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Guihai / Yichou:** a capital prefect. **Jimao / Gengyin:** a chief supervising secretary. **Guihai / Gengshen:** an assistant bureau director. **Guiwei / Gengshen:** Yin Feng, first in the military palace examination. **Jihai / Xinwei:** a Secretariat official. **Jiayin / Bingyin:** a supervising secretary. **Renchen / Renyin:** a county magistrate. **Wuyin / Yimao:** Daoist Qingxia, preceded by an uncertain character in the caption; one hundred and twenty years old. **Yiwei / Gengchen:** a son, Huang Shijun, palace-examination first and Grand Secretary. **Wuyin / Jiazi:** Grand Secretary Qian Longxi, banished to frontier service. **Wuwu / Gengshen:** Minister Zhou Yingqiu. **Xinyou / Guisi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^47]

**Gengxu day.** Kuigang. A region of substantial Earth gives high office and weighty emolument. Zi month with southwest luck gives distinction; Yin and Chen in year and month are ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingchen / Xinchou:** a chancellor. **Yichou / Jichou:** Chief Censor Hu Lian. **Guichou / Jiwei:** a bureau director. **Jiashen / Dingmao:** a ministry secretary. **Jiyou / Dingchou:** served in a county and was brought to court for questioning. **Guiyou / Yimao:** an earl.〉

〈Thunder Resounding Afar:  
The person possesses the workings of transformation.  
Where Blessing Star descends,  
The body reaches the Phoenix Pool.〉

> Examine carefully the six Geng days at Chen hour:  
> A key opens Seal’s Storehouse within Chen.  
> If the Four Pillars contain clash or punishment,  
> Blessing and Lu arrive serenely from Heaven.

> A Geng day at Gengchen hour:  
> Triple Combination in the branches is foremost.  
> Mao and Xu open the Storehouse gate;  
> Without damage, purple robes and golden belt.  
> Si, Wu, Bing, and Ding diminish blessing;  
> In undertakings, wealth goes and comes.  
> Repeated wives and late children are allotted by the fate:  
> Wealth, distinction, and untroubled freedom.

## 18. Geng Days, Xinsi Hour
### 六庚日巳時斷
<!-- source: L810-L857 -->

> A Geng day born at Xinsi hour:  
> Indirect Officer combines with Blade, and the body is born.  
> The person is resolute, but wife and wealth suffer loss;  
> Luck reaching Metal’s country brings noble Lu and success.

On a Geng day at Xinsi hour, there are Yang Blade and Indirect Officer. Geng takes Xin as Blade and Bing as Killings. Visible Xin and hidden Bing combine with Killings to form authority. A month communicating with bodily strength gives distinction; without that communication, there is no distinction, but life is long.[^48]

**Gengzi day.** Spring gives wealth; summer and autumn distinction; winter poverty. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei in year and month give Seal-Ribbon, with distinction through southwest luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Dingchou:** a prefect. **Dingchou / Guimao:** a ministry secretary. **Jiwei / Jisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jichou / Wuchen:** a deputy commissioner. **Jiaxu / Renshen:** a transport commissioner. **Wuxu / Renxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiayin / Wuchen:** Wang Tingzhuan, palace-examination third. **Bingyin / Guisi:** Censor Liu Guangfu, banished for remonstrating about the cudgel attack; when Guangzong ascended the throne, recalled and promoted to assistant minister of the Court of Imperial Banquets.〉

**Gengyin day.** Si, You, and Chou months strengthen the body and give distinction. A month strengthening Killings and weakening the body gives poverty. Pure Shen in year and month gives great, enduring distinction. Hai month with southeast luck gives authority and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengzi / Jiashen:** a vice minister. **Jiawu / Guiyou:** Chief Censor Chen Xiang. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renchen / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate. **Gengchen / Jichou:** a provincial graduate. **Guichou / Guihai:** a prefect. **Renzi / Renyin:** a censor.〉

**Gengchen day.** Hardship to the end; no Wealth in year and month gives high standing. Communication with Wood qi and southwest luck, or Wei and Shen with northeast luck, give distinction. 〈One account says:〉 having nothing at first, then having something. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Renyin:** Minister Wang Tang. **Bingwu / Dingchou:** a vice minister in a court. **Gengchen / Yihai:** a ministry secretary. **Xinyou / Bingshen:** a subprefect. **Jiashen / Bingzi:** Minister Zhang Xueyan, according to the original text.〉[^49]

**Gengwu day.** Si, Wu, and Mao in year and month give a metropolitan degree and censorial office. Si, Shen, You, and Xu with [Earth]-and-Wood luck give a marquis or earl. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Jisi:** a ministry secretary. **Jiashen / Jiaxu:** a provincial graduate. **Xinwei / Wuxu:** a ministry secretary. **Xinyou / Dingyou:** Shao Jingyao, palace-examination second. **Wuchen / Renxu:** lived beyond eighty.〉[^50]

**Gengshen day.** Punishment. Si, You, and Chou months give exceptional advancement. Spring and summer strengthen Wood and Fire, giving Wealth and Officer their ground: auspicious. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Seal-Ribbon, with distinction in southern luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Jihai:** Lord Tao Zhu. **Xinwei / Wuxu:** a vice minister in a court. **Guiyou / Yichou:** a county magistrate. **Dingwei / Renyin:** a provincial administration commissioner.〉

**Gengxu day.** Wealth encountered in year and month is auspicious. Wu and Wei with southeast luck give authority in civil office, but fear an unhappy end. Yin and Shen give Hanlin or chancellorial office; Fire luck also makes a good end difficult. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Bingyin:** Cao Nai, palace-examination first and Grand Secretary, killed in the Tumu disaster. **Renxu / Dingwei:** Minister Weng Pu. **Renshen / Xinhai:** an academician. **Xinsi / Wuxu:** palace-examination first. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** a provincial administrator. **Dingchou / Bingwu:** Yang Dan, metropolitan graduate in Guichou, drowned in Xinyou. **Guisi / Yichou:** a ministry secretary. **Guimao / Yiwei:** a woman whose husband Liu Bo and sons Yuanzhen and Yuanlin were all metropolitan graduates.〉

〈The Day Stem Born and Flourishing:  
Within joy, sorrow is concealed.  
Luck travels to flourishing Wealth:  
Wealth and distinction need not be sought.〉

> A Geng day meeting Si at the hour of birth:  
> Blessing and Lu seem roughly ordinary.  
> If punishment, clash, breaking, or harm appear in the pillars,  
> Independence and self-establishment avert calamity’s alarms.

> A Geng day at Xinsi hour:  
> Ruined Wealth and hidden ghost seek secretly.  
> Wealth goes and comes; petty people encroach;  
> The ancestral foundation offers little shelter.  
> External unions and repeated movement develop blessing;  
> Of brothers and the ranks of geese, no word.  
> Late wife and late children open the heart to joy:  
> A fate of hardship first and honor afterward.

## 19. Geng Days, Renwu Hour
### 六庚日午時斷
<!-- source: L858-L911 -->

> A Geng day born at Renwu hour:  
> Officer and Seal’s blessed palace gathers Food God.  
> Metal and Earth assisting the body must bring distinction;  
> A month of flourishing Fire obstructs the fate’s passage.

On a Geng day at Renwu hour, Officer and Seal are strong. Geng takes Ding as Officer, Ji as Seal, and Ren as Food. At Wu, Ren Food lacks qi, while Ding and Ji have flourishing Lu. A month whose Metal-and-Earth qi assists the body gives distinction. Communication with Fire qi makes noble Officer excessively heavy, turning it into ghost; yielding Geng Metal cannot develop blessing. Luck assisting the body also gives distinction.[^51]

**Gengzi day.** Hour and day clash, raising concern for wife and children. Communication with Metal qi gives distinction; with Fire qi, great distinction, but much worry and toil. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiyou / Jiwei:** Vice Minister Hu Shao. **Gengzi / Bingxu:** Chief Censor Zhou Xu. **Jiayin / Wuchen:** a deputy commissioner. **Jiachen / Gengwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiwei / Bingyin:** provincial-examination first. **Wuzi / Jiazi:** Yu Menglin, palace-examination second and chancellor of the Directorate of Education, childless.〉

**Gengyin day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, Wei, Si, Shen, and You months strengthen the body and all give distinction. Yin and Wu strengthen Fire; luck encountering it again shortens life, or otherwise gives disability. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Dingmao:** Zhao Shichun, metropolitan-examination first and chief censor. **Yimao / Jichou:** a ministry secretary. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuwu / Wuwu:** palace-examination first. **Jisi / Jisi:** a provincial graduate. **Bingxu / Bingshen:** Luo Fengxiang, provincial graduate and chief censor; the hour falls in Void, giving few sons. **Dingyou / Jiachen:** Grand Secretary Shen Yiguan.〉

**Gengchen day.** Food God flourishes; the person enjoys food and drink and has self-command. Development is followed immediately by death. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengzi / Wuyin:** Liu, Earl of Chengyi; the linked succession of earthly branches is especially excellent. **Jiashen / Bingzi:** Minister Zhang Xueyan, according to the *Star Cases*.〉

**Gengwu day.** Yin–Wu–Xu months make Metal yielding and Fire strong: timidity and disability. Hai, Zi, and Wu months give distinction in Wood-and-Fire luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuzi / Jiazi:** an official in Personnel. **Guiyou / Dingsi:** an assistant minister in a court. **Jisi / Gengwu:** a prefect. **Bingchen / Yiwei:** a censor. **Guisi / Bingchen:** a wealthy commander. **Renyin / Guichou:** a judicial official. **Jichou / Dingchou:** a prefect. **Jimao / Dingmao:** a provincial graduate. **Gengyin / Yiyou:** a metropolitan graduate and associate prefect. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a censor.〉

**Gengshen day.** Lu and Horse Sharing a Country is most auspicious. Wu and Wei in year and month give office in the censorate or Grand Secretariat. Mao, Chen, and Si give distinction. Xu month with Fire-and-Earth luck gives a pure but unimportant post. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Gengzi:** an official in Works. **Jiazi / Xinwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingxu / Dingyou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yihai / Jimao:** a prefect. **Renshen / Jiachen:** a prefect. **Jiazi / Xinwei:** Grand Secretary Lü Ben, from Zhejiang; a man of Jiangning shared the chart but remained ordinary. **Gengxu / Guiwei:** Chief Censor Xu Zhaokui. **Xinwei / Dingyou:** Hanlin scholar Kong Zhenshi. **Jiyou / Bingzi:** poverty. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** misfortune.〉[^52]

**Gengxu day.** Mao month gives auspicious Direct Wealth; Wei month gives distinguished Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer. Chen and Chou months are judged alike. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Xinmao:** Academician Zhu. **Xinmao / Gengzi:** Minister Yang Junmin. **Yiwei / Guiwei:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiwei / Dingmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Yimao:** a supervising secretary. **Gengchen / Jiashen:** Grand Secretary Yang Dan. **Renshen / Dingwei:** Hong Chengxuan, provincial-examination first in Dingyou and metropolitan graduate in Guichou; died before the palace examination. **Xinchou / Jiawu:** an official whose ministry caption is damaged.〉[^53]

〈A Fine Horse Leaving the Herd:  
A man with the gifts of minister or general.  
The Four Pillars without clash or breaking:  
Wealth and distinction are allotted in the fate.〉

> Ren and Geng assemble at Wu hour;  
> Without breaking or clash, blessing comes of itself.  
> Henceforth name and profit may both be hoped for;  
> Noble Person recommends him up the heavenly steps.

> A Geng day at Renwu hour:  
> Officer and Seal both lie within the branch.  
> Noble Person, Lu, and Horse share one country;  
> Calamity vanishes unseen, and blessing grows.  
> Control, breaking, punishment, and clash are inauspicious;  
> Void in the pillars is also feared.  
> General Star and Tianyi bring honor and flourishing;  
> In middle and later life, the household prospers.

## 20. Geng Days, Guiwei Hour
### 六庚日未時斷
<!-- source: L912-L957 -->

> A Geng day born at Guiwei hour:  
> Officer Star is [restrained], and Authority Star retreats.  
> Ji in the pillars, without encountering Ding,  
> Can nevertheless bring advancement and official rank.[^54]

On a Geng day at Guiwei hour, Seal and Wealth are stored. Geng takes Yi as Wealth, entering the Storehouse at Wei, Ding as Officer, and Ji as Seal. Ji and Ding within Wei are controlled by visible Gui. Ji in the pillars restraining Gui, with no Ding exposed, gives official advancement. Without Ji, and with Ding exposed, troubles arise in a hundred forms. Luck communicating with Fire and Earth gives distinction.

**Gengzi day.** No Ding exposed in year and month, and Ji Earth restraining Gui Water, give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Gengwu:** the worthy Prince of Zheng, falsely accused and confined behind high walls, then restored to his proper position. **Dingyou / Jiyou:** the Prince of Yi, with thirty-six sons. **Renzi / Wushen:** wealth.〉

**Gengyin day.** Distinction. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give high standing; summer gives wealth. Years and luck follow the same rule. Si, You, and Shen months strengthen the body and give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuzi / Gengshen:** Minister Wang Jun. **Xinsi / Xinmao:** Chief Censor Zeng Yugong. **Xinyou / Guisi:** Chief Censor Wang Shiqiao. **Wuzi / Jiazi:** a prefect. **Yihai / Wuzi:** a censor. **Guimao / Yichou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner.〉[^55]

**Gengchen day.** A Kuigang day. Earth within Chen restrains Gui; no Ding in year and month gives distinction. Mao month with Metal-and-Water luck gives seventh rank. 〈Recorded case—**Renwu / Renzi:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Gengwu day.** Distinction. You, Shen, Si, and Hai in year and month give command of military forces and second rank: first insubstantial, then substantial. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Jiayin:** Vice Minister Jiang Zhao. **Guiyou / Jiayin:** Hanlin Compiler Pan Zhongcan, sentenced to death for an offense. **Dingyou / Gengxu:** Chief Censor Wang Xuanxi. **Wushen / Jiazi:** Minister Zhang Minggang. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** a ministry bureau director.〉

**Gengshen day.** You month gives military distinction, though authority is stripped away in early life. Yin–Wu–Xu and Shen–Zi–Chen in year and month bring the two configurations into relation and give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Bingzi:** Minister Hu Xun. **Gengchen / Gengchen:** Han Jing, first in the metropolitan and palace examinations. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** an assistant regional commander.〉

**Gengxu day.** Xu month with eastern luck gives distinction; Chen month and Mao year give great distinction. Autumn birth is most auspicious; summer requires northwest luck for a favorable result. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinmao / Renchen:** Minister Li Chengxun. **Xinsi / Renchen:** a censor. **Xinchou / Bingshen:** Hanlin scholar Liu Yixiu, called Jiuyi, from Sichuan, residing in Fuzhou; a gentleman.〉

〈Viewing One’s Face in a Mirror:  
Joy still carries sorrow.  
Clouds part, and the bright moon emerges;  
Yet affairs may become entangled.〉

> A Geng day at Wei hour stores Wealth within;  
> A key opens it, and one alone becomes foremost.  
> Luck reaches flourishing Wealth generating Officer:  
> Wealth, distinction, and splendor need no guessing.

> A Geng day meeting Guiwei hour  
> Welcomes Chou and Xu to prosper Wealth.  
> Hurting Officer turns from Lu; the Storehouse is hard to open;  
> The fullness of the ancestral estate is obstructed.  
> Parents suffer early loss, solitude, Void, and control;  
> A late wife and late children bring harmony.  
> Luck through auspicious ground averts calamity;  
> Flourishing Officer and Wealth’s country give free passage.

## 21. Geng Days, Jiashen Hour
### 六庚日申時斷
<!-- source: L958-L1011 -->

> A Geng day born at Jiashen hour:  
> Returning Lu carrying Wealth is a most pure configuration.  
> Without Si, Bing, or Yin in the pillars,  
> Achievement, wealth, and distinction arrive naturally.

On a Geng day at Jiashen hour, the Day’s Lu Occupies the Hour. Geng Metal meets Lu at Shen, and Jia is Indirect Wealth. Without communication with Bing Fire or the clash and punishment of Si and Yin in the pillars, distinction is indicated, though some are ordinary. Years and luck follow the same rule.

**Gengzi day.** The hour encounters the day’s Lu and sees Wealth Star. Without Si, Yin, or Bing in the pillars, wealth and distinction are complete. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei Earth generating Metal are auspicious; Yin–Wu–Xu is ordinary. Shen and You give distinction in Fire-and-Wood luck; Yin and Hai give third or fourth rank. Mao punishing Zi is often ominous. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Wuzi:** Confucius, as the caption relates through the *Records of the Historian*, *Family Sayings*, and *Lushi*; the latter also records Jiyou year and Guiyou month. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** a censor. **Jiwei / Jisi:** Li Mo, Minister of Personnel. **Dingwei / Xinhai:** Minister Peng An. **Gengwu / Jichou:** Provincial Administration Commissioner Fan Weiyi. **Yichou / Gengchen:** Prefect Hong Yuan. **Wuchen / Jiazi:** Deputy Commissioner Liu Cunde. **Gengwu / Xinsi:** Censor Yang Yicheng. **Yihai / Wuyin:** Prefect Zhang Xing. **Renzi / Xinhai:** the scholar Xu, named Qigao, of Cangzhou. **Jisi / Dingchou:** twins; the younger was a provincial graduate.〉

〈All the foregoing gentlemen possessed moral principle and righteousness, and so all their names are listed. Could this too have resulted from sharing Confucius’s birth?〉[^56]

**Gengyin day.** Yin and Hai months give third rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Jihai:** a transmission commissioner. **Bingxu / Gengzi:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** wealth.〉

**Gengchen day.** The day is Kuigang and also has Returning Lu; neither welcomes Wealth or Officer. Without Yin, Wu, Xu, Bing, Ding, or Si in the pillars, there is distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengyin / Yiyou:** no caption is supplied. **Dinghai / Renzi:** a prefect. **Wuzi / Xinyou:** an assistant prefect. **Yisi / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guisi / Xinyou:** Censor Liu Xiaoqing, whose official career rose and fell.〉

**Gengwu day.** Distinction. A month strengthening the body, without Bing, Si, Yin, Wu, or Ding causing injury and breaking, gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Bingwu:** Academician Lai. **Jisi / [Wuchen]:** a senior provincial administrator. **Gengwu / Dinghai:** Huang Jia, palace-examination first. **Jiayin / Xinwei:** a provincial graduate. **Gengwu / Jimao:** Meng Shifang, deputy director of the Heir Apparent’s Household.〉[^57]

**Gengshen day.** Distinction. Without Mao, Wu, Wei, Xu, Bing, or Ding, there is distinction. Zi and Chou months with Metal-and-Water luck give literary distinction. I have seen Gengshen–Jiashen with Exclusive Lu and Returning Lu: hard Metal cannot be forged without Fire, and therefore those born in Si, Wu, or Xu months are often distinguished. There are seven methods of examining Returning Lu; do not regard these months as forbidden. A Wealth month is most auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Jisi:** Vice Minister Wu Jiahui. **Renwu / Bingwu:** Chief Censor Wu Jin. **Gengchen / Yiyou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Wuzi / Renxu:** vice chancellor of the Directorate of Education. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** a supervising secretary. **Jimao / Jiaxu:** a prefect. **Dingchou / Guimao:** a provincial graduate. **Guiwei / Jiashen:** first in a military examination. **Gengyin / Jiashen:** an official with a damaged title; violent death. **Dingyou / Guichou:** Shi Xing, Minister of War, from Dongming, who died in prison; his son was banished to frontier service. An impoverished scholar from Zhenjiang shared the chart.〉[^58]

**Gengxu day.** Yin, Si, Wu, and Xu months give a worthy wife, filial children, and distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Metal and Water carrying Seal produce the blessing of pure distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Wuyin:** Minister Jin. **Yichou / Dinghai:** Senior Provincial Administrator Zhang. **Wuwu / Jiayin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiwei / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate. **Yimao / Bingxu:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈A Geng Day at Jiashen Hour:  
Punishment and clash leave affairs without excellence.  
Luck through ground free of breaking  
Gives level steps up Heaven’s ladder.〉

> The day’s Lu in the hour welcomes Shen;  
> The pillars dislike Si, Bing, and Yin.  
> When the time comes, an eminent person recommends him:  
> Green willows, crimson peaches—spring for ten thousand li.

> A Geng day with Shen hour as principal  
> Is Wealth and Lu supporting one another.  
> Growing up in leisure, fond of poetry and books,  
> His nature holds neither resentment nor anger.  
> Unfavorable luck gives farming, trade, craft, or commerce;  
> When the time arrives, appointments bring promotion.  
> Eminent people welcome him, humble people support him;  
> Without breaking, there is a road into azure clouds.

## 22. Geng Days, Yiyou Hour
### 六庚日酉時斷
<!-- source: L1012-L1057 -->

> A Geng day born at Yiyou hour:  
> Meeting within Metal, they transform into true Metal.  
> Without Fire in the pillars, excessive hardness often breaks;  
> With Fire to bring completion, noble qi flourishes.

On a Geng day at Yiyou hour, qi transforms into true Metal. Geng takes Yi as Wealth and Xin as Blade; at You, Wealth is extinguished and Blade flourishes. If true Metal forms but the pillars contain no Fire, Metal becomes too hard and fractures. Fire luck brings the shaping forces of restraint and enhancement into the middle way. Gengchen day is best. Horse carried in year and month gives third or fourth rank, but few or unworthy children.

**Gengzi day.** Wealth. A month communicating with Fire qi gives distinction; otherwise, an ordinary life. Corresponding luck also fulfills one’s wishes. Mao and Hai in year and month give military command. 〈One account says:〉 ancestral ruin and ominous punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Guichou:** a metropolitan court minister. **Jichou / Gengwu:** a National University student.〉

**Gengyin day.** Punishment. Yin, Wu, and Xu are auspicious; Shen and Zi give violence and misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Guisi / Yimao:** a transmission commissioner. **Jiayin / Bingyin:** Chief Censor Duan Fengshe. **Renchen / Jiyou:** a commander-in-chief. **Dingchou / Guichou:** a childless prefect. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** Prefect Sun Meng. **Dingwei / Guimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jisi / Jisi:** a provincial graduate. **Jiashen / Bingzi:** Lin Shizhang, palace-examination third and minister.〉

**Gengchen day.** Punishment. Yin, Mao, Wu, and Wei in year and month give second-rank authority and distinction; Xu and Hai give great distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 transformation into Metal gives substantial blessing. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Xinhai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** a provincial graduate. **Yiyou / Wuzi:** Minister Zhang Guoyan.〉

**Gengwu day.** Distinction amid punishment. The two configurations Yin–Wu–Xu and Hai–Mao–Wei give distinction; Si is also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Dingwei:** a prefect. **Dinghai / Guimao:** an education commissioner. **Dinghai / Guimao:** Minister Cai Guozhen.〉

**Gengshen day.** True Punishment. Yin–Wu–Xu gives great distinction; Si–You–Chou, moderate distinction; Shen–Zi–Chen, wealth; Hai–Mao–Wei, decline and desolation. 〈One account says:〉 Wealth is auspicious. 〈Recorded case—**Dingmao / Dingwei:** Zhao, vice chancellor of the Directorate of Education.〉

**Gengxu day.** Development after punishment. Yin–Wu–Xu makes Officer Star bright and gives distinction; Hai–Mao–Wei gives wealth or fifth- or sixth-rank distinction. Si–You–Chou strengthens Blade and confers authority, indicating success upon the frontier. It particularly suits military officers; civil officers likewise command troops. Ultimately, disaster from Yang Blade follows. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Xinsi:** Minister Wu Dui; a licentiate shared the chart, both from Zhejiang. **Wushen / Yichou:** Minister Li Ruhua. **Yichou / Gengchen:** Grand Secretary Shen Que. **Gengwu / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^59]

〈Entering the Sea in Search of Pearls:  
By nature rich in literary gifts.  
The gentleman rises in office and Lu;  
Scholar and commoner have wealth and [grain].〉

> The Heavenly Origins join and transform at You hour;  
> Earth encountered in the birth month brings success.  
> Honored through Wealth and Officer, all attain distinction;  
> Achievement, profit, and Lu are bathed in favor.

> A Geng day meeting Yiyou hour:  
> Within it, combination transforms into true Metal.  
> Wealth-and-Officer luck gives birth to blessing’s source;  
> Literary refinement and intelligence are outstanding.  
> Without Hai and Zi, the wife is injured;  
> High or low, they lose their composure.  
> In every affair, later good follows much early misfortune:  
> A fate of entry into rank and manifest distinction.[^60]

## 23. Geng Days, Bingxu Hour
### 六庚日戌時斷
<!-- source: L1058-L1115 -->

> A Geng day born at Bingxu hour:  
> Metal and Fire contend, and affairs are inauspicious.  
> A strong body, a month communicating with Seal, are good;  
> Without communication or rescue, calamity is hard to bear.

On a Geng day at Bingxu hour, Metal and Fire contend. Geng takes Bing as ghost; Bing Fire and Wu Earth form a configuration, while Metal lacks qi. A month communicating with a strong body and [flourishing] Seal, with rescuing assistance, gives distinction. Otherwise there is an ordinary life, or early death and low station. Corresponding luck is also auspicious. Geng belongs to the large bowel; excessive Bing and Ding indicate hemorrhoidal fistulas, visceral toxins, pus, and blood.[^61]

**Gengzi day.** In spring, Indirect Officer employing Wealth is auspicious; in summer, strong Killings and a weak body give violence and misfortune. In autumn, body and Killings are both strong. Si, You, and Chou months with western luck give golden belt and purple robes. Yin, Hai, and Zi months bring Metal to Extinction, Illness, and Death, often giving poverty and low station. 〈One account says:〉 deafness, muteness, or injury by dogs and wolves. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengyin / Bingxu:** Vice Minister Wu Shen'an. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** a vice minister. **Guisi / Bingchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renwu / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Jiachen / Yihai:** Wei Yunzhong, provincial-examination first and a leading metropolitan candidate. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** vice minister of the Court of Imperial Sacrifices.〉

**Gengyin day.** Shen, Zi, and Chen months restrain Indirect Officer and are auspicious. Autumn birth strengthens both body and Killings, giving Lu and authority. Pure Wu gives unsupported solitary poverty, or otherwise disability. Chou and Wu with southwest luck give distinction at or above the rank of duke or high minister. Pure Yin also gives distinction.

〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Wuyin:** Zou Yinglong, Minister of Personnel. **Gengzi / Jiashen:** Wen Yuan, in Personnel, with five sons. **Dingwei / Guichou:** Vice Minister Huang Zongming. **Bingwu / Xinchou:** Academician Jiang Rubi. **Yiwei / Wuzi:** Zheng Jizhi, Minister of Personnel. **Xinmao / Xinmao:** Fang Kongzhao, Governor of Chu, banished to frontier service. **Renyin / Jiyou:** an official by inherited privilege. **Dingchou / Bingwu:** a supervising secretary. **Guisi / Yichou:** a vice minister in a court. **Wuyin / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate. **Wuyin / Yichou:** Commander-in-Chief Li Yong, called a faithless petty man. **Gengxu / Jiashen:** a prefect, immensely rich, with many sons. **Bingxu / Gengzi:** a provincial graduate. **Wuzi / Wuwu:** a county magistrate. **Gengshen / Guiwei:** an impoverished scholar, childless. **Yichou / Bingxu:** blind in one eye, extremely poor; died in Jiayin year, childless. **Gengchen / Yiyou:** a bare pair without a clear caption. **Guihai / Renxu:** a violent death. **Renwu / Guichou:** Junior Provincial Administrator Wan Yuwu; Commander-in-Chief Fu Jin shared the chart. Fu was from Yong and Wan from Ji. Geng born in Yong obtains its ground, whereas birth in Ji is too cold. Thus Fu served in the northwest as a military officer, while Wan served in the southeast directing military surveillance. Wan had three sons, Fu one.〉[^62]

〈Different years and months give different courses of luck; the differences between them are as vast as this.〉

**Gengchen day.** Hour and day clash, raising concern for wife and children. A month communicating with Metal-and-Water qi gives distinction. Heavy Wood-and-Fire qi gives hardship, poverty, early death, or disability. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Wuxu:** Vice Minister He Qian, devoted to the Learning of the Way. **Renwu / Guichou:** a censor. **Xinyou / Bingshen:** a censor. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** Education Commissioner Su Zixi. **Jiaxu / Guiyou:** a censor. **Renchen / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Gengwu day.** Shen, You, Hai, and Chou give wealth, distinction, and third rank. Si, Wu, Wei, and Shen, with Ren exposed, give a long-lived minister or chancellor through luck in Gen, Kan, and Qian. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Dinghai:** Vice Minister Wu Daozhi. **Renyin / Guichou:** minister of the Court of Judicial Review. **Jihai / Guiyou:** great distinction. **Bingyin / Guisi:** lesser distinction. **Jiaxu / Bingyin:** enjoyment of blessing. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jihai / Dingchou:** poverty and early death.〉

**Gengshen day.** Xu and Hai in year and month give distinction in southeast luck. Shen month gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Xinchou:** Cai Maochun, metropolitan-examination first, who rose no higher than deputy commissioner. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** a surveillance commissioner. **Jiwei / Jiaxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Guisi / Jichou:** a judicial official. **Bingzi / Dingyou:** a judicial official. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** a bureau director. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** a tribute student admitted through the contribution route. **Wushen / Yimao:** a prefect. **Bingyin / Xinchou:** a violent death.〉

**Gengxu day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give Seal-Ribbon’s generation and support: auspicious. Shen–Zi–Chen restrains Indirect Officer and gives distinction. A month communicating with Metal qi, without Ren exposed, gives distinction in northern luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiachen / Gengwu:** Minister Zhang Yongchuan. **Jiashen / Dingmao:** Minister Lin Kuang. **Guimao / Jiazi:** Deputy Surveillance Commissioner Jin Benheng, devoted to the Learning of the Way; all three sons were metropolitan graduates. **Dingwei / Bingwu:** a capital prefect. **Dinghai / Guimao:** an administrative secretary. **Bingwu / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Bingchen:** a provincial graduate. **Gengxu / Guiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Renyin / Gengxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Renchen / Renzi:** Zhou Shixuan, Minister of War in the southern capital.〉[^63]

〈Indirect Officer upon the Hour:  
A strong body is most excellent.  
Autumn birth, flourishing in Wealth’s country:  
Blessing and Lu arrive naturally.〉

> Xu hour’s Officer Storehouse is foremost;  
> Chou and Xu punish one another, and the Storehouse opens.  
> Early and middle life show no advancement;  
> In the last years, the heart’s desire is fulfilled.

> A Geng day meeting Bingxu hour:  
> Indirect Officer lies buried within the Storehouse.  
> Within it, ghost and assailant are ill matched;  
> A weak body’s money drifts away in vain.  
> Strong luck and a strong body develop blessing;  
> Brothers and blood kin stand apart like Shen and Shang.  
> Repeated wives and late children avoid punishment’s injuries;  
> In old age, investiture as marquis or appointment as chancellor.

## 24. Geng Days, Dinghai Hour
### 六庚日亥時斷
<!-- source: L1116-L1167 -->

> A Geng day born at Dinghai hour:  
> Officer Star loses its ground, and the body declines.  
> Without the month’s qi, blessing is difficult to form;  
> But meeting Kuigang is instead most excellent.

On a Geng day at Dinghai hour, Geng takes Jia as Wealth, Ren as Food, and Ding as Officer. At Hai, Ding Fire lacks qi, Ren flourishes, Jia is born, and Geng Metal loses its ground: Wealth and Food are hard to sustain. Without a month strengthening the body, blessing cannot form. Communication with the month’s qi, with yin Earth assisting the body, develops Wealth; assistance to Officer Star gives some distinction. Gengxu and Gengchen are Kuigang days, unsuitable for flourishing Wealth and Officer; meeting Dinghai hour therefore gives distinction instead.

**Gengzi day.** Distinction. Shen, Zi, and Chen months give Hurting Officer and toil. Mao month with Metal-and-Fire luck gives fifth or sixth rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengyin / Wuzi:** a county magistrate. **Renyin / Jiachen:** Gu Kexue, formerly a provincial administrator, who advanced through arts of self-cultivation and rose to minister.〉[^64]

**Gengyin day.** Ordinary: poverty first, refinement in middle life, wealth and prosperity at the end. Chen and Xu in year and month give advancement. Shen, You, Hai, Mao, Si, and Wu in year and month, with Yi and Ji exposed in the stems, give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengzi / Jiashen:** Vice Minister Gao Gongshao. **Xinyou / Xinmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** wealth. **Xinsi / Guisi:** a provincial graduate. **Xinsi / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate. **Yisi / Jiashen:** the highest distinction. **Renwu / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinsi / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Gengchen day.** Pure Wei gives advancement. Si and Wu give censorial office, gold and purple, in northwest luck. Hai and Mao give gold and purple with authority. Shen month’s Established Lu is most auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Xinhai:** Minister Min Xu. **Guiyou / Bingchen:** a bureau director. **Jiachen / Renshen:** an academician. **Wuchen / Yimao:** Wei Zhongxian.〉

**Gengwu day.** Distinction. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give Earth that generates Metal: honor and advancement. Shen, Hai, and You months with Wood-and-Fire luck give the highest rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Jichou / Guiyou:** Grand Secretary Jiang Yao. **Jimao / Dingchou:** Chief Censor Chen Daoji. **Dingchou / Wushen:** a minister. **Wuwu / Guihai:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Yimao / Jimao:** a prefect. **Xinhai / Gengzi:** a prefect. **Dingchou / Jiyou:** entered through the classical-learning route and served in provincial administration. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** a censor. **Renxu / Gengxu:** a provincial graduate. **Guisi / Renxu:** Vice Censor-in-Chief Li Qiao. **Dingwei / Dingwei:** distinguished first, then died poor. **Gengzi / Dinghai:** distinction. **Xinwei / Xinmao:** distinction. **Wuchen / Wuwu:** distinction. **Gengshen / Renwu:** distinction.〉

**Gengshen day.** Autumn birth gives manifest distinction in southern luck; northern luck is ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Jichou:** Minister Li Zuan. **Dingchou / Guichou:** Minister Lu Wan, banished to frontier service for dealings with the Prince of Ning. **Yiyou / Yiyou:** Commander-in-Chief Tong Deng. **Jiashen / Bingyin:** a deputy general. **Jisi / Renyin:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yiwei / Bingxu:** a censor. **Renwu / Renzi:** distinction. **Guimao / Bingchen:** distinction. **Dingwei / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiaxu / Jisi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Gengxu day.** Chen, Si, and Wu months mix Officer and Killings, yet still give military distinction. Chou month with western luck gives a bureau directorship; Chen month with northwest luck, provincial authority. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Bingwu:** Vice Minister Liang Jian'an. **Guichou / Dingsi:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Guichou / Guihai:** a senior provincial administrator. **Dingchou / Wushen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jisi / Jiaxu:** a supervising secretary. **Xinwei / Renchen:** an assistant bureau director. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** an assistant prefect. **Jiashen / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate. **Jiazi / Bingzi:** an assistant prefect.〉

〈Name Fulfilled, Achievement Completed:  
Hemp clothing is exchanged for brocade.  
If clash, control, and breaking are absent,  
Blessing and Lu grow ever more abundant.〉

> Dinghai hour arrayed beside a Geng day:  
> Layer upon layer of joy lights the vermilion gate.  
> Crimson peaches bloom profusely in day and hour;  
> A single gust of spring wind shows its power.

> A Geng day meeting Dinghai hour:  
> The Three Wonders combine in concealment within the pillars.  
> Know flourishing children and three wives;  
> Parents and the ranks of geese may fare well.  
> Without Gui, Si, Wu, or Xin,  
> Wide literary learning brings abundant knowledge.  
> No clash or breaking: Noble Person raises him;  
> At the end, free passage and good fortune.[^65]

## 25. Xin Days, Wuzi Hour
### 六辛日子時斷
<!-- source: L1168-L1219 -->

> A Xin day born at Wuzi hour:  
> Seal-Ribbon and the Hall of Learning sit with Food God.  
> Without Bing, Ding, or Wu to break it,  
> This must be a person of splendor and manifest distinction.

On a Xin day at Wuzi hour, the Six Yin Face the Yang. Xin Metal at Zi has Birth and the Hall of Learning. Xin takes Wu as Seal and Gui as Food; Wu is visible and Gui concealed in the hour. Without Bing, Ding, or Wu clashing it open in the pillars, and with a month strengthening the body, there is great distinction. Those offending do not attain distinction. Without communication with the month’s qi, corresponding luck also gives distinction.

**Xinchou day.** Chou month gives distinguished Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer. Pure Chou gives a scholarly post; pure Chen with western luck gives chief-censor rank. You month gives distinction in northeast luck. Avoid Gengyin month: facial tattooing and misfortune; Guiwei month: punishment; Guichou month: ruin, solitude, and misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Bingxu:** Chief Censor Sun Shen. **Guiyou / Yichou:** minister of the Court of Imperial Stud. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** an assistant commissioner.〉

**Xinmao day.** In spring, Yin gives poverty and Mao distinction. Summer gives pure distinction; autumn’s Yang Blade brings no benefit; winter gives wealth. Chou month is especially auspicious. Chen, Xu, and Wei months give Wealth, Officer, and Seal-Ribbon, all auspicious. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Metal-and-Fire transformation gives distinction. Avoid Xinmao month: the parents die violently; Xinsi month: great ruin and misfortune; Renshen month: death with the body mutilated. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingchen / Renchen:** a chief censor. **Gengchen / Guiwei:** a censor. **Dingchou / Guichou:** a censor. **Xinmao / Xinmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiwei / Jimao:** a provincial graduate and judicial official, poor. **Renzi / Renzi:** a provincial graduate. **Yihai / Yimao:** a provincial graduate. **Bingshen / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate. **Xinyou / Gengyin:** Governor Liu Yikun. **Jichou / Xin—:** a vice minister; the month’s branch is damaged. **Dingyou / Renyin:** Huang Xiyin, currently chief minister of the Court of Imperial Stud.〉[^66]

**Xinsi day.** Yin, Si, and Wu months give Direct Officer and manifest distinction. Hai and Zi give Hurting Officer, few children, and many wives. Mao and Xu give fifth-rank distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 the Metal-transformation image gives distinction. Avoid Bingyin month: punishment and an ordinary outcome; Yisi month: early poverty; Dingyou month: early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Wuxu:** a minister. **Guichou / Xinyou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Bingyin / Dingyou:** an associate prefect. **Wuzi / Dingsi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renwu / Gengxu:** a vice minister. **Dinghai / Renyin:** Censor Wang Jiquan, promoted to reader-in-waiting for seeking out extraordinary arts and extraordinary practitioners.〉

**Xinwei day.** Distinction. Yin, Si, Wu, and You in year and month give distinction in Fire-and-Wood luck. Hai and Zi give Hanlin office. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Mixed-Qi Wealth, Officer, and Seal, all auspicious. Avoid Gengshen month: death with the body mutilated; Renzi month: solitary misfortune; Guichou month: death through ominous punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Wuchen:** Hanlin scholar Peng Feng. **Renshen / Renzi:** Minister Wang Guoguang. **Jiazi / Yihai:** Luo Hongxian, palace-examination first, from Jiangxi; a Songjiang licentiate and a Shandong member of the imperial clan shared the chart. **Bingshen / Wuxu:** a bureau director. **Yimao / Guiwei:** a censor. **Jiaxu / Gengwu:** a capital prefect. **Guisi / Bingchen:** a censor. **Jiaxu / Dingchou:** a prefect, violently killed and childless. **Renyin / Xinhai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guihai / Dingsi:** an academician. **Guiyou / Jiayin:** a prefect. **Jiwei / Bingzi:** Meng Yanghao, in the southern censorate.〉

**Xinyou day.** Birth in Zi or You month, without Bing or Ding Fire and without southern luck, gives third or fourth rank, injury to wife and children, and weighty military authority. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Metal-and-Fire transformation gives distinction; only after losing the ancestral establishment does one develop. Avoid Renyin month: violent death; Xinsi month: great ancestral ruin and misfortune; Gengxu month: violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Dingmao:** a vice minister. **Bingzi / Xinmao:** a vice minister. **Guisi / Jiayin:** a commander-in-chief. **Dinghai / Yiyou:** a deputy commissioner. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** a prefect. **Jiazi / Guiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingyin / Wuxu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Wuzi / Yichou:** Vice Minister Wang Ning. **Wuzi / Xinyou:** an education commissioner. **Bingxu / Xinchou:** a vice censor-in-chief. **Wuyin / Wuwu:** Capital Prefect Song Shixiang. **Yihai / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate who died in the fourth month of that year. **Dingyou / Wushen:** a vice minister. **Wuxu / Yimao:** poverty.〉

The six Xin days meeting Wuzi hour are called **The Pure and Illustrious Qi of Heaven’s Court**. Xinyou and Xinhai obtain its highest form. Avoid Bingyin month: death with the body mutilated; Bingshen month: displacement and violent death; Dingyou month: disappearance or loss and violent death.

**Xinhai day.** If not distinguished, then rich. Hai month without Officer gives Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed, developing blessing but giving few children. Spring, summer, Hai, Zi, and Chou, with a Wood-and-Fire luck configuration, give gold and purple and censorial office. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Gengshen:** a minister. **Renwu / Xinhai:** Hu [Jie], chancellor of the Directorate of Education. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renshen / Guihai:** a bureau director. **Yiyou / Xinsi:** a prefect. **Bingxu / Gengzi:** distinction. **Jichou / Bingzi:** misfortune. **Renxu / “shizi”:** a marquis; the month is corrupt. **Dingwei / Bingwu:** a man and woman sharing the chart; the man suffered punishment, the woman was a courtesan.〉[^67]

〈Distinction Born in a Humble House:  
Blessing and Lu naturally shine forth.  
Without punishment, clash, or breaking,  
Advancement reaches the provincial hall.〉

> The six Xin Heavenly Origins, born at Zi hour:  
> Spring comes, flowers open in radiant brilliance.  
> If Bing, Si, Wu, or Ding brings damage,  
> Achievement is hard to hope for and comes only late.

> A Xin day meeting Wuzi hour:  
> The Six Yin gather to face the Yang.  
> Metal Spirit and Seal-Ribbon display majestic light,  
> Assisting the body to honor and flourishing.  
> Si and Wu diminish blessing when encountered;  
> Bing parts the ranks of geese and the honored hall.  
> Wife and children labor to prosper house and fields;  
> Without breaking, a humble house produces ministers and generals.[^68]

## 26. Xin Days, Jichou Hour
### 六辛日丑時斷
<!-- source: L1220-L1261 -->
〈For the six Xin days below, unfavorable months are to be considered together with the hour, as above.〉

> A Xin day born at Jichou hour:  
> Metal and Earth contend, their situation unsettled.  
> Wealth and Officer in year and month bring rescue,  
> Preventing poverty, hunger, and cold.

On a Xin day at Jichou hour, Metal and Earth contend. Xin takes Ji as Inverted Food; Chou has visible Ji and hidden Xin. Without rescue from Wealth and Officer in year and month there is poverty. Wealth-and-Officer luck is also auspicious.

**Xinchou day.** Solitary, turbid, and delayed. Shen or You month strengthens Metal; Fire luck opens passage, but wife and children suffer injury. A monk or Daoist attains distinction. Pure Chou gives great distinction in southwest luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Renyin:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Gengxu / Xinsi:** a deputy commissioner. **Bingwu / Gengyin:** an assistant commissioner. **Wuchen / Xinyou:** a prefect. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** Zhao, palace-examination first. **Wuzi / Jiazi:** a county magistrate. **Bingshen / Yiwei:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiashen / Xinwei:** a violent death.〉

**Xinmao day.** Yin, Mao, Hai, and Wei months give the Wealth-Star configuration and distinction in southern luck. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Seal-Ribbon, auspicious in southern luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Guichou:** a county magistrate. **Bingchen / Dingyou:** a provincial graduate. **Gengyin / [Bingxu]:** wealth and distinction.〉[^69]

**Xinsi day.** Chou, Si, Shen, and You months bring much Metal controlling Wood and injury to wife and children. Monks and Daoists attain distinction. Yin, Mao, and Chen months give distinction in southern luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Jiyou:** the Ming Shizong emperor; another account gives Wuzi hour. **Bingxu / Wuxu:** the Prince of Dai. **Guiyou / Jiazi:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Yimao / Guiwei:** Minister Wang Bangrui. **Renshen / Yisi:** a provincial graduate. **Gengchen / Wuyin:** a Hanlin examiner. **Jiachen / Yihai:** received an honorary title.〉[^70]

**Xinwei day.** Yin and Mao Wood months give the Wealth-Star configuration. Si and Wu make Officer manifest. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuyin / Yimao:** a senior provincial administrator. **Xinhai / Wuxu:** a provincial graduate. **Xinyou / Jiawu:** Gong Yongqing, palace-examination first.〉

**Xinyou day.** Loss of the ancestral establishment. Si, You, and Chou months give much Metal controlling Wood: handsome and wealthy, but wife and children are difficult. Yin and Wu months give distinction in close attendance. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Yisi:** a minister. **Guiwei / Jiayin:** Vice Minister Wang Zongmu. **Xinsi / Dingyou:** a county magistrate. **Wuxu / Yichou:** a county magistrate. **Gengyin / Jiashen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guisi / Jiayin:** Shi, a vice minister in a court whose given-name graph is uncertain; died in prison.〉[^71]

**Xinhai day.** Yin, Si, and Wu months make Officer Star bright, allowing plans to establish a household. Mao and Wei form a Wealth configuration, giving wealth and distinction; the clash of Wei and Xu is auspicious. Shen–Zi–Chen in year and month gives Metal and Water holding refinement, a fine prospect. You gives Established Lu, auspicious in Wood-and-Fire luck. Pure Hai gives distinguished Metal-and-Water refinement. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Yihai:** Vice Minister Wang Jiao. **Dingwei / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiyou / Jisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wushen / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate. **Jiashen / Xinwei:** a county magistrate. **Xinhai / Yiwei:** an associate prefect. **Xinchou / Gengyin:** a transmission commissioner.〉[^72]

〈Inverted Food Meeting the Hour:  
Wealth and Officer lie hidden in the Storehouse.  
An unfavorable time brings many obstacles;  
Blood kin stand apart like Shen and Shang.〉

> Jichou hour meeting a Xin day is perilous;  
> Wealth and Officer buried are no excellence yet.  
> The Six Relations and blood kin suffer much punishment and harm;  
> Year and month clashing it open promise wealth and distinction.

> A Xin day at Jichou hour  
> Is detained throughout by Inverted Food.  
> The golden coffer is locked tightly shut;  
> Wu, Wei, and Xu open it to accomplishment.  
> Jia, Bing, Mao, and Yin develop blessing;  
> Gui, Ren, Hai, and Zi bring wandering.  
> In youth, plans and hopes are hard to fulfill;  
> Middle and later life have a future of their own.

## 27. Xin Days, Gengyin Hour
### 六辛日寅時斷
<!-- source: L1262-L1307 -->

> A Xin day born at Gengyin hour:  
> Wealth flourishes, generating Officer and meeting Noble Spirit.  
> Within a Metal-and-Wood configuration, the month’s qi communicates:  
> Surely a person of honor, distinction, and abundant wealth.

On a Xin day at Gengyin hour, there are Noble Person, Wealth, and Officer. Xin takes Yin as Tianyi Noble Person, Bing Fire as Officer, and Jia Wood as Wealth. Bing and Jia flourish upon Yin. Communication with a Metal or Wood month’s qi, or with the luck course, gives wealth, distinction, and manifest advancement.

**Xinchou day.** Spring gives distinction; summer flourishing Officer; autumn advancement; winter good fortune. Chou month with southern luck gives censorial or military distinction. 〈One account says:〉 development is followed immediately by a wind disorder. Shen and You complete in year and month, with no Fire qi at all, give poverty and early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Wuyin:** Minister Gu Qing. **Jihai / Xinwei:** Censor-in-Chief Liu Tianhe. **Renxu / Dingwei:** a censor. **Renxu / Xinhai:** a bureau director. **Jimao / Jiaxu:** a censor. **Jichou / Bingyin:** a bureau director. **Bingyin / Yiwei:** Provincial Administrator Shen Yan. **Jiashen / Yihai:** Geng Dingxiang, head of the Nanjing Censorate. **Yiyou / Jiashen:** poverty and early death.〉

**Xinmao day.** Wu, Wei, Hai, and Zi all give distinction; Yin, Mao, Shen, and You give sixth or seventh rank. 〈One account says:〉 the father dies early, and wind disorders are frequent. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Jiwei:** a commander-in-chief. **Yihai / Bingxu:** a prefect. **Xinwei / Jihai:** a provincial graduate. **Dingmao / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate. **Yiyou / Jiashen:** a provincial graduate. **Bingyin / Yiwei:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Xinsi day.** Spring gives distinction. Summer gives Officer and Lu, with great distinction in northwest luck. Autumn strengthens the body; winter gives violent cruelty. 〈One account says:〉 wealth follows punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Bingwu:** a Grand Preceptor. **Wuwu / Jiwei:** a chancellor. **Gengchen / Jimao:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Xinwei day.** Si, Wei, and Chou months put the name upon the golden [examination list], with a worthy wife and filial children; later life brings a wind disorder. Yin, Mao, Wu, and Xu are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Jiaxu:** Liu, palace-examination first. **Jiaxu / Gengwu:** longevity. **Dingmao / Bingwu:** wealth. **Wuyin / Yimao:** a provincial graduate. **Wuwu / Bingchen:** solitude, poverty, and early death.〉[^73]

**Xinyou day.** Distinction. Spring gathers Wealth; summer completes Officer and Lu; autumn strengthens the body. Hai, Zi, Chou, Xu, Wu, You, and similar years and months all give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Jichou:** Vice Minister Wu Sanle. **Bingshen / Xinmao:** a vice minister. **Jiachen / Bingzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingyou / Xinhai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinyou / Gengyin:** violent death. **Renyin / Gengxu:** dependence on others and a search for livelihood. **Bingchen / Wuxu:** a supervising secretary for Personnel. **Jihai / Xinwei:** Minister Wen Chun. **Guiwei / Jiwei:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Xinhai day.** Distinction, with hidden illness. Bing exposed in the stems brings development. Wu, You, Yin, and Hai in year and month give distinction in southern luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Xinmao:** Minister Li Youzi. **Guiyou / Renxu:** a vice minister. **Gengzi / Jichou:** a chief censor. **Jiazi / Gengwu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Wuyin / Xinyou:** a bureau director. **Renchen / Gengxu:** a ministry secretary. **Renshen / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Xinwei / Dingyou:** a regional military commissioner. **Wuxu / Yimao:** immense wealth. **Jihai / Guiyou:** violent death.〉

〈Turning from Darkness toward Light:  
Throughout life, aspiration rises high.  
Wealth and Officer in year and month:  
Orchids emerge from wormwood and weeds.〉

> The six Xin days encountering Yin hour:  
> Wealth flourishes and generates Officer—judge their reciprocal relations.  
> Ill-favored luck leaves profit and fame obstructed;  
> Travel through Wealth and Lu, and no further fear remains.

> A Xin day meeting Gengyin hour:  
> Brothers and blood kin grow estranged.  
> Parents and ancestral property are hard to rely upon;  
> In middle life the married pair lose their mirror’s clarity.  
> Hai, Gui, Kan, and Ren diminish blessing;  
> Bing, Ding, Si, and Wu spread the name abroad.  
> Spring birth or winter birth earns the noble person’s regard:  
> A fate of splendor in middle and later years.[^74]

## 28. Xin Days, Xinmao Hour
### 六辛日夘時斷
<!-- source: L1308-L1359 -->

> A Xin day born at Xinmao hour:  
> Companions encountered make wife and children hard to sustain.  
> Autumn birth or winter birth forms a poor and lowly pattern;  
> Yet Bing approaching Yin and Horse commands authority.

On a Xin day at Xinmao hour, Companions divide Wealth. Xin takes Yi as Wealth; Yi flourishes at Mao, but Companions divide and seize it, injuring wife and children. Autumn and winter give Wealth and Officer no qi: an ordinary fate. In Yin, Si, or Wu month, Bing Fire exposed combines with Xin, generating Officer and bringing distinction and advancement. Xinmao is Suspended-Needle Sha; many occurrences in the pillars are inauspicious.

**Xinchou day.** Spring and summer birth in a region of substantial Earth gives wealth and distinction. Autumn harms wife and children; winter brings hardship. Yin, Si, and Wu months give distinction in close attendance. Jiaxu in year and month, with Wood-and-Fire luck, gives censorial office. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingshen / Xinchou:** a minister. **Renchen / Gengxu:** Provincial Administration Commissioner Zha; violent death. **Jiashen / Bingzi:** a censor. **Gengshen / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinchou / Gengyin:** Geng Dingli, head of the Nanjing Censorate. **Yichou / Wuzi:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Xinmao day.** Yin, Wu, and Xu months give Wealth and Officer in Double Beauty and manifest distinction. Mao, You, Shen, and Chen in year and month give distinction in close attendance; Officer, Seal, and Wealth Star appearing are excellent. 〈One account says:〉 hardship first, great development in middle age. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Yimao:** Grand Secretary Xu. **Guiyou / Bingchen:** Yan Shifan, who rose through inherited favor to vice minister; his crimes were grievous and his wickedness extreme, and he was duly executed. **Wuwu / Yichou:** Hanlin scholar Yu Yuanli. **Jihai / Bingzi:** Minister Zheng Shen. **Renzi / Renzi:** a provincial administrator. **Dingmao / Jiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** a censor; died in Wuwu year. **Jiaxu / Jisi:** a county magistrate. **Jiazi / Renshen:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Jimao / Dingmao:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Dingmao / Jiyou:** a censor; died young.〉

**Xinsi day.** Spring strengthens Wealth; summer Officer; autumn the body; winter gives weakness. Si and You in year and month, with Wood-and-Fire luck, give distinction among imperial relatives. Shen month with Water-and-Fire luck gives gold and purple; Si month with northwest luck gives censorial office. 〈Recorded cases—**Jihai / Jiaxu:** a surveillance commissioner. **Xinwei / Bingshen:** violent death in Wuwu year during Chen luck.〉

**Xinwei day.** Pure Wei and Ding in year and month give great authority and distinction, but no good end. Pure Yin, Si, or Hai is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Dingsi:** Minister Zhao Feng. **Xinyou / Gengyin:** Chief Censor Fu Zhen. **Yihai / Yiyou:** Grand Secretary Yin Shidan. **Wushen / Jiazi:** a metropolitan graduate and subprefect. **Jiazi / Yihai:** a subprefect.〉

**Xinyou day.** Solitude and hardship at the outset, blessing in middle life, and later distinction bringing titles to the wife and hereditary favor to the children. Si month gives Officer and Seal meeting Heavenly Virtue: first rank. Chou month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives moderate distinction. Shen, You, or Hai in year and month gives censorial office in southeast luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Xinsi:** Grand Secretary Zhang Juzheng, who held exclusive authority for ten years; died in Renwu year at the end of Bingzi luck because year and luck clashed. **Wushen / Wuwu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Xinmao / Bingshen:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** Hua Yingqi, palace-examination first and vice minister; died by hanging, according to *Star Cases*. **Bingzi / Xinchou:** an envoy. **Jiaxu / Guiyou:** Sun Jiaji, metropolitan graduate and director in the Bureau of Military Operations, excellent in talent and learning; reached vice minister at the end of the Ming. **Xinhai / Xinchou:** Vice Minister Wang Guo.〉[^75]

**Xinhai day.** Spring and summer give Wealth and Officer in Double Beauty and manifest distinction. Autumn and winter turn the back on Lu and drive away Horse: toil and repeated reversals. Pure Hai gives Metal and Water holding refinement and often a high examination placing. Chen and Xu are auspicious; Chou and Wei are not auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinsi / Wuxu:** Minister Wu Guifang; another account gives Gengyin hour. **Yiyou / Jichou:** Vice Minister Wang Daokun, renowned for literature. **Yichou / Bingxu:** Censor Qian Sichuang; violent death. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** Fan Yingqi, palace-examination first and vice minister; died by hanging, according to the original text. **Jiwei / Renshen:** a censor. **Xinchou / Guisi:** a provincial graduate. **Wuyin / Renxu:** a censor. **Bingyin / Bingshen:** a censor.〉[^76]

〈Yang Blade Robbing Wealth:  
Hour and month have their fullness and decline.  
Wealth and Lu gather in middle life;  
Fear sorrow for the mandarin pair.〉

> The six Xin meeting Mao have Lu clearly defined;  
> Companions and Rob Wealth meeting Wealth leave affairs unfulfilled.  
> Spring and summer births have flourishing Wealth and Lu;  
> Autumn and winter, punishment and harm—an ordinary fate.

> A Xin day meeting Xinmao hour:  
> Two Xin divide and seize wife and Wealth.  
> Ranks of geese and mandarin pairs find little harmony;  
> Stand alone and establish yourself without hindrance.  
> Wealth Star at Birth and flourishing in year and month:  
> Gladly, Lu itself descends from Heaven.  
> Luck through Companions and Rob Wealth buries undertakings;  
> Fire-and-Wood luck brings free and tranquil passage.[^77]

## 29. Xin Days, Renchen Hour
### 六辛日辰時斷
<!-- source: L1360-L1409 -->

> A Xin day born at Renchen hour:  
> Hurting Officer wholly removing Officer doubles the spirit’s vigor.  
> Fire weak in the Four Pillars—guard against control and harm;  
> Among the various callings, the arts of divination and medicine.

On a Xin day at Renchen hour, hidden Metal sinks beneath the Water. Xin uses Bing as Officer and Ren as Hurting Officer; Chen is Water’s Storehouse. Bing and Xin lack qi, while Ren Water forms its configuration. If Bing emerges in year or month, this is Hurting Officer Meeting Officer, with a hundred kinds of punishment and calamity. The person is haughty and boastful, refined but without substance. Without communication with the month’s qi or other support, success and failure alternate; the calling is medicine, divination, or an art. With Wood and Fire in the pillars and a strong body, southeast luck gives distinction.

**Xinchou day.** Spring brings advancement; summer is ordinary, with name and profit but a cruel disposition. You month with eastern luck gives distinction. Xu and Wei complete the Four Storehouses: the highest distinction. Pure Wei gives Mixed-Qi Wealth and Seal; the clash between Chou and Wei opens them, bringing authority and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Jiwei:** Vice Minister Wang Jijin. **Guiyou / Jiwei:** Minister Yin Zhengmao. **Wushen / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiashen / Wuchen:** first in the provincial examination. **Renwu / Jiyou:** Minister Liu Yaohui.〉

**Xinmao day.** Spring gives flourishing Wealth, a worthy wife, and filial children. Summer gives Hurting Officer Meeting Officer, yet also often wealth. Autumn is auspicious; winter brings solitude and injury, but frequently distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Xinhai:** a supervising secretary. **Dingmao / Bingwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingmao / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yisi / Renwu:** a poor student. **Renshen / Wushen:** Metropolitan Graduate He Dongru, whose career rose and fell, reaching secretary in the Nanjing Ministry of War.〉

**Xinsi day.** Spring gives Hurting Officer Generating Wealth. Summer brings reversals in public life and retirement, or violent misfortune; Wu and Wei are nevertheless exceptional. In autumn, Shen and You strengthen the body, and Fire obtained is auspicious. In winter, Hai and Zi give Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed; Wood and Earth are excellent. Distinction is weighty and standing high, though punishment and injury remain. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give military distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Wuzi:** Luo Qinshun, called palace-examination first, an eminent minister. **Bingwu / Xinchou:** Minister Sun Yingkui, firm and upright. **Gengwu / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingchou / [Bingwu]:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guichou / Bingchen:** an officer of the Embroidered-Uniform Guard. **Wuchen / Xinyou:** a deputy commander. **Dingsi / Dingwei:** a capital prefect.〉[^78]

**Xinwei day.** Solitary. Spring’s Wealth-Star configuration is auspicious; summer gives toil; autumn distinction; winter gives the excellence of Hurting Officer wholly removing Officer. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengshen / Jiashen:** a minister of Rites. **Renshen / Wushen:** Vice Minister Yang Lian. **Bingxu / Wuxu:** Vice Minister Wang Tingsheng. **Renwu / Bingwu:** a provincial graduate. **Xinwei / Gengzi:** a county magistrate. **Wuzi / Yichou:** Hanlin scholar Zhang Zhi, who died early without an heir.〉

**Xinyou day.** Spring gives wealth and distinction together; summer is good; autumn strengthens the body and gives distinction; winter gives an untitled commoner. Zi month is Xin’s Birth and Hall of Learning, indicating literary learning; Fire in year and month bringing balance gives distinction. Jiaxu month gives third rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengzi / Jimao:** Minister Wang Jiu’an. **Dingsi / Renzi:** Assistant Surveillance Commissioner Wang Zhongshan; his son was a metropolitan graduate. **Bingzi / Guisi:** a prefect. **Yiyou / Xinsi:** a provincial graduate. **Xinsi / Guisi:** a provincial graduate. **Yiyou / Yiyou:** a man subjected to punishment. **Jiyou / Yihai:** Cui Hengqian, a profligate son; punished in Jiashen year for joining the rebels.〉

**Xinhai day.** Distinction. Spring’s flourishing Wealth supports the body; summer is auspicious; autumn’s strong body is good; winter wholly removes Officer through Hurting Officer, giving independent establishment and accomplishment. Mao and Wei in year and month give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Dingwei:** Grand Secretary Xu Guo. **Wuchen / Yimao:** a prefect. **Jiyou / Guiyou:** a bureau director. **Renzi / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingmao / Bingwu:** a vice minister. **Guisi / Renxu:** a provincial graduate. **Wuwu / Renxu:** a provincial graduate. **Jiaxu / Gengwu:** Run Rumei, from Jiangdu, provincial graduate in Dingyou and metropolitan graduate in Wuchen, secretary in the Ministry of Justice, a gentleman of substantial character. **Bingyin / Renchen:** the mother of Grand Secretary Zhang Juzheng.〉[^79]

〈A Fine Stream Flowing Long:  
The six Xin all belong to yin.  
When luck travels flourishing Wealth’s ground,  
Office and Lu arrive of themselves.〉

> A Xin day stem, with Renchen in the hour:  
> Wealth and Officer locked away, undertakings cannot yet succeed.  
> Without a key’s passage, and held down besides,  
> From of old, the young have found advancement hard.

> A Xin day meeting Renchen hour:  
> Hurting Officer wholly removing Officer is exceptional.  
> Ancestral property and parents are difficult early on;  
> The ranks of geese fly apart without attachment.  
> Spring and summer bring Wealth and Officer to flourishing;  
> In southeast luck, put your abilities to use.  
> Plan for yourself, establish yourself, build the family’s resources:  
> No strength comes from those close to you.

## 30. Xin Days, Guisi Hour
### 六辛日巳時斷
<!-- source: L1410-L1453 -->

> A Xin day born at Guisi hour:  
> Noble qi is unharmed; Officer and Seal are strong.  
> The month’s qi communicates, with support besides:  
> Early honor and distinction, a fragrant name.

On a Xin day at Guisi hour, Officer and Seal support the body. Xin takes Bing as Officer, Wu as Seal, and Gui as Food God. Bing and Wu are vigorous upon Si; Gui combines and transforms into Fire, giving the literary brilliance of red and white. Support and communication with the month’s qi bring advancement. If the month does not communicate, a communicating luck course also gives distinction.

**Xinchou day.** Ominous; leaving the parents brings blessing. Spring and summer are auspicious; autumn and winter give violent misfortune. 〈One account says:〉 distinction through transformation; abstain from wine. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Guihai:** a first-place examination graduate. **Wuchen / Dingsi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingchen / Guisi:** Zhang Maoxiu, palace-examination first; stripped of status. **Xinmao / Yiwei:** a man subjected to punishment.〉

**Xinmao day.** Spring and summer weaken the body and shorten life; autumn strengthens the body but brings labor and hardship. Add a month’s qi of Fire and Wood: third- or fourth-rank distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Jiashen:** a bureau director. **Bingwu / Xinmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yihai / Jiashen:** a bureau director. **Bingchen / Yiwei:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Xinsi day.** Distinction through transformation. Zi or Wu years and Yin, Wu, or Xu months give distinction; Hai, Mao, and Wei give pure distinction; Shen, Zi, and Chen make a person without righteousness. Xinsi and Guisi have Two Stems Unmixed. Yin and Chen with Bing exposed give the highest distinction; Chou and You completing the Triple Combination are also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Guisi:** Lü Chunyang, a Daoist immortal. **Bingchen / Bingyin:** Fu Bi, Duke of Zheng, an eminent minister. **Xinsi / Guisi:** Kou Zhun, Duke of Lai, an eminent minister.〉

〈On examining Daoist books, Master Lü was born in Tianbao’s fourteenth year, Yiwei, on the fourteenth day of the fourth month, at Si hour. On examining the *History of the Song*, Master Kou was born on the fourteenth day of the seventh month. Wei Ye’s poem says: “When was the chief minister born? Tomorrow is the Mid-Prime.” The astrologers’ transmitted accounts are all false.〉[^80]

〈Further recorded cases—**Renwu / Renzi:** a prefect. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** Hu Chaochen, assistant commissioner in the Office of Transmission; **Jiaxu / Jisi:** Shen Shu. Both men languished in prison. **Wuwu / Yichou:** a commander-in-chief. **Guihai / Gengshen:** a provincial graduate. **Gengzi / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^81]

**Xinwei day.** Yin, Wu, and Xu months give an elevated fate. Si, You, and Chou strengthen the body and bring stability. Shen, Zi, and Chen give an untitled commoner; Hai, Mao, and Wei pure distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Xinwei:** Minister Zou. **Bingwu / Bingshen:** a prefect. **Jiazi / Yihai:** Zhang Run of the Embroidered-Uniform Guard, immensely rich, with many sons, living sixty years. **Jiwei / Gengwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuxu / Jiwei:** dissipation and infatuation ruined the household.〉[^82]

**Xinyou day.** Manifest distinction, with a strong appetite for wine and sex. Yin and Mao give flourishing Wealth and good fortune; Chou completes the Triple Combination, giving wealth and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renyin / Guimao:** a prefect. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** wealth and distinction. **Bingyin / Yiwei:** a deputy surveillance commissioner.〉

**Xinhai day.** Hour and day clash, raising concern for wife and children. Spring strengthens Wealth; summer is auspicious; autumn ordinary; winter violently ominous. You and Wu in year and month, with Wood-and-Fire luck, give censorial office and a seat in the Censorate. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Jihai:** Minister Zhan Ruoshui, a scholar of the Way. **Guiyou / Gengshen:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Xinsi / Wuxu:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Jiwei / Renshen:** a bureau director.〉

〈The Xin Stem Facing the Hour:  
Officer and Seal gladly meet.  
Without punishment, clash, or breaking,  
The person rises to the imperial palace.〉

> Guisi hour encounters a Xin day stem:  
> Wealth and Officer made manifest in the pillars are especially welcome.  
> Luck through Lu and Horse, on ground without punishment:  
> A name inscribed on the golden list, steps beside the imperial carriage.

> A Xin day approaching Guisi hour:  
> Spring birth strengthens Wealth and establishes the foundation.  
> Bing, Ding, and Wu years are most exceptional;  
> Breaking, control, punishment, and clash are adverse.  
> Ren, Gui, Geng, and Shen, with no breaking:  
> Achievement, riches, and distinction are assured.  
> A worthy wife and filial children are both fitting;  
> Punishment and breaking at Si hour are unfavorable.[^83]

## 31. Xin Days, Jiawu Hour
### 六辛日午時斷
<!-- source: L1454-L1501 -->

> The six Xin days encountering Jiawu hour:  
> Hidden ghost and Owl Spirit are truly to be feared.  
> Without support, life becomes a burden of toil;  
> Do not say that Xin meeting Horse must be noble.

On a Xin day at Jiawu hour, ghost flourishes and the body declines. Xin uses Ding as ghost, Ji as Inverted Food, and Jia as Wealth. Jia Wood dies at Wu, leaving the influence without qi, while Ding and Ji are vigorous. Although Wu is Tianyi Noble Person, life is full of reversals. Even communication with flourishing qi gives distinction that does not endure. Birth in a Fire or Earth month gives distinction in western luck.

**Xinchou day.** With Wei or Shen in year and month and punishment or harm in the Four Pillars, there is wealth but a short life. Communication with Metal gives distinction, yet it does not endure. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingchen / Jiawu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Renxu / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingyou / Dingwei:** one metropolitan graduate and one official’s son share the chart. **Renchen / Dingwei:** a bureau director.〉

**Xinmao day.** Mao month gives military distinction. Yin and Xu ruin the ancestral establishment; otherwise disability or early death. Si and Wu give seventh- or eighth-rank distinction; luck is judged likewise. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Guisi:** a provincial graduate. **Gengzi / Jiawu:** the Prince of Zhou.〉

**Xinsi day.** Ordinary. Yin, Wu, and Xu strengthen Officer and are auspicious; Shen, Zi, and Chen are ordinary. Mao and Xu years give Officer and Seal both complete: duke or marquis. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiyou / Wuwu:** a provincial administrator. **Xinhai / Bingshen:** a county magistrate. **Xinsi / Jihai:** Minister Yang Cheng. **Renchen / Yisi:** violent death.〉

**Xinwei day.** Shen, Zi, and Chen restrain Indirect Officer and are auspicious. Chou and Xu give provincial authority; Wei month gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Wushen:** Vice Minister Ouyang Duo. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** a censor. **Wuwu / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Jiazi / Dingchou:** the father of Grand Secretary Zhang Juzheng. **Renchen / Gengxu:** a provincial graduate who was blind.〉

**Xinyou day.** Spring’s flourishing Wealth and summer’s noble Officer are both auspicious. Autumn strengthens the body: wealth, but no lasting span. Winter is ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Jisi:** Master Xue Wenqing, honored with associated worship in the temple court. **Yimao / Wuyin:** a provincial graduate. **Gengxu / Renwu:** Ji Zhengshi, who, at a drinking gathering, played a game of enfeoffing marquises; a temple monk denounced him for plotting rebellion, and he was executed in the marketplace.〉[^84]

**Xinhai day.** Hai and Zi in year and month give Metal and Water holding refinement: praiseworthy literary learning. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer and manifest distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Renzi:** Minister Yang. **Guihai / Jiwei:** Minister Zhao Wenhua, of Yan Song’s faction.〉

〈Four Intentions and Three Minds:  
Plans and undertakings stall in doubt.  
Though Wealth and Lu gather,  
One shoulders both approval and blame.〉

> The six Xin day stems with Jiawu in the hour:  
> Wealth Spirit lacks qi and cannot answer to the task.  
> Spring birth, Wood flourishing, and Wealth-and-Officer luck:  
> A name flows far and wide along an unbroken course.

> A Xin day approaching Jiawu hour:  
> Wife and Wealth lack qi, the body declines.  
> Strong stems and flourishing Fire make the ghost’s embryo;  
> Heavy Fire over-refines yielding Metal and ruins it.  
> Above all, avoid injury to the useful influence:  
> Metal sinking to the seabed brings calamity.  
> Without punishment or breaking, the heart’s desires are fulfilled:  
> Weighty distinction, radiant and far-reaching.[^85]

## 32. Xin Days, Yiwei Hour
### 六辛日未時斷
<!-- source: L1502-L1547 -->

> A Xin day born at Yiwei hour:  
> Fire and Wood complete one another; Metal has no fear.  
> The month communicates with Metal qi, or spring brings flourishing:  
> Wealth strengthens Officer, and the person is noble.

On a Xin day at Yiwei hour, Heavenly Wealth enters the Storehouse. Xin uses Yi as Wealth, entering its Storehouse at Wei; Ji is Inverted Food and Ding the proper ghost. Hidden Ding and Ji upon Wei are restrained by visible Yi and cannot cause harm. Communication with a Si, You, or Chou month gives distinction. Communication with Fire followed by western luck, or with Metal followed by southern luck, likewise gives distinction.

**Xinchou day.** Punishment. Hai, Mao, Wei, and Yin months give Wealth flourishing to generate Officer: if not distinguished, then rich. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Zi are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiwei / Dinghai:** Vice Minister Lin Shengwu. **Jichou / Jiaxu:** a censor. **Xinyou / Wuxu:** an academician. **Renshen / Dingwei:** Chief Censor Hu Rulin. **Dingmao / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Renwu / Jiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Dingchou / Wushen:** a provincial graduate. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** a provincial graduate. **Gengyin / Guiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Yiyou / Bingxu:** a remonstrance official. **Bingyin / Gengyin:** Minister Chen Shaoru of Nanhai; an Imperial Academy student from Jiangxi shared the chart.〉[^86]

**Xinmao day.** Hai, Mao, and Wei months give abundant Wealth generating Officer: auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinwei / Xinmao:** a provincial graduate. **Jichou / Xinwei:** Commander-in-Chief Li Guoliang; died for missing a military opportunity.〉

**Xinsi day.** A month communicating with Metal and Fire gives distinction; a communicating luck course does likewise. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiayin / Yiyou:** Education Commissioner Yi. **Renyin / Guimao:** a senior provincial administrator, greedy and base.〉

**Xinwei day.** Yin, Mao, and Wei in year and month give Wealth flourishing to generate Officer: high reputation and substantial emolument.
