Communication with Fire qi followed by western luck, or with Metal qi followed by southern luck, is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Yimao:** Grand Secretary Jiang Mian. **Bingyin / Bingshen:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Dingyou / Renyin:** a ministry secretary. **Jiayin / Xinwei:** Shen Jingjie, governor of Yunnan. **Wuzi / Yimao:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Xinyou day.** A month communicating with Fire qi gives distinction in eastern luck. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei are most auspicious. Hai and Mao give manifest distinction that does not endure. Autumn birth makes the body too strong; Wealth and Officer lack qi, giving misfortune and early death. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Jiaxu:** Ouyang Shen, who obtained a guard command by contribution and rose to regional military commissioner, killed in battle; hereditary favor conferred the guard command, and his son was a metropolitan graduate. **Jisi / Wuchen:** an official’s son. **Jiwei / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉[^87]

**Xinhai day.** Hai, Mao, and Wei months give an auspicious Wealth-Star configuration. Autumn and winter give solitude. Chen and Chou give third rank in close attendance. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuchen / Jiazi:** distinction. **Jiyou / Dingchou:** first in the provincial examination.〉

〈Indirect Wealth Flourishing in the Hour:  
Yiwei holds it within the Storehouse.  
Where Chou and Xu encounter it,  
Achievement is fragrant with brush and ink.〉

> Wei hour on a Xin day opens the Storehouse gate;  
> Standing firm, the house is founded and wealth develops of itself.  
> Metal-and-Wood luck with a strong body is auspicious;  
> Through repeated dangers, blessing comes again.

> A Xin day meeting Yiwei hour:  
> Indirect Wealth emerges from the Storehouse.  
> Wood-and-Metal luck dislikes a declining body;  
> Chou and Xu luck brings open passage.  
> Passing through the early years brings blessing;  
> The bridal curtain withstands opposition without calamity.  
> Splendor, wealth, and distinction are arrayed within the fate;  
> With no breaking, office is pure and tranquil.[^88]

## 33. Xin Days, Bingshen Hour
### 六辛日申時斷
<!-- source: L1548-L1585 -->

> A Xin day born at Bingshen hour:  
> The month communicates with Metal and Fire, renewing the spirit’s vigor.  
> Transformation into Metal and Water meets Metal’s ground:  
> Blessing gathers, making a person rich and noble.

On a Xin day at Bingshen hour, Bing and Xin transform into Water, which is born upon Shen. If the month communicates with the Metal qi of Si, You, or Chou, the spirit is refined and beautiful, and literary talent gathers blessing. Communication with flourishing Fire qi, with support, gives distinction. Xinyou and Xinwei are best. If the transformation image does not form, Officer Star has no qi upon Shen, giving ordinary food and clothing.

**Xinchou day.** Distinction through transformation. Youth brings obstruction and loss; middle life, manifest distinction. In Hai month, judge transformed qi: auspicious. Yin, Wu, [Xu], Si, and Wei months give Officer Star qi; spring strengthens Wealth. All are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Jihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinyou / Wuxu:** distinction. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jihai / Renshen:** distinction.〉[^89]

**Xinmao day.** Distinction through transformation. Yin, Si, Wu, Wei, Xu, You, and Hai months make Officer Star bright: mountains clear and waters beautiful, examination success, honor, and distinction. Communication with Earth qi also gives distinction. 〈One account says:〉 youth obstructed, middle and later life good. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingshen / Yiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Xinhai / Wuxu:** a prefect.〉

**Xinsi day.** Seeing blood brings blessing. 〈Another account says:〉 loss amid distinction. Shen and Wei in year and month give a substantial body, a wife of consequence, filial children, and third rank in military office. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Jiazi:** a vice minister. **Gengzi / Wuzi:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yiyou / Jiashen:** a commander-in-chief. **Xinhai / Jiawu:** a prefect. **Dingsi / Gengxu:** Zhou, palace-examination first. **Dinghai / Renyin:** immense wealth. **Yisi / Xinsi:** a metropolitan graduate and subprefect.〉

**Xinwei day.** Wealth comes through service under a noble person. Spring gives pure distinction; summer an ordinary fate; autumn wealth; winter great distinction but a short life. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Metal and Water join discordantly; transformation into Water, again, gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jisi / Dingmao:** Lu Shusheng, metropolitan-examination first and minister, who lived ninety-seven years. **Renxu / Bingwu:** a prefect. **Jimao / Guiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiawu / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate.〉[^90]

**Xinyou day.** Yin, Si, Wu, Wei, and Xu months strengthen Officer and give honor and distinction. 〈The *Shenbai Classic* says:〉 Water transformation gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiyou / Gengwu:** a prefect. **Dingyou / Jiyou:** immense wealth.〉

**Xinhai day.** Blessing develops; guard against solitude, injury to kin, and illness. Communication with Metal qi gives a refined appearance and abundant blessing; communication with Water qi gives great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinhai / Bingshen:** a vice minister. **Renzi / Guichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiashen / Yihai:** an education commissioner. **Guiwei / Yichou:** a prefect. **Yiwei / Jichou:** Grand Secretary Feng Quan. **Renwu / Guimao:** Xue Guoguan, who entered the Grand Secretariat from provincial office and was ordered to die. **Gengxu / Dinghai:** a subprefect.〉

〈The Heavenly Origin Combining and Transforming:  
Winter birth brings blessing in abundance.  
When the time arrives, distinction follows;  
The name is inscribed upon the golden list.〉

> A Xin day meets the favorable Bingshen hour;  
> The Heavenly Origins combine and transform in truth.  
> Winter birth without punishment or breaking:  
> Manifest distinction reaches the crossings of high office.

> A Xin day meeting Bingshen hour:  
> Birth with Lu and Horse is rare excellence.  
> The Heavenly Origins combine and transform, displaying splendor:  
> Weighty office, a high name, and imposing authority.  
> The gentleman establishes himself through letters;  
> The common person flourishes and honors the family’s foundation.  
> If the birth hour is truly fixed, there is no deficiency;  
> Luck welcomes the lands of west, south, and east.[^91]

## 34. Xin Days, Dingyou Hour
### 六辛日酉時斷
<!-- source: L1586-L1627 -->

> A Xin day born at Dingyou hour:  
> Ghost breaks the Lu origin, bringing calamities of a hundred kinds.  
> Only support and a strong body allow an auspicious judgment;  
> Restraint communicating through the month makes Indirect Officer.

On a Xin day at Dingyou hour, Fire and Metal contend. Xin Metal is vigorous upon You; Ding is its proper ghost. Ding is born at You and breaks Lu, preventing blessing from taking form: success and failure alternate. If the body is strong, the month’s qi communicates, and restraint is present, judge Indirect Officer. Further body-strengthening luck gives distinction. Communication with Metal qi, no Ding, and a strong body give great distinction in southern luck.[^92]

**Xinchou day.** Elevated and stable. You month with Wood-and-Earth luck gives provincial authority. Xu, Hai, Zi, Si, and Wei in year and month give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Gengxu:** Vice Minister Zhang Yue, an eminent minister. **Guichou / Guihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** a provincial graduate. **Renwu / Yisi:** an imperial clansman. **Yiwei / Gengchen:** sentenced to death for a forged seal, but escaped by deceit.〉

**Xinmao day.** Hour and day clash. The month communicating with Metal qi, with neither Bing nor Ding exposed, gives distinction in southern luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Guiyou:** Vice Minister Wang Ling. **Wuyin / Jiazi:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Gengchen / Yiyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingzi / Jihai:** Wu Zongda, palace-examination third and grand secretary.〉

**Xinsi day.** Ordinary. Hai month gives continual reversals and uncertainty; Wu month gives Indirect Officer, illness, advancement, and a short life. Zi month gives distinction among imperial relatives. 〈One account says:〉 wealth and harm, with life not exceeding thirty-one. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Bingyin:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Xinsi / Xinmao:** an associate prefect. **[Dinghai] / Renzi:** Minister Shu Hua. **Gengchen / Wuzi:** an official appointed through hereditary favor, banished to frontier service. **Bingzi / Wuxu:** a provincial graduate.〉[^93]

**Xinwei day.** If not distinguished, then rich. A month communicating with Metal qi, with neither Bing nor Ding, gives distinction in southern luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiyou / Renshen:** Hanlin Compiler Dai Dabin. On examination, Master Dai himself said he was born in Gengxu; attaching him to Master Zhu Wengong’s Jiyou is probably mistaken. Alas, he died. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** a ministry secretary. **Jiwei / Bingzi:** wealth. **Bingshen / Gengzi:** lived more than a hundred years.〉[^94]

**Xinyou day.** Year and month communicating with Metal qi are auspicious. Chou month with western luck gives censorial office; Yin, Wu, and Xu give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiachen / Jiaxu:** Minister Wei Chengfang. **Renxu / Bingwu:** a provincial administrator. **Gengshen / Jimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yimao / Wuyin:** Yang Hao, military commissioner and minister.〉

**Xinhai day.** Distinction. Chou, Yin, Mao, and You in year and month give gold and purple in close attendance. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Jihai:** a man of Wuyuan, who passed the Zhejiang provincial examination as Hu Zhengsui, the northern examination as Zhengdao, and the southern examination as Wang Guochang; impeached and stripped of status. **Jihai / Xinwei:** a prefect, immensely rich, without sons.〉[^95]

〈Killings Born upon the Hour:  
Let the Heavenly Origin show no Officer.  
A strong body and Wealth-and-Lu luck:  
Manifest distinction steps into the Golden Luan Hall.〉

> You hour and a Xin day belong to one kind;  
> Step beyond the door to greet new joyous events.  
> Without punishment, clash, Void, control, or breaking,  
> Why fear that wealth and distinction will not reach you?

> A Xin day approaching Dingyou hour:  
> Indirect Officer meets a fitting configuration.  
> Repeated Bing and Ding indicate lingering obstruction;  
> Among offspring, daughters are many and sons few.  
> Ancestral property is a fading flower in autumn dusk;  
> For the traveler, clouds clear from the bright moon.  
> A strong body and flourishing Officer bring leisurely blessing;  
> Luck reaching Wealth and Officer brings great abundance.

## 35. Xin Days, Wuxu Hour
### 六辛日戌時斷
<!-- source: L1628-L1669 -->

> A Xin day born at Wuxu hour:  
> Seal-Ribbon generates the body, seated in the Hall of Lu.  
> A supported, fortunate person finds the ancestors hard to rely upon;  
> Without communication with the month’s qi, the fate is ordinary.

On a Xin day at Wuxu hour, Lu and Seal occupy the same hall. Xin takes Xu as the Hall of Lu; visible Wu upon Xu is Seal-Ribbon, while Bing and Ding serve as Officer. Wu Earth has its proper position at Xu, and Bing and Ding form the Fire configuration. With support and communication with the month’s qi, the ancestral establishment is difficult to sustain; without communication, the fate is ordinary.[^96]

**Xinchou day.** Punishment and harm. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Seal-Ribbon and great distinction in southern luck. Yin and Si give noble Direct Officer; Zi, Wu, Mao, and You give military or censorial office. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Jiachen:** a grand secretary. **Renwu / Gengxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Wuchen / Jiayin:** a prefect. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Xinmao day.** Punishment, harm, and greed. Hai or Mao month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives fifth- or sixth-rank distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinmao / Jiawu:** the Prince of Xiang. **Bingzi / Xinmao:** Minister Huo Ji, who lived sixty years. **Dingwei / Xinhai:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Dingyou / Bingwu:** a prefect. **Gengyin / Wuzi:** an assistant prefect.〉

**Xinsi day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give Seal-Ribbon, literary distinction, and little illness. Birth in a region of substantial Earth gives distinction; Xu month with Wood-and-Fire luck gives censorial office. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Dingwei:** an earl. **Yihai / Renwu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Yichou / Jichou:** Supervising Secretary Gao Shi, sentenced to death. **Dingwei / Yisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengshen / Jiashen:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Dingmao:** Vice Minister of Rites Zhou Yingbin. **Yiwei / Jiashen:** Zeng Chaojie, palace-examination third and vice minister. **Jihai / Bingzi:** a subprefect.〉

**Xinwei day.** Ominous, cruel, and violent; concern for wife and children. Yin, Si, Wu, Xu, and Chou months give distinction; Shen, Zi, Chen, and Hai give Metal and Water holding refinement, with exceptional distinction. 〈One account says:〉 injury to parents, a strong body, and wealth in middle life. 〈Recorded cases—**Renshen / Renzi:** Minister Wang Guoguang. **Yihai / Xinsi:** a deputy court minister. **Jiazi / Dingchou:** a deputy commissioner. **Xinwei / Wuxu:** a deputy commissioner, with Two Stems Unmixed. **[Jiaxu] / Bingyin:** a censor. **Renwu / Gengxu:** a ministry secretary. **Xinwei / [Jiawu]:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** violent death.〉[^97]

**Xinyou day.** Ominous punishment. Si, You, Chou, Chen, Xu, and Wei in year and month give the fate of a first-place examination graduate, court minister, or capital prefect. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Jihai:** Minister Wan Shihe, a gentleman. **Jisi / Guiyou:** a vice minister. **Bingchen / Gengyin:** a censor. **Wuzi / Jiwei:** a prefect. **Bingyin / Xinchou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuxu / Gengshen:** a county magistrate. **Xinchou / Yiwei:** a provincial graduate. **Guichou / Bingchen:** a deputy court minister. **Jimao / Bingyin:** succeeded in the Gengxu metropolitan examination and died that year.〉

**Xinhai day.** Cruel and ominous, scheming, crafty, and calculating. 〈One account says:〉 development follows misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Wuyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiashen / Renshen:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Yisi:** violent death. **Wuchen / Xinyou:** a censor. **Xinmao / Yiwei:** an earl. **Jichou / Bingyin:** a censor.〉

〈Officer and Seal Approaching the Hour:  
Early undertakings find no accord.  
In middle and later life the time arrives;  
The Storehouse opens of itself.〉

> A Xin day at Xu hour closes the Wealth Storehouse;  
> To open it, await Chou and Chen.  
> Jia and Bing exposed in year and month’s heavenly stems:  
> Wealth, distinction, and splendor need no conjecture.

> A Xin day approaching Wuxu hour:  
> The Five Phases bring Wealth and Lu to splendor.  
> Chen and Xu stand opposite in the pillars:  
> This is called the Key Opening the Storehouse.  
> Fire and Water shine forth in advancement;  
> Void locks it shut, leaving an ordinary fate.  
> Luck travels Wealth’s ground and Officer’s country:  
> Without breaking, a naturally fortunate prospect.

## 36. Xin Days, Jihai Hour
### 六辛日亥時斷
<!-- source: L1670-L1715 -->

> A Xin day born at Jihai hour:  
> Turning from Lu and stripping Officer bring breaking and injury.  
> To make the distinction of Flying Heavenly Lu and Horse—  
> Miss the season and lack combination, and all is fruitless toil.

On a Xin day at Jihai hour, Flying Lu forms a combining configuration. Xin uses Bing as Officer; flourishing Ren upon Hai injures it, leaving Officer without qi. With a further Hai month or Hai day, Hai clashes forth Bing Fire within Si as Officer Star. Xinyou and Xinchou combine into the configuration and are noble. The other Xin days, without combination, communication with the month’s qi, or support, give poverty and low station. Support makes the result auspicious.

**Xinchou day.** Birth in Chen, Xu, Chou, Wei, Yin, or Mao month is judged as noble Flying Heavenly Lu and Horse. Wei month with Metal-and-Water luck gives gold and purple and provincial authority. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Dinghai:** Surveillance Commissioner Lin, conforming to the Flying-Heaven configuration. **Xinyou / Bingshen:** southeast luck is welcome and also gives distinction. **Yichou / Bingxu:** office in the two provincial commissions. **Xinyou / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiachen / Dingmao:** succeeded in the Gengwu provincial examination; met calamity in Renzi.〉

**Xinmao day.** Yin, Mao, Hai, and Wei months give Wealth flourishing to generate Officer, with rejoicing throughout life. Si, You, and Ding in year and month give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Renyin:** a bureau director. **Xinchou / Jihai:** Academician Zhang. **Yimao / Wuzi:** a provincial graduate. **Xinmao / Dingyou:** a supervising secretary. **Renxu / Renyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guimao / Xinyou:** a court minister. **Wushen / Jiazi:** low station.〉

**Xinsi day.** Si fills the position, so this does not enter the Flying-Heaven configuration. Judge Officer and Seal: the parents exert their utmost support, and wealth and distinction come afterward. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingmao / Jiyou:** the Ming Shizong emperor, reigning forty-five years and dying in Dingmao year. **Yichou / Jichou:** Vice Minister Shen Liangcai. **Guiwei / Jiazi:** Zhu Dashou, palace-examination first and vice minister, who died in Guiyou year; an impoverished solitary man shared the chart. **Dinghai / Renyin:** a subprefect. **Jiazi / Dingmao:** distinction and early death.〉[^98]

**Xinwei day.** Distinction. In Hai, Mao, Wei, and Yin months, Metal can control Wood: abundant Wealth generates Officer, bringing fame, profit, and manifest distinction. Si and Wu in year and month give wealth and distinction; autumn birth with Bing exposed is also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuyin / Yimao:** Qin Minglei, palace-examination first. **Bingwu / Bingshen:** first in the provincial examination. **Dingyou / Renzi:** a senior provincial administrator. **Guihai / Yichou:** Wang Daokun’s father, granted the honorary title of vice minister. **Guimao / Xinyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiaxu / Jisi:** first in the provincial examination. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** Assistant Censor-in-Chief Tian Pi.〉

**Xinyou day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give auspicious Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer. Pure Hai clashes forth Bing Fire within Si as Officer: distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingchou / Xinhai:** a provincial graduate. **Xinhai / Xinchou:** a provincial graduate. **Xinyou / Jihai:** Vice Minister Zheng Gang. **Bingzi / Xinmao:** Minister Zhao Jin; an eminent monk from Sichuan shared the chart. **Gengzi / Jichou:** a deputy commissioner. **Gengwu / Gengchen:** Song Fengxiang, first in the provincial examination.〉

**Xinhai day.** Further Hai in year and month gives Flying Heavenly Lu and Horse, with third- or fourth-rank distinction; in military office, the highest rank, marquis or earl. Yin and Si filling the position diminish it. Zi and Chen in year and month are also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Yimao:** a censor. **Renchen / Wushen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinhai / Guisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dinghai / Yichou:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Jiazi / Yihai:** a ministry secretary. **Jiaxu / Dingchou:** a bureau director. **Jichou / Jisi:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Gengwu:** an earl. **Bingchen / Gengyin:** an education commissioner. **Gengchen / Dinghai:** a school instructor who lived seventy years.〉[^99]

〈Spirit Fungus Emerging from Grass:  
Familiar faces [each] scattered apart.  
Keeping to the old establishment brings little accomplishment;  
Throughout life, act on your own.〉

> A Xin day’s Heavenly Stem at Jihai hour:  
> Owl Spirit turns from Lu, bringing fears of calamity.  
> Without clash, blessing develops but carries little weight;  
> Lu and Horse Flying Heavenward give distinction of their own.

> A Xin day approaching Jihai hour:  
> Owl Spirit and Turning from Lu share one palace.  
> Just like a waning moon enclosed by clouds,  
> Ren, Si, Jia, and Yin have no use.  
> Parents are both present, yet [not] harmonious;  
> Fading flowers bear fruit—guard against the wind.  
> Without control, breaking, punishment, or clash,  
> Flying Heavenly Lu and Horse brings weighty blessing.[^100]

## 37. Ren Days, Gengzi Hour
### 六壬日子時斷
<!-- source: L1716-L1771 -->

> A Ren day born at Gengzi hour:  
> Geng stands openly upon Zi; injury is hidden within.  
> A Fire or Earth month still promises good fortune;  
> Without communication, cruelty and an ordinary fate.

On a Ren day at Gengzi hour, Blade flourishes and the body is strong. Ren takes Geng as Inverted Food and Gui as Yang Blade; the hour has visible Geng and flourishing Gui. Communication with the qi of a Fire or Earth month restrains Geng and Gui and gives great distinction. Without communication, the person is cruel and ordinary. A communicating luck course also gives distinction.

**Renzi day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer; Si, Shen, You, and Chou give Seal-Ribbon. Birth in a region of substantial Earth gives distinction in either case. Yin month with Metal-and-Water luck gives gold and purple in close attendance; Wu month with western luck gives sixth rank, but Zi luck brings defeat and misfortune. Pure Renzi gives Yang Blade, poverty, and misfortune. 〈One account says:〉 rich in youth, hardship after thirty-five, immense wealth at the end. Avoid Xinsi month, an evil death; Xinhai month, great misfortune; Jiazi month, punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Guiyou:** a minister of Justice. **Guiyou / Yimao:** a vice minister. **Guiyou / Jiwei:** Chief Censor Li Bangzhen. **Jiaxu / Yihai:** an official in the Personnel administration. **Jiashen / Jisi:** Minister Yan Qing of Jiaxing; an ordinary man from Northern Zhili shared the chart. **Jiazi / Yihai:** Zhang, palace-examination first. **Xinmao / Gengyin:** Minister Ye Mengxiong, with a hereditary Embroidered-Uniform Guard appointment; a princely descendant shared the chart. **Gengshen / Jimao:** Yuan Zongdao, metropolitan-examination first, serving in the Palace Secretariat. **Wuwu / Wuwu:** a provincial graduate. **Jiayin / Dingmao:** a provincial graduate. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** a deputy commissioner.〉[^101]

**Renyin day.** Distinction with punishment. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei months give Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer; Si, Shen, and You give Seal-Ribbon. All give distinction. Avoid Yisi month, punishment; Dingyou month, great destruction of the ancestral estate and violent death; Yihai month, punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Gengchen:** Grand Secretary Fang Xianfu. **Guiyou / Jiayin:** a vice minister. **Gengwu / Jichou:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Renchen / Jiachen:** Minister of Personnel Sun Piyang, according to the original text. **Gengchen / Xinsi:** Song Decheng, provincial graduate in Wuwu, who met calamity in Yihai. **Yihai / Jiashen:** a provincial graduate.〉[^102]

**Renchen day.** Yin and Wu months strengthen Wealth Star; Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei strengthen Officer. All are auspicious. Pure Chen with Geng and Ren exposed forms Ren Riding the Dragon’s Back and gives great distinction. Avoid Bingchen month, early death with the body mutilated; Dingwei month, ruin of the ancestral establishment and solitude; Xinchou month, great defeat and punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengchen / Gengchen:** Chancellor Zhao. **Yiyou / Jiashen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jiaxu / Bingyin:** Chief Censor Song Yiwang. **Gengxu / Wuzi:** a leading graduate in a classics division. **Jichou / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate, successful in Jiazi and dead in Wuchen. **Xinhai / Wuxu:** wealth and longevity. **Renyin / Wushen:** a clerical official still vigorous at eighty-nine. **Guiyou / Renxu:** a supervising secretary for Works. **Guiyou / Yimao:** poverty and misfortune. **Gengzi / Jiashen:** Minister Li Zhen. **Xinhai / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

**Renwu day.** Hour and day clash: daughters many, sons few. Chou and Wei months give wealth and distinction in southern luck. The other years and months can be understood by examining the noble cases. Avoid Jiawu month, self-punishment and many disputes; Guiyou month, great defeat, displacement, and violent death; Xinhai month, violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Jiawu:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Dingmao / Jiyou:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Wuzi / Jichou:** a commander-in-chief. **Yimao / Yiyou:** a ministry secretary. **Guimao / Yichou:** Honorary Official Yuan; his son Zongdao was metropolitan-examination first, Hongdao and Zhongdao were also metropolitan graduates, and his grandson Pengnian was a metropolitan graduate. **Bingyin / Yihai:** a provincial graduate. **Guichou / Renxu:** a provincial graduate. **Bingshen / Gengyin:** second in the military palace examination.〉[^103]

**Renshen day.** Poverty and punishment. Wei month gives Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer, with manifest distinction in flourishing-Wealth luck: difficulty first, ease afterward. Chen assembles Water too abundantly; Fire-and-Earth luck is needed for good fortune. Chou and Xu give Wealth, Officer, and Seal-Ribbon, all auspicious. Spring and winter are ordinary; autumn and summer auspicious. Avoid Yisi month, punishment and early death; Dingyou, early death; Yihai month, solitude and self-punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiachen / Dingchou:** Minister Xia Bangmo. **Renwu / Dingwei:** a vice minister. **Jihai / Wuchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jimao / Yihai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiwei / Wuwu:** Hanlin scholar Chen Wanyan.〉

**Renxu day.** Early death. Chou month with Metal-and-Fire luck, and Chen or Wei with the Wealth-and-Officer configuration, all give distinction. Yin and You give distinction in northwest luck; Shen–Zi–Chen gives the highest rank in southeast luck. Avoid Jiyou month, solitude and misfortune; Dinghai month, ominous punishment and violent death; Xinchou month, punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Renzi:** Minister Zeng Shengwu, later erased from the official rolls. **Gengwu / Wuyin:** Xing Yifeng, palace-examination third. **Yichou / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate.〉

〈A Laden Boat upon the Sandbank:  
Raise the sail and await a following wind.  
Through repeated places of danger,  
Meeting good fortune restores passage.〉

> A Ren day’s Heavenly Stem at Gengzi hour:  
> The Owl day encounters Rob Wealth, as it ever has.  
> In weak luck, guard against injury to wife and children;  
> In strong luck, Wealth and Lu descend from Heaven.

> A Ren day meeting Gengzi hour:  
> Rob Wealth and Inverted Food linger together.  
> Luck through Companions and Rob Wealth torments undertakings;  
> Wealth and Lu cannot gain clear passage.  
> Siblings and parents meet disappointment;  
> Wife and children, if delayed, finally gather.  
> Steps through Wealth-and-Officer luck bring a flood of blessing;  
> The ancestral establishment is changed anew.[^104]

## 38. Ren Days, Xinchou Hour
### 六壬日丑時斷
<!-- source: L1772-L1819 -->

> A Ren day born at Xinchou hour:  
> Officer Star below, Seal-Ribbon above.  
> With communication through the month’s qi and southwest luck,  
> Officer and Seal support the body; the person is pure and refined.

On a Ren day at Xinchou hour, Officer and Seal have their places. Ren uses Ji as Officer and Xin as Seal; the Metal configuration upon Chou and hidden Ji obtain their positions. Communication with the month’s qi gives purity and refinement, secure Lu and distinction. Without communication, the nature is eccentric, crafty, and deceptive.

**Renzi day.** Si, You, and Chou months give much Seal-Ribbon and the parents’ protection. Shen month with Fire-and-Earth luck gives authority in close attendance. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Officer and Seal, handsome bearing, an expansive disposition, and a name reaching the horizon. In Yin, Wu, and Xu months, Wealth damages Lu and Seal; the configuration does not form, and the fate is ordinary. Hai and Shen in year and month give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingchen / Bingshen:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Dinghai / Wushen:** a supervising secretary. **Wuzi / Xinchou:** a censor. **Renzi / Xinhai:** minor distinction. **Dingmao / Gengxu:** a provincial graduate. **Xinsi / Jiaxu:** wealth. **Bingxu / Bingshen:** Senior Provincial Administrator Chen Zubao.〉

**Renyin day.** Chou month gives elevated Mixed-Qi Seal-Ribbon. Chen and Si bring strong Killings without restraint; Wealth and Officer burden a weak body, giving hardship. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Yichou:** a supervising secretary. **Guisi / Jiwei:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guiyou / Yichou:** a county magistrate. **Bingshen / Xinchou:** a regional military commander. **Renchen / Jiachen:** Minister of Personnel Sun Piyang, without sons, according to *Star Cases*. **Dingyou / Yisi:** Minister Dong Yu.〉

**Renchen day.** Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Mixed-Qi Wealth, Officer, and Seal-Ribbon, all auspicious. Spring is ordinary; summer gives Wealth and Lu; autumn contains misfortune within good fortune; winter strengthens the body, and southern luck is needed for advancement. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinchou / Xinchou:** Master Fan Wenzheng. **Xinchou / Bingshen:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Bingshen / Xinchou:** Chief Censor Pan Dan. **Wuchen / Bingchen:** Hanlin Compiler Qin Mingxia. **Gengyin / Dinghai:** Minister Zheng Luo. **Xinwei / Dingyou:** Chief Censor Li Tianchong; violent death. **Xinwei / Wuxu:** Lei Zongdao, minister of Rites. **Wuxu / Bingchen:** Censor Zhu Dazhou, banished to frontier service; a Secretariat drafter shared the chart. **Jiazi / Bingyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinwei / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuxu / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate.〉[^105]

**Renwu day.** Lu and Horse in One Country gives distinction. Summer is auspicious; autumn has Wealth, Officer, and Seal complete and also gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingchen / Dingyou:** a chief surveillance commissioner. **Guiyou / Wuwu:** an assistant commissioner. **Jisi / Guiyou:** a prefect. **Wuchen / Dingsi:** a supervising secretary. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** Censor Liu Tai, who [impeached] Zhang Juzheng, was banished to frontier service, and died. **Xinhai / Dingyou:** Metropolitan Graduate Zou Yuanbiao, who impeached Zhang Juzheng and rose to minister of Personnel. **Jichou / Dingchou:** Wei Shichun, Nanjing minister of Justice, from Jiangxi; a Wuxi licentiate and a Sichuan man described as “shi” shared the chart. **Guiyou / Gengshen:** an imperial relative. **Renyin / Xinhai:** Deng Yizan, metropolitan-examination first, palace third, and vice minister.〉[^106]

**Renshen day.** Yin, Mao, Hai, and Wei in year and month give metropolitan success, censorial office, and rank as high as second, but ultimately there are rises and falls. Chen and Xu give abundant wealth; Shen and You give natural enjoyment of resources. Northern luck also gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Bingyin:** Minister Zheng Xiao, widely learned and refined. **Gengyin / Dinghai:** Academician Cao Xi. **Dingyou / Wushen:** an imperial son-in-law. **Dingwei / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wushen / Jiayin:** an honorary official. **Jiaxu / Renshen:** a duke. **Guihai / Xinyou:** Governor Huang Chengxuan. **Renwu / Renyin:** Governor Lian Guoshi, banished to frontier service. **Jisi / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate. **Bingchen / Gengyin:** a man subjected to punishment. **Dingyou / Xinhai:** no caption supplied.〉

**Renxu day.** Chen month clashes open Wealth and Officer, giving distinction; Chou and Xu are also auspicious. Yi, Gui, or Mao in the pillars gives an ordinary fate. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Wuxu:** Privy Councillor He Luo. **Yiyou / Jichou:** a vice minister. **Yiwei / Xinchou:** a ministry secretary. **Jiazi / Yihai:** Honorary Official Liang, whose son Menglong was a minister. **Xinyou / Guisi:** an earl.〉

〈Officer and Seal Approaching the Hour:  
A weak body remains ordinary for now.  
To make achievement manifest,  
Advance through Wealth-and-Officer luck.〉

> The six Ren day stems at Xinchou hour:  
> Officer and Seal generate each other—everything is exceptional.  
> Wu month further communicating with flourishing Metal and Earth:  
> Pure distinction in office is assured.

> A Ren day approaching Xinchou hour:  
> Wealth, Officer, and Seal-Ribbon are all within.  
> Know the key that opens the Storehouse’s passage:  
> Wu and Ji meet, and Fire is heavy.  
> Gui, Mao, and Yi diminish blessing;  
> Dragon and Tiger may encounter one another in clash.  
> Only sooner or later divides the change of family standing:  
> Wealth and distinction receive favor and acknowledge imperial grace.[^107]

## 39. Ren Days, Renyin Hour
### 六壬日寅時斷
<!-- source: L1820-L1871 -->

> A Ren day born at Renyin hour:  
> Water and Fire meet—judge Achieving Completion.  
> A Water or Wood month communicates: Wealth, Lu, and distinction;  
> Without communication or rescue, an ordinary person.

On a Ren day at Renyin hour, Water and Fire Achieve Completion. Ren uses Bing as Wealth and Jia as Food. Bing is born upon Yin and Jia flourishes there, while Ren Water lacks qi. Communication with a Water configuration and support gives distinction in every case; without communication or rescue, blessing is thin. A vigorous Renyin day indicates great wealth and gives distinction even without communication with the month’s qi.

**Renzi day.** Pure Zi in year and month, without Wu to clash and break, enters the Flying-Heaven configuration and gives wealth and distinction. Yin, Mao, Si, Shen, You, Xu, and Hai in year and month all give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinsi / Wuxu:** Minister Fang Liangyong. **Gengyin / Yiyou:** a chief censor. **Xinyou / Xinmao:** a surveillance commissioner. **Renzi / Dingwei:** a deputy court minister. **Jiwei / Bingyin:** a ministry secretary. **Yisi / Dinghai:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingchou / Yisi:** a county magistrate. **Bingyin / Gengzi:** Empress Dowager Li. **Dingyou / Renzi:** an imperial relative. **Gengyin / Wuyin:** an imperial relative. **Renzi / Renzi:** great distinction. **Guimao / Bingchen:** Sun Kuang, metropolitan-examination first and Nanjing minister of War. **Xinsi / Bingshen:** distinction through contribution. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** an earl. **Guisi / Dingsi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Renyin day.** Ren Tending toward Gen. In a region of substantial Earth, mountains are clear and waters refined: gold at the waist and purple robes. Jihai in year and month gives third rank in military office, substantial wealth, purity, and sincerity. Yin year and Wu month give gold and purple in northern luck; Si month gives distinction in northwest luck. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei are also auspicious; pure Yin is especially so. 〈One account says:〉 distinction in middle age, great adversity after fifty. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Bingchen:** an official in Personnel. **Bingshen / Bingshen:** a [court minister]. **Renyin / Renyin:** Chief Censor Han, with One Qi Generating and Completing; an immensely rich man shared the chart. **Renwu / Wushen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Guisi / Jiazi:** Lin, palace-examination first. **Wuchen / Bingchen:** a prefect. **Dingmao / Jiayin:** a provincial graduate. **Guiyou / Jiazi:** Zhang Zhifa of Zichuan, who entered the Grand Secretariat from vice minister of Justice. **Bingxu / Yiwei:** great wealth. **Renchen / Jiachen:** a man subjected to punishment.〉[^108]

**Renchen day.** Pure Chen forms Ren Riding the Dragon’s Back. Without Bing, Ding, Wu, or Ji in the stems, Companions luck gives great distinction. Pure Yin gives great wealth. Mao or Xu month with Wood-and-Metal luck gives third- or fifth-rank distinction; Wu month is ordinary. 〈Recorded cases—**Wushen / Guihai:** Minister Fang Chun. **Jiaxu / Xinwei:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Yichou / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiazi / Wuchen:** Xu Gu, metropolitan-examination first and vice minister, immensely rich. **Xinsi / Xinchou:** a provincial graduate. **Dingwei / Dingwei:** a provincial graduate. **Jiachen / Dingchou:** Liu Chuxian, minister of Rites. **Jimao / Guiyou:** Grand Secretary Xie Sheng. **Jimao / Dingchou:** Cheng Guoxiang, a selection-bureau director of high rank. **Guiwei / Renxu:** Lin Zhen, palace-examination third and grand secretary. **Yimao / Guiwei:** an assistant prefect.〉[^109]

**Renwu day.** Youth brings many illnesses and dangers. Renwu has Lu and Horse in One Country; Chen and Yin in year and month give second rank. Shen and You give elevated standing. Communication with Wood and Fire gives distinction and a reputation for talent surpassing the age. Xu month with southeast luck gives the highest provincial authority. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Bingxu:** Minister Sheng Duanming. **Gengyin / Jichou:** a chancellor. **Renshen / Renzi:** a prefect. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** Hu Yan, chancellor of the Directorate of Education. **Xinwei / Gengzi:** a prefect. **Xinyou / Jiawu:** a censor. **Renyin / Xinhai:** Shen Sixiao, Hanlin probationer and vice minister, without sons. **Wuchen / Xinyou:** a provincial graduate. **Wuxu / Renxu:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Renzi:** violent death.〉

**Renshen day.** Si, You, and Chou months give third rank in military office. Xin, Mao, and Xu months also give distinction; Zi and Yin give great distinction. 〈One account says:〉 poor, and development does not last. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingshen / Jiawu:** Vice Minister Cai Tianyou. **Xinhai / Renyin:** a bureau director. **Renchen / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinmao / Wuxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yiyou / Wuyin:** a provincial graduate. **Wuwu / Jiayin:** Han Bangyu, metropolitan graduate and subprefect, slain in Jiachen.〉[^110]

**Renxu day.** Si month gives the Indirect-Officer configuration: the name upon the golden list, the person seated in the Jade Hall. Pure Zi gives third rank; Yin and Mao with northern luck give censorial office. The six Ren days meeting Renyin hour are also called **Great Void**: distinction does not last, and prosperity gives rise to calamity. 〈Recorded cases—**Guihai / Xinyou:** Vice Minister Min Rulin. **Jiazi / Bingzi:** a court minister. **Renyin / Wushen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Renxu / Renzi:** an associate prefect. **Renshen / Renzi:** a deputy surveillance commissioner. **Wuyin / Renxu:** Yi Yingchang, head of the Censorate. **Gengxu / Yiyou:** a censor.〉[^111]

〈A Ren Day and a Ren Hour:  
Even without Officer, there is Wealth.  
Yin and Chen encountered repeatedly:  
Wealth and distinction descend from Heaven.〉

> The six Ren meeting Tiger are bubbles on the stream;  
> Do not force the quest for riches, rank, and fame.  
> Seal and Officer make the upper configuration;  
> Wealth and Lu arriving suddenly dispel sorrow.

> A Ren day meeting Renyin hour:  
> Companions encounter Food God.  
> Brothers, like ranks of geese, seldom flock together;  
> This is the allotted portion fixed by the birth hour.  
> The configuration seated, luck travels Officer’s ground;  
> A strong body raises office beyond its peers.  
> A declining body meets encroaching punishment, harm, and calamity:  
> A fate of ordinary food and clothing.

## 40. Ren Days, Guimao Hour
### 六壬日夘時斷
<!-- source: L1872-L1915 -->

> A Ren day born at Guimao hour:  
> Drawn into Death’s ground, its situation finds no rest.  
> Rob Wealth, Killings, and Blade encounter the injury of ghost;  
> Without support, judge an ordinary fate.

On a Ren day at Guimao hour, the body dies and Blade is born. Ren takes Gui as Blade; hidden Yi upon Mao injures Officer and ghost. At Mao, Gui is born, Yi flourishes, and Ren dies. Without communication with a body-strengthening month’s qi, rescue, or support, there is early death and low station. Si, You, and Chou months give flourishing Seal; without transformation, the nature is eccentric, aloof, and deceitful. Communication with bodily strength and the presence of Metal qi give distinction in Wealth luck. Hurting Officer wholly removing Officer also gives distinction in southern luck.[^112]

**Renzi day.** Zi and Mao punish each other, raising concern for wife and children. Noble people offer support, wealth is abundant, and the nature is substantial and sincere. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Guiwei:** a deputy commissioner. **Dingmao / Renyin:** a provincial graduate. **Bingshen / Xinchou:** a robber, duly executed.〉

**Renyin day.** Turning from Wealth and defeating Lu: now rising, now falling. Zi in year and month gives distinction. Si month gives Wealth and Officer; despite punishment, there is distinction. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei give Officer and Killings restraining Blade and are all auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiazi / Jisi:** Minister Ni Yue, an eminent minister. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a registrar, who died in Jiazi year. **Bingzi / Wuxu:** first in the provincial examination. **Guisi / Jiwei:** a military commander.〉

**Renchen day.** A month communicating with bodily strength, with Earth and Metal to break Blade, gives distinction in Wealth luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Gengzi:** Education Commissioner Tian Rucheng. **Renshen / Guichou:** a supervising secretary. **Yimao / Jimao:** an assistant commissioner. **Jiachen / Dingmao:** minister of the Court of Imperial Sacrifices.〉

**Renwu day.** Ren occupying Wu has Lu and Horse in One Country. Mao hour adds manifest distinction and advancement. Si and Wu in year and month give military or censorial office. 〈Another account says:〉 breaking amid flourishing. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Jiayin:** Tang Yijun, first in the provincial examination; died after attaining the metropolitan degree. **Jichou / Dingmao:** a chief councillor. **Bingxu / Bingshen:** a prefectural instructor. **Yiwei / Wuyin:** Censor Liu Tai, who impeached Zhang Juzheng and was banished to frontier service; restored to office in Renwu and posthumously granted a deputy court minister’s title. Another account gives Renshen day, Xinchou hour. **Jisi / Dingchou:** Vice Minister Wu Shen.〉[^113]

**Renshen day.** Hai, Mao, Wei, and Yin months give Hurting Officer with Officer wholly removed, a worthy wife, and filial children. Substantial Earth makes a noble fate. Pure Chen gives medicine or divination; Zi month gives Water-and-Wood distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingwu / Gengzi:** Empress Dowager Li. **Wuxu / Renxu:** Zhen Dexiu, an eminent Confucian scholar. **Bingwu / Gengyin:** Wang Xiangqian, minister of War, with fifty years in official service. **Jiazi / Guiyou:** Metropolitan Graduate Zhou Tingshi. **Xinchou / Xinchou:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Renxu day.** Jia, Gui, Wu, and You in year and month give literary distinction. Shen is ordinary; Metal-and-Fire luck gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Dingwei:** a provincial graduate. **Yihai / Jichou:** a provincial graduate. **Dinghai / Xinhai:** a commander of a hundred households.〉

〈Rob Wealth and Hurting Officer:  
Early undertakings lie submerged.  
From birth, wealth does not gather;  
Spent to the last, it comes again.〉

> Ren and Gui meeting Mao encounter nobility;  
> Punishment, clash, breaking, and harm leave it incomplete.  
> The month meets the Two Virtues, and the body is strong:  
> Misfortune becomes auspiciousness, and joy comes naturally.

> A Ren day approaching Guimao hour:  
> Defeated Wealth and Turning from Lu pursue one another.  
> Throughout life, affairs reverse, uncertain and delayed;  
> Water reaching the east loses its position.  
> A noble person’s rescue is needed;  
> One’s own literary blessing is hard to complete.  
> Ancestral wealth and blood kin have their gains and losses:  
> Early ruin, late accomplishment is the fate.

## 41. Ren Days, Jiachen Hour
### 六壬日辰時斷
<!-- source: L1916-L1961 -->

> A Ren day born at Jiachen hour:  
> Ren rides the Dragon’s Back, seated upon Food God.  
> Support within the pillars, without punishment or harm:  
> Surely a person of splendor, wealth, and distinction.

On a Ren day at Jiachen hour, this too is Ren Riding the Dragon’s Back. Ren takes Jia as Food God. Upon Chen, Ren Water forms its configuration and Jia has generating qi. Food God flourishing and supported, with communication through the month’s qi, gives wealth, distinction, and substantial blessing. A winter month followed by Mao luck is unfavorable.

**Renzi day.** Pure Zi in year and month gives Flying Heavenly Lu and Horse; without breaking, one of the six ministries. Si and You give great distinction; Hai also gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Renzi:** a minister. **Renyin / Renzi:** a minister. **Jiawu / Jisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingshen / Xinchou:** a provincial graduate. **Dingchou / Gengxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dinghai / Renyin:** a provincial graduate. **Jiawu / Xinwei:** Right Censor-in-Chief Chen Dake. **Bingchen / Wuxu:** Zhao Gongji, in the Nanjing Censorate.〉

**Renyin day.** Si month gives the Indirect-Officer configuration; without clash or breaking, manifest distinction. Wu gives noble Direct Officer. Chen and Mao complete the eastern configuration: early examination success, honor, and distinction. 〈Another account says:〉 injury and the utmost poverty. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Gengzi:** Minister Ma Sen. **Dingyou / Bingwu:** a prefect. **Yimao / Dinghai:** a salt-transport commissioner. **Jiayin / Bingzi:** an assistant capital prefect. **Guimao / Dingsi:** a commander-in-chief. **Xinchou / Wuxu:** a provincial graduate. **Renyin / Renyin:** wealth. **Gengyin / Jiashen:** Zhong Yeji, Nanjing minister of War. **Yiwei / Jiashen:** Liu Ruozai, palace-examination first. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** longevity.〉[^114]

**Renchen day.** Chen-month birth gives Ren Riding the Dragon’s Back and great distinction. Yin month gives wealth if not distinction. Xu month gives Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer, clashing open Chen’s Storehouse: an elevated fate. Zi month severs roots and damages leaves, with unsettled success and failure. Wei month gives distinction in Metal-and-Water luck. 〈One account says:〉 Water or Fire calamity and early solitude. 〈Another:〉 wealth followed by loss through self-punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Renzi / Jiachen:** Hanlin probationer Cao Yipeng. **Wuchen / Dingsi:** the Prince of Qi. **Gengchen / Bingxu:** Grand Secretary Yang. **Xinchou / Gengyin:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuyin / Xinyou:** a supervising secretary. **Jiachen / Dingmao:** a salt-transport commissioner. **Gengchen / Gengchen:** an assistant prefect. **Renyin / Renzi:** distinction. **Wuxu / Wuwu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingwu / Jihai:** a first-rank titled lady, without sons. **Bingyin / Gengchen:** wealth. **Wushen / Guihai:** violent death.〉

**Renwu day.** Water or Fire calamity. Yin and Chen in year and month give a seasoned Hanlin scholar, perhaps chancellor of the Directorate of Education. Pure Zi gives censorial office; Mao and Yin substantial wealth; Chen and Zi a scholarly office. 〈Another account says:〉 self-punishment, misfortune, and violent death. 〈Recorded cases—**Yichou / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yichou / Dinghai:** Deputy Court Minister Sheng Guquan, with many sons. **Jiwei / Guiyou:** a ministry secretary. **Bingshen / Renchen:** first in the provincial examination.〉

**Renshen day.** Water or Fire calamity. Chou and Yin in year and month give literary conduct and military authority. Pure Zi with Metal-and-Fire luck gives provincial authority. 〈Recorded cases—**Guisi / Dingsi:** Minister Zhou Jin. **Dingsi / Xinhai:** a deputy commissioner. **Xinhai / Wuxu:** a deputy commissioner. **Yimao / Jichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinwei / Gengzi:** a senior provincial administrator. **Dingyou / Wushen:** Deputy Commissioner Xu Xuegu, who died in Xinsi year during Guimao luck. **Dingyou / Gengxu:** “Jia ministry,” an uncertain designation. **Guihai / Guihai:** a provincial graduate. **Jisi / Renshen:** first in the provincial examination.〉[^115]

**Renxu day.** Yin, Shen, and You months give distinction among imperial relatives in southern luck. Hai–Mao–Wei, Shen–Zi–Chen, and Si or Wu in year and month are all auspicious. 〈One account says:〉 wealth and solitude. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Gengzi:** a prefect. **Yiyou / Jiachen:** a prefect. **Xinhai / Xinmao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuchen / Jiazi:** a commander-in-chief. **Bingwu / Jihai:** Wang Jiheng, metropolitan graduate in Dingchou, appointed magistrate of Nanhe; within half a month the city was lost, but he escaped the legal sentence.〉

〈Ren Riding the Dragon’s Back:  
Lu and Horse are naturally abundant.  
Many Chen give weighty office and Lu;  
Many Yin rival [Shi Chong].〉

> Jiachen in the hour meets a Ren day stem;  
> Joyous influences repeat, and blessings are manifold.  
> When the time arrives, sooner or later achievement follows;  
> Luck reaching Shen and Chen makes an illustrious official.

> A Ren day at Jiachen hour is good;  
> The Azure Dragon entering its temple is exalted.  
> Like orchids emerging from wormwood and weeds,  
> Water and Wood nourish luxuriant splendor.  
> Hour and day clash open the flourishing Storehouse;  
> Naturally a nest and home take shape.  
> Luck traveling auspicious ground displays heroic talent;  
> Distinguished though one is, kin are hard to rely upon.[^116]

## 42. Ren Days, Yisi Hour
### 六壬日巳時斷
<!-- source: L1962-L2003 -->

> A Ren day born at Yisi hour:  
> The body is extinguished; Wealth is present, but does not gather.  
> Advance Spirit and hidden ghost come to exert control;  
> Ji exposed, with punishment, is the embryo of calamity.

On a Ren day at Yisi hour, Wealth flourishes and the body reaches Extinction. Ren uses Bing as Wealth, Wu as ghost, and Geng as Inverted Food. Yi Wood upon Si is Hurting Officer; Bing and [Wu] are vigorous, Geng Metal is at Birth, and Ren Water’s qi is extinguished. Ji Officer in the pillars brings calamities of a hundred kinds. The person scorns others and boasts of high standing. Without communication with a body-strengthening month or rescue, there is poverty. Support and communication with flourishing qi, or body-strengthening luck, are all auspicious.[^117]

**Renzi day.** Distinction, but difficulty with wife and children. The disposition is firm and will not tolerate provocation. Xu in year and month gives pure Killings under restraint; Wood-and-Metal luck brings third rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiashen / Jiaxu:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Jiaxu / Jiaxu:** a vice minister. **Guiyou / Yimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengwu / Renwu:** a Secretariat drafter. **Gengchen / Wuzi:** Liu Cunye, palace-examination second. **Gengxu / Yiyou:** a subprefect.〉

**Renyin day.** Aloof and solitary, injuring wife and children. A monk or Daoist becomes rich and distinguished. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuyin / Renxu:** a deputy commissioner. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a registrar. **Yisi / Jichou:** Grand Secretary Chen Yubi. **Renshen / Bingwu:** Wang Sishan, metropolitan graduate in Xinchou; on the banquet day, [mounting] a horse, he suffered ruptured testicles and died.〉[^118]

**Renchen day.** Spring is ordinary; summer gives wealth; autumn stability and substance. Winter without assistance or rescue gives poverty and hardship. 〈Recorded cases—**Guiwei / Xinyou:** Minister Zhou Meng. **Jiayin / Jiaxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiashen / Jisi:** a provincial graduate. **Yisi / Wuzi:** an associate prefect, long-lived.〉

**Renwu day.** Chen and Xu months give distinction. Hai month with Metal luck brings the rank of commander-in-chief. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiachen / Wuchen:** Ao Xian, chancellor of the Directorate of Education. **Guiyou / Wuwu:** a censor.〉

**Renshen day.** The body sits at Birth and the Hall of Learning. Wealth advances and retreats; fame and profit drive one hither and thither. A Wood month with northern luck gives distinction; Chen, Xu, and Chou are all auspicious. 〈Another account says:〉 injury to hands and feet, and death through self-punishment. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingchen / Gengyin:** a commander-in-chief. **Guiwei / Xinyou:** Chief Censor Xia Hanshou. **Xinhai / Wuxu:** a minister. **Jiaxu / Guiyou:** a senior provincial administrator. **Guichou / Yichou:** a deputy commissioner. **Gengwu / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jihai / Wuchen:** a provincial graduate.〉[^119]

**Renxu day.** Yin in year and month enters the Day-Virtue configuration and gives distinction; Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei also give distinction. Autumn’s Seal and summer’s Wealth and Officer are auspicious. Spring gives injury and winter flourishing; inspect what emerges in the stems, and distinction may also be obtained. 〈Recorded cases—**Yiyou / Gengchen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** a county magistrate. **Dingwei / Renzi:** a provincial graduate. **Bingshen / Dingyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingyou / Guichou:** Zhao Canlu, Hanlin probationer, demoted to a county police office and later promoted step by step to minister.〉

〈A Ren Day at Yisi:  
Let the pillars show no Officer.  
Seeing it, fear deficiency;  
Punishment and harm bring troubles of many kinds.〉

> A Ren day meeting Yisi in the hour:  
> Plans not yet favored—pause and ponder.  
> A noble person recommends; Wealth and Officer flourish;  
> Children and the bridal curtain do not share one heart.

> A Ren day approaching Yisi hour:  
> Hurting Officer and Turning from Lu offer nothing to take.  
> Though Tianyi Noble Person gives support,  
> Distinction is encountered yet not encountered.  
> Plans, hopes, words, and deeds reverse themselves;  
> Life’s solid undertakings turn to emptiness.  
> When the time arrives, advancement changes the family gate:  
> Like parched seedlings receiving rain.

## 43. Ren Days, Bingwu Hour
### 六壬日午時斷
<!-- source: L2004-L2047 -->

> A Ren day born at Bingwu hour:  
> At the gathering place of Wealth, it sits within the embryo.  
> A month of Metal or Water promises wealth and distinction;  
> Abandoning the Fate has always meant following Wealth.

On a Ren day at Bingwu hour, there are the Three Wonders of Lu and Horse. Ren takes Ji as Officer and Bing and Ding as Wealth; Ding and Ji upon Wu are Lu and Horse. Ren Water receives its embryo there. Support and communication with a Metal or Water month’s qi, following Wealth at Birth and flourishing, give wealth and distinction. Communication with Fire qi also gives distinction.[^120]

**Renzi day.** Public-spirited, upright, and steadfast; solitary in loftiness, yet noble. Repeated Zi and Wu in year and month give Water and Fire Achieving Completion and the highest rank. 〈One account says:〉 loss amid flourishing, with injury to wife and children. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Yisi:** a prefect. **Bingwu / Renzi:** great distinction. **Wuxu / Guihai:** a woman whose husband and son were metropolitan graduates. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Renyin day.** Yin, Wu, and Xu months give the Wealth-Star configuration, but a weak body is undesirable; the life of a monk or Daoist is auspicious. Autumn birth gives Seal-Ribbon, winter a strong body; Metal-and-Fire luck brings gold and purple. 〈Recorded cases—**Xinyou / Gengzi:** a vice minister. **Guihai / Yimao:** first in the provincial examination. **Xinwei / Xinchou:** Grand Secretary He Ruchong. **Yimao / Yiyou:** an associate prefect.〉

**Renchen day.** Yin, Wu, and Xu months give flourishing Wealth and great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuchen / Yichou:** a minister. **Jisi / Guiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Bingshen / Renchen:** a senior provincial administrator.〉

**Renwu day.** A winter [month] strengthens the body and is auspicious; summer brings much Wealth to a weak body. Chou [month] gives distinction in close attendance. Yin or Wu with Metal-and-Earth luck: the solitary weak follows the strong, Abandoning the Fate to Follow Wealth, an elevated fate. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuchen / Guihai:** Yuan Wei, metropolitan-examination first, palace third, and grand secretary, without sons. **Jisi / Jisi:** a bureau director. **Gengchen / Dinghai:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Bingwu / Gengzi:** a chancellor. **Dinghai / Renzi:** a prefect. **Yihai / Renwu:** immense wealth.〉[^121]

**Renshen day.** Communication with a Metal or Water month’s qi gives distinction. Without communication, Abandoning the Fate to Follow Wealth gives riches; otherwise, extreme poverty and low station. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Jichou:** Chief Censor Wen Ruzhang. **Guisi / Gengshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dinghai / Wushen:** a Secretariat drafter. **Bingwu / Guisi:** wealth. **Renxu / Bingwu:** wealth.〉

**Renxu day.** Yin month forms the Triple-Combination Wealth configuration; abandoning the fate to follow it gives riches. Autumn gives the body support and brings distinction; winter is also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingyou / Wushen:** Huang Chu, palace-examination second. **Jimao / Wuchen:** Chief Censor Liao Fengshi. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** Prefect Shi Hongxun, rich, with a distinguished son. **Xinsi / Gengyin:** an assistant surveillance commissioner. **Yihai / Jimao:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈Lu and Horse in One Country:  
A strong body brings Wealth and Lu to flourishing.  
The common person can develop blessing;  
The gentleman sits in the court hall.〉

> Born at Bingwu hour, the Ren day is strong;  
> Lu and Horse within the hour are no ordinary thing.  
> Luck through auspicious ground without clash or breaking:  
> Sooner or later, advancement reaches the provincial hall.

> A Ren day meeting Bingwu hour  
> Is also called Lu and Horse in One Country.  
> Achieving Completion reveals literary brilliance;  
> A broad spirit has the ocean’s capacity.  
> Without encountering punishment, clash, breaking, or harm,  
> Wealth and Lu naturally fill the chests.  
> Luck through flourishing Wealth and Officer’s country:  
> Surely a chancellor in the imperial court.[^122]

## 44. Ren Days, Dingwei Hour
### 六壬日未時斷
<!-- source: L2048-L2085 -->

> A Ren day born at Dingwei hour:  
> The husband transforms, the wife follows—an exceptional configuration.  
> If Water and Wood communicate within the pattern,  
> Wealth and blessing both find their proper increase.

On a Ren day at Dingwei hour, the husband follows the wife in transformation. Ren combines with Ding; upon Wei they share the Wood configuration, giving distinction. If the month communicates with a Wood configuration and there is support, wealth develops. Without communication, assistance is still present, and riches come through the wife.[^123]

**Renzi day.** A month communicating with Wood qi gives distinction; with Metal qi, wealth; with Fire and Earth qi, wealth and distinction together. Without communication, Fire-and-Earth luck is also auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Renchen / Xinhai:** Wang, palace-examination first. **Dingyou / Renzi:** a military governor. **Bingshen / Bingshen:** a prefectural official, the title abbreviated. **Gengyin / Renwu:** a provincial graduate. **Wuzi / Jichou:** a tribute scholar.〉

**Renyin day.** Ding and Ren transform into Wood; Hai, Mao, Wei, and Yin months give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengzi / Jichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** a county magistrate.〉

**Renchen day.** Chen-month birth gives Ren Riding the Dragon’s Back and manifest distinction. Yin month gives a wife of consequence and honored children. A triple Water configuration makes the body strong without support, often giving poverty and injury to the wife. 〈Recorded cases—**Guisi / Xinchou:** a censor. **Renwu / Guimao:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiawu / Yihai:** a prefect.〉

**Renwu day.** Spring is ordinary; summer rich; autumn distinguished; winter auspicious. Si and Wu in year and month give the rank of prince or marquis. Wei month gives distinction in eastern luck. Zi month conceals Direct Seal and the Three Wonders together: the highest distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiaxu / Bingzi:** Grand Secretary Yang Yiqing, an eminent minister, without sons. **Yihai / Guiwei:** a bureau director. **Renzi / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Gengwu / Renwu:** wealth. **Renshen / Renyin:** misfortune. **Wuxu / Yichou:** an official appointed through hereditary favor.〉

**Renshen day.** Great wealth. Si and Wu in year and month, or Wu and Ji exposed, give wealth and distinction together. 〈Recorded case—**Yimao / Yiyou:** an assistant director in a government directorate.〉

**Renxu day.** Day Virtue brings a title to the wife and hereditary favor to the children, yet in middle life wife and children suffer injury. Hai, Zi, Shen, and You months give distinction; Si and Wu give riches if not distinction. Chen month gives [Heavenly] and Monthly Virtue and distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jimao / Wuchen:** Minister Liu Sijie. **Dingchou / Jiachen:** a senior provincial administrator. **Dingchou / Guimao:** a provincial graduate. **Jisi / Bingzi:** a supervising secretary. **Bingwu / Bingshen:** a metropolitan graduate. **Xinmao / Bingshen:** distinction. **Dingmao / Renzi:** a chief secretary. **Bingzi / Yihai:** a provincial graduate.〉[^124]

〈The Small Departs, the Great Arrives:  
The household gradually prospers.  
One day, the time and luck arrive;  
Name and profit come to fulfillment of themselves.〉

> A Ren day approaching Dingwei hour:  
> Wood transforms into a forest and avoids Metal.  
> With no breaking or harm in year and month,  
> Wealth, distinction, and blessing must grow deeper.

> A Ren day approaching Dingwei hour:  
> Within it, wife and Wealth combine in secret.  
> Welcome Chou and Xu—the key opens the lock;  
> Money and property accumulate abundantly.  
> Year and month offend neither control nor clash;  
> Food and Lu are naturally arranged.  
> Marriage and offspring go awry in early years;  
> Middle and later life nevertheless bring peace and passage.

## 45. Ren Days, Wushen Hour
### 六壬日申時斷
<!-- source: L2086-L2133 -->

> A Ren day born at Wushen hour:  
> Upon Birth’s ground, ghost injures the body.  
> A strong body with restraint makes an elevated fate;  
> The reverse, know surely, makes a person poor and deprived.

On a Ren day at Wushen hour, Water and Earth mingle turbidly. Ren uses Wu as ghost and Geng as Seal. Upon Shen, Geng Metal is vigorous, Ren Water is at Birth, and Wu Earth is Indirect Officer. Body and ghost are both strong, making the person bold and violent. Communication with a flourishing month, body-strengthening luck, and Jia Wood providing restraint give distinction. Without communication there is intelligence but no distinction; further ghost-strengthening luck makes advancement difficult.

**Renzi day.** Yang Blade carries Killings; a noble person offers support. Si and Wu in year and month give authority in close attendance. 〈Recorded cases—**Yimao / Jiashen:** Deputy Court Minister Shi Ji, foremost in wealth in Jiangnan. **Bingwu / Wuxu:** a deputy commissioner. **Jiaxu / Yihai:** a deputy commissioner. **Jichou / Yihai:** an assistant prefect. **Dingsi / Renzi:** great distinction. **Bingshen / Yiwei:** a capital-city rogue.〉

**Renyin day.** Hour and day clash, leaving the person solitary. A monk or Daoist fares well. Yin, Wu, and Xu, or pure Chen in year and month, also give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Dingwei:** a commander-in-chief. **Wuyin / Jiayin:** Chief Censor Wu Yuan. **Bingxu / Gengzi:** an associate prefect. **Bingchen / Jiawu:** a senior provincial administrator. **Xinhai / Renchen:** a vice censor-in-chief. **Yiwei / Guiwei:** an imperial son-in-law. **Yimao / Jiashen:** a censor. **Yiyou / Jimao:** Ministry Secretary Zhou Xian, who rose from an impoverished scholar, passed after forty, and had a good son; the Four Clashes in his Eight Characters make the chart difficult to judge.〉[^125]

**Renchen day.** Xu-month birth brings early examination success and provincial authority. Yin, Mao, Chou, Shen, Zi, Wu, and similar years and months give the highest distinction; in military office, first rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Guichou / Yimao:** Liu Jian, Master Wenjing, an eminent minister. **Wuyin / Gengyin:** Vice Minister Cao Jin. **Jiaxu / Renshen:** Supreme Commander Zhang Song. **Wuxu / Jiazi:** killed at forty-five. **Jiyou / Jiaxu:** Minister of Personnel Zhang Wenda. **Xinhai / Jiawu:** a ministry bureau director.〉

**Renwu day.** Indirect Officer and Indirect Seal. Hai, Mao, Wei, and Yin months give imposing authority; Zi and Chou with Wood-and-Fire luck give pure distinction in the Hanlin Academy. Xu month with southeast luck gives high rank. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingyin / Xinchou:** Vice Minister Zhang Binghu. **Xinwei / Bingshen:** a supervising secretary for Revenue. **Xinwei / Gengzi:** Lin Daqin, palace-examination first in Renchen, dead in Dingyou; a provincial graduate shared the chart. **Bingzi / Gengzi:** a commander-in-chief sentenced to death.〉[^126]

**Renshen day.** Ren Water’s Birth lies in Shen: Killings flourishes and the body is strong. Further birth in Hai, Mao, or Wei month gives Indirect Officer under restraint and is auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Guichou:** Chief Censor Dong Yaofeng. **Guimao / Renxu:** a vice minister. **Wuzi / Dingsi:** a surveillance commissioner. **Renshen / Bingwu:** a provincial graduate. **Renshen / Jiachen:** distinction. **Jiazi / Jisi:** first in a military examination.〉

**Renxu day.** Breaking, delay, and many obstructions. Pure You gives immense wealth. Without communication with a body-strengthening month, travel through flourishing Killings is inauspicious; Jia providing restraint can also make it acceptable. Heavy Wu and Ji Earth give blindness and an ordinary fate. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuwu / Jiazi:** a censor. **Gengchen / Renwu:** a provincial graduate. **Gengzi / Gengchen:** Cheng De, metropolitan graduate and ministry bureau director, who died in the Chongzhen calamity. **Guisi / Guihai:** Xuan Guozhu, metropolitan graduate in Gengchen, appointed to a supervising secretary’s office and demoted within that same year. **Jiwei / Dingmao:** a rebellious servant.〉[^127]

〈Shen Hour and a Ren Day Stem:  
A strong body knows neither poverty nor cold.  
A great name reaches the tiger list;  
Wealth and Officer emerge amid danger.〉

> Shen hour and Renxu join the Heavenly Origins;  
> Luck going to Wealth and Officer brings blessing naturally.  
> A flourishing ghost and declining body without rescue  
> Leave lifelong toil and incomplete accomplishment.

> A Ren day at Wushen hour is manifest:  
> Stems and branches have flourishing Killings complete.  
> Chen and Zi linked together are welcome,  
> Precisely forming the prospect of brocade robes.  
> Harm, punishment, clash, and controlling conflict  
> Make literary blessing difficult within it.  
> Luck travels good ground; the purple-sealed edict is proclaimed:  
> Wealth and distinction, many wives, and vigorous children.[^128]

## 46. Ren Days, Jiyou Hour
### 六壬日酉時斷
<!-- source: L2134-L2171 -->

> A Ren day born at Jiyou hour:  
> Visible Officer and hidden Seal provide support.  
> A month communicating with bodily strength gives pure distinction;  
> Yet fear a love of flowers and an appetite for the wine cup.

On a Ren day at Jiyou hour, Birth is encountered within Defeat. Ren Water is at Bath upon You; Xin provides generating qi as Seal-Ribbon, and Xin Metal flourishes at You. Ji is used as Officer, visible upon You. Communication with the month’s qi, support, and Wealth-and-Officer luck give distinction; the reverse is ordinary. But Peach Blossom occupies the fate: an amorous person, fond of flowers and wine.[^129]

**Renzi day.** Spring is ordinary; summer auspicious; autumn usual; winter flourishing. Mao-month birth gives a taste for flowers and wine. 〈Recorded case—**Wuzi / Jiazi:** Vice Minister Wu Aheng.〉

**Renyin day.** Great wealth and distinction. Communication with Water qi is welcome; Officer luck gives distinction. Yet the person is amorous, fond of flowers and wine. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiachen / Jiaxu:** Sun, palace-examination first and grand secretary. **Guiwei / Yichou:** a provincial graduate. **Jiwei / Dingchou:** a provincial graduate.〉

**Renchen day.** Elevated. The same month as day gives Ren Riding the Dragon’s Back and great distinction. Si month gives distinction in northeast luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Dingwei / Guihai:** a provincial graduate. **Jiwei / Yihai:** Food-Service Officer Zhao Tang, immensely rich, with five sons. **Yiwei / Bingxu:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingshen / Wuxu:** Minister Lü Kun.〉

**Renwu day.** Lu and Horse in One Country: difficulty first, ease afterward. Chou and Yin months with Metal-and-Water luck give second rank in military office. 〈One account says:〉 loss of the ancestral establishment and misfortune. 〈Recorded cases—**Wuxu / Renxu:** Gui E, provincial graduate and grand secretary. **Bingshen / Jihai:** a deputy commissioner. **Guisi / Jiwei:** distinction. **Xinyou / Gengyin:** Vice Minister Cheng Gui.〉[^130]

**Renshen day.** Officer and Seal approach the gate. With no clash or breaking in the configuration, Lu and Horse are both complete: if not distinguished, then rich. Xu-month birth with southeast luck brings gold at the waist and purple robes. 〈Recorded cases—**Renwu / Bingwu:** a first-place examination graduate. **[Renshen] / Jiyou:** Liu, palace-examination first. **Jiawu / Jisi:** Chen Siyu, chancellor of the Directorate of Education.〉[^131]

**Renxu day.** Hai, Yin, and Zi months give extraordinary distinction in Wealth-and-Officer luck. Pure Hai gives literary distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiawu / Bingyin:** Minister Zhao Xian. **Wuzi / Dingsi:** a provincial graduate. **Jimao / Bingyin:** a provincial graduate.〉

〈Officer and Seal Approaching the Gate:  
Without clash, blessing and Lu are complete.  
East and west should answer the heart’s desires;  
South and north are naturally at peace.〉

> A Ren day’s Heavenly Stem at You hour is true;  
> Misfortune turns auspicious when a noble person is met.  
> If official rank is not bestowed,  
> Later life enjoys blessing and strengthens the family gate.

> A Ren day meeting Jiyou hour:  
> Direct Officer and Seal-Ribbon are without partiality.  
> Flourishing, decline, high rank, and low station arise from their conditions;  
> They can be seen from the person’s formation at birth.  
> Yi, Gui, and Mao bring clash, breaking, and control;  
> A confused mixture halves Wealth and Officer.  
> Amid adversity, riches and distinction cannot both be complete;  
> If the birth hour is mistaken, judgment is difficult.

## 47. Ren Days, Gengxu Hour
### 六壬日戌時斷
<!-- source: L2172-L2211 -->

> A Ren day born at Gengxu hour:  
> The body approaches Wealth’s Storehouse, yet it becomes a demon.  
> Visible Geng and hidden [Wu] punish and control;  
> Throughout life, Wealth and Lu repeatedly gather and disperse.

On a Ren day at Gengxu hour, Owl approaches the Wealth Storehouse. Ren uses Bing and Ding as Wealth, stored at Xu. Geng is Inverted Food and Wu Indirect Officer. Communication with the qi of a Fire or Wood month and support give distinction. Without communication, wealth repeatedly gathers and scatters.[^132]

**Renzi day.** Name and profit advance and retreat. Geng and Xin in year and month give distinction. Chen and Chou punish and clash; You month breaks and harms, making the Wealth Storehouse usable. Si and Wu give flourishing Wealth and Officer. All are auspicious. 〈Recorded cases—**Yisi / Gengchen:** a prefect. **Yiyou / Jichou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Jiachen / Bingzi:** a guard commander. **Bingshen / Xinchou:** a guard commander. **Xinchou / Gengyin:** ungrateful and unrighteous.〉

**Renyin day.** Injury to wife and children. Spring birth is ordinary; summer and winter auspicious. Chen, Chou, and Wei months give distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Gengxu / Jichou:** a prefect. **Wuxu / Jiazi:** a provincial administration commissioner. **Wuzi / Renxu:** Shen Ji, first in the provincial examination, metropolitan graduate, and subprefect.〉

**Renchen day.** Summer gives distinction if not wealth; spring is ordinary; autumn stable; winter laborious and toilsome. Or the person becomes a monk or Daoist, with wealth, breaking, and punishment. 〈A gap in the original.〉[^133]

**Renwu day.** Lu and Horse in One Country. Ren itself sits upon Lu; Geng and Xin restraining Jia and Yi give distinction. Chen, Xu, Chou, and Wei in year and month are auspicious. Shen month gives the Hall of Learning and great literary distinction. Hai and Zi strengthen Water; Ren exposed indicates misfortune. Wu and Xu with Wu exposed make Killings strong and are also ominous. 〈One account says:〉 name and profit advance and retreat amid confusion. 〈Recorded cases—**Yihai / Jiashen:** Minister Zhang Shouzhi. **Bingxu / Renchen:** a chancellor. **Wuzi / Renxu:** a vice minister. **Renchen / Xinhai:** a county magistrate. **Wuzi / Yichou:** a military commander. **Dingmao / Xinhai:** established a household from nothing, without sons.〉[^134]

**Renshen day.** Communication with a Fire or Wood month’s qi gives wealth and distinction; without it, a communicating luck course is also good. Chou and Xu in year and month give great distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingzi / Dingyou:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingyou / Wushen:** Hu Yi, assistant director of the Imperial Seals Office. **Guisi / Jiazi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Bingxu / Xinchou:** Cai Maochun, metropolitan-examination first, registered in Sanhe County and originally from Min County; a Songjiang licentiate and a Jiangnan monk shared the chart. **Wushen / Wuwu:** a commander-in-chief.〉

**Renxu day.** Unfavorable to the wife; the person is harmed by wife or concubines, and success and failure alternate. Pure Xu gives distinction; Yin and Chou military office. Shen month gives Birth and the Hall of Learning: cultivating letters often brings distinction. Hai month gives Established Lu, with Wealth and Officer exposed in the stems; Mao month gives Hurting Officer, with Wealth Star exposed. Both give distinction. Wei month gives Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer, while Chou and Xu punish each other auspiciously. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingxu / Gengyin:** a prefect. **Wuchen / Gengshen:** a prefect. **Xinwei / Bingshen:** a prefect. **Bingxu / Jihai:** a commander-in-chief. **Jiwei / Jiaxu:** an official in the Imperial Seals Office. **Wuxu / Yimao:** a defense commander. **Bingwu / Jihai:** a metropolitan graduate and county magistrate, executed in the marketplace for losing the city.〉

〈Clarity and Turbidity Hard to Distinguish:  
In ill-favored luck, await the time.  
If blessing develops in youth,  
The key has opened the Wealth Storehouse.〉

> Ren and Geng meet in a birth at Xu hour;  
> Wealth and Officer lie within the Storehouse, its gate locked.  
> No thread can be found, and reversals multiply;  
> Achievement in the end resembles floating clouds.

> A Ren day meeting Gengxu hour:  
> Inverted Food among the stems and branches is hard to accommodate.  
> Wealth, Officer, and Seal-Ribbon are sealed inside the Storehouse;  
> Without a key, they cannot be put to use.  
> Chou, You, and Chen encountered bring blessing;  
> Ding and Si encountered further bring a name.  
> Fear excessive control, harm, punishment, and clash:  
> A confused mixture gives an ordinary fate.

## 48. Ren Days, Xinhai Hour
### 六壬日亥時斷
<!-- source: L2212-L2267 -->

> The six Ren days encountering Xinhai hour:  
> Seal and Lu accompany each other—supremely rare excellence.  
> No Wealth or Officer seen, no clash or breaking:  
> I tell you, a road opens through the azure clouds.

On a Ren day at Xinhai hour, the Day’s Lu Dwells in the Hour. Without control or breaking, with support, and with no Wealth or Officer in the pillars, there is wealth, distinction, and advancement. Eastern luck gives great distinction. If qi communicates, blessing diminishes. Southern luck gives no distinction but immense wealth.[^135]

**Renzi day.** The parents do not come to a good end; later development does not bring wealth. Hai and Mao in year and month give advancement through letters and great distinction. 〈Another account says:〉 a Wood fate gives distinction, an Earth fate wealth. 〈Recorded cases—**Bingshen / Xinchou:** He Luowen, vice minister of Rites. **Guisi / Renxu:** a censor. **Gengyin / Renwu:** a Grand Commandant. **Guichou / Renxu:** Rao Jinghui, Nanjing vice minister of War. **Renzi / Renzi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Dingmao / Renyin:** misfortune. **Jiachen / Bingyin:** poverty.〉

**Renyin day.** Development amid hardship. Wei month gives Mixed-Qi Wealth and Officer, a substantial nature, distinction in middle life, and death after fifty. Hai month gives provincial authority in southeast luck. 〈Recorded cases—**Jiwei / Bingzi:** Minister Li Ji. **Yiwei / Gengyin:** Chief Censor Ling Xiang. **Jimao / Xinwei:** a senior provincial administrator. **Jimao / Jiaxu:** a censor. **Dingyou / Yisi:** a metropolitan graduate. **Yisi / Yiyou:** a provincial graduate. **Bingxu / Jiawu:** a regional military commissioner. **Jimao / Yihai:** a selection-bureau director.〉[^136]

**Renchen day.** Refinement and distinction, but violent death. Wu month with no Ji Earth in the stems opens a road through the azure clouds. Jia combining removes Officer and retains Killings: third- or fourth-rank distinction. You month also gives distinction. 〈Recorded cases—**Dinghai / Renyin:** Cai, Duke of Lu; another person with the same chart drowned at nineteen. **Jiwei / Yihai:** the Prince of Zhao, who died by hanging. **Jiashen / Yihai:** a vice minister. **Jiyou / Yihai:** a chief secretary. **Dingsi / Xinhai:** Chief Censor Su Zhigao. **Xinmao / Xinmao:** a prefect. **Jisi / Dingmao:** first in the provincial examination. **Guisi / Yichou:** Gao Guangxian, minister of War; after his death the coffin was broken open and his remains subjected to execution. **Renyin / Wushen:** Minister Wang Xuekui, who lived ninety-three years. **Jimao / Dingchou:** a violent rogue. **Jiaxu / Yihai:** early death. **Jichou / Yihai:** immediate death.〉[^137]

**Renwu day.** Ordinary. Chou month gives censorial office. Yin month gives Lu and Horse in One Country and provincial authority in Metal-and-Water luck. Xu month gives fourth or fifth rank in northeast luck.
